/* FILE: src\app\dashboard\page.tsx */
/* ================================================================ */

'use client';
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import {
  DepositFlow,
  WithdrawFlow,
  BalanceCard,
  HalalStockScanner,
  PortfolioDashboard,
  FinancialHealthScore,
  PurificationTracker,
  FractionalSukuk,
  DigitalCommittee,
  AgriFinancing,
  HajjVault,
  GlobalHalalETFs,
  WirasatCalculator,
  SovereignScan,
  PortfolioChart,
  ZakatDistribution,
  RoziqAI,
  PinSecurity,
  LanguageSelect,
  Onboarding,
  SettingsTab,
  WaliAutopilot,
  AuthGate,
  FamilyHealthSaving,
  OverseasInvest,
  ToastContainer,
} from '@/components';
import { UserProfile, Holding, ZakatRecord, Transaction, DashboardTab, SupportedLanguage } from '@/types';
import { getStockBySymbol } from '@/lib/halal-stock-data';
import { ZAKAT_NISAB_PKR, WALI_INVESTED_THRESHOLD, WALI_PROFIT_THRESHOLD } from '@/lib/constants';


export default function Dashboard() {
  const [currentUser, setCurrentUser] = useState<UserProfile | null>(null);
  const [isAuthorized, setIsAuthorized] = useState(false);
  const [balance, setBalance] = useState(0);
  const [transactions, setTransactions] = useState<Transaction[]>([]);
  const [modal, setModal] = useState<'deposit' | 'withdraw' | null>(null);
  const [holdings, setHoldings] = useState<Holding[]>([]);
  const [zakatHistory, setZakatHistory] = useState<ZakatRecord[]>([]);
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<DashboardTab>('home');
  const [lang, setLangState] = useState<SupportedLanguage>('en');
  const [showLangSelect, setShowLangSelect] = useState(false);
  const [showOnboarding, setShowOnboarding] = useState(false);
  const [darkMode, setDarkMode] = useState(false);

  const [showTradingPin, setShowTradingPin] = useState(false);
  const [pendingTrade, setPendingTrade] = useState<any>(null);
  const [isScanning, setIsScanning] = useState(false);
  const [pendingTx, setPendingTx] = useState<any>(null);
  const [showResetModal, setShowResetModal] = useState(false);

  const t = (en: string, ur: string) => lang === 'ur' ? ur : en;

  const toggleDark = (val: boolean) => {
    setDarkMode(val); localStorage.setItem('roziq_dark', val ? '1' : '0');
    if (val) document.documentElement.classList.add('dark');
    else document.documentElement.classList.remove('dark');
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    const savedLang = localStorage.getItem('roziq_lang') as 'en' | 'ur';
    const hasOnboarded = localStorage.getItem('roziq_onboarded');
    const savedDark = localStorage.getItem('roziq_dark');
    if (!savedLang) setShowLangSelect(true);
    else { setLangState(savedLang); if (!hasOnboarded) setShowOnboarding(true); }
    if (savedDark === '1') { setDarkMode(true); document.documentElement.classList.add('dark'); }
  }, []);

  const handleAuthSuccess = (user: UserProfile) => {
    setCurrentUser(user); setIsAuthorized(true);
    const cnicKey = user.cnic || 'default';
    const b = localStorage.getItem(`roziq_balance_${cnicKey}`);
    const h = localStorage.getItem(`roziq_holdings_${cnicKey}`);
    const txs = localStorage.getItem(`roziq_tx_${cnicKey}`);
    const zk = localStorage.getItem(`roziq_zakat_${cnicKey}`);

    if (b) setBalance(parseFloat(b));
    else { setBalance(5000000); localStorage.setItem(`roziq_balance_${cnicKey}`, '5000000'); }
    if (h) setHoldings(JSON.parse(h));
    if (txs) setTransactions(JSON.parse(txs));
    if (zk) setZakatHistory(JSON.parse(zk));
  };

  const persistUserData = (newBal: number, newHoldings: Holding[], newTxs: any[], newZk: ZakatRecord[]) => {
    if (!currentUser) return;
    const cnicKey = currentUser.cnic || 'default';
    localStorage.setItem(`roziq_balance_${cnicKey}`, newBal.toString());
    localStorage.setItem(`roziq_holdings_${cnicKey}`, JSON.stringify(newHoldings));
    localStorage.setItem(`roziq_tx_${cnicKey}`, JSON.stringify(newTxs));
    localStorage.setItem(`roziq_zakat_${cnicKey}`, JSON.stringify(newZk));
  };

  const handleLangSelect = (l: 'en' | 'ur') => {
    setLangState(l); localStorage.setItem('roziq_lang', l);
    setShowLangSelect(false);
    if (!localStorage.getItem('roziq_onboarded')) setShowOnboarding(true);
  };
  const handleLangChange = (l: 'en' | 'ur') => { localStorage.setItem('roziq_lang', l); setLangState(l); };

  const totalPortfolioValue = holdings.reduce((sum, h) => sum + h.invested + h.pnl, 0);
  const dailyPnl = holdings.reduce((sum, h) => sum + h.pnl, 0);
  const isZakatEligible = (balance + totalPortfolioValue) >= ZAKAT_NISAB_PKR;

  const totalInvestedHistory = holdings.reduce((sum, h) => sum + h.invested, 0);
  const totalRealizedProfit = transactions.reduce((sum, t) => sum + (t.profit || 0), 0);
  const isPremium = totalInvestedHistory >= WALI_INVESTED_THRESHOLD && totalRealizedProfit >= WALI_PROFIT_THRESHOLD;

  const handleTransactionSuccess = async (data: any) => {
    if (['buy_stock', 'buy_sukuk', 'zakat', 'withdraw', 'health_save', 'overseas_invest'].includes(data.type)) {
      setPendingTx(data); setIsScanning(true); return;
    }
    await processTransaction(data);
  };

  const processTransaction = async (data: any) => {
    let newBal = balance; let newHoldings = [...holdings]; let newTxs = [...transactions]; let newZk = [...zakatHistory];
    const txRecord: Transaction = {
      id: Date.now(),
      type: data.type,
      amount: data.amount,
      symbol: data.symbol || data.name || data.type,
      status: 'SUCCESS',
      date: new Date().toISOString(),
    };

    if (data.type === 'deposit') { newBal += data.amount; newTxs.unshift(txRecord); }
    else if (data.type === 'withdraw') { newBal -= data.amount; newTxs.unshift(txRecord); }
    else if (data.type === 'buy_stock') {
      newBal -= data.amount;
      const existing = newHoldings.find(h => h.symbol === data.symbol);
      if (existing) { existing.invested += data.amount; existing.units += data.units; }
      else { newHoldings.push({ symbol: data.symbol, name: data.name, invested: data.amount, currentPrice: data.amount / data.units, units: data.units, pnl: 0 }); }
      newTxs.unshift(txRecord);
    }
    else if (['buy_sukuk', 'health_save', 'overseas_invest'].includes(data.type)) { newBal -= data.amount; newTxs.unshift(txRecord); }
    else if (data.type === 'zakat') { newBal -= data.amount; newZk.unshift({ date: new Date().toISOString(), amount: data.amount, recipient: data.recipient }); newTxs.unshift(txRecord); }

    setBalance(newBal); setHoldings(newHoldings); setTransactions(newTxs); setZakatHistory(newZk);
    persistUserData(newBal, newHoldings, newTxs, newZk);
    setModal(null); setIsScanning(false); setPendingTx(null);
  };

  const onScanComplete = () => { if (pendingTx) processTransaction(pendingTx); };

  const initiateBuyStock = (symbol: string, amount: number) => {
    const stock = getStockBySymbol(symbol); if (!stock) return;
    setPendingTrade({ type: 'buy_stock', amount, symbol: stock.symbol, name: stock.name, units: amount / stock.price });
    setShowTradingPin(true);
  };

  const initiateSecureTransaction = (data: any) => {
    setPendingTrade(data);
    setShowTradingPin(true);
  };

  const handleTradingPinSuccess = () => {
    setShowTradingPin(false);
    if (pendingTrade) { handleTransactionSuccess(pendingTrade); setPendingTrade(null); }
  };

  const handleBuySukuk = (amount: number) => initiateSecureTransaction({ type: 'buy_sukuk', amount, symbol: 'SUKUK' });
  const handleZakatDonation = (amount: number, recipient: string) => initiateSecureTransaction({ type: 'zakat', amount, recipient, symbol: 'ZAKAT' });
  const handleHealthSave = (amount: number) => initiateSecureTransaction({ type: 'health_save', amount, symbol: 'HEALTH' });
  const handleOverseasInvest = (amount: number, asset: string) => initiateSecureTransaction({ type: 'overseas_invest', amount, name: asset, symbol: asset });
  const handleHajjDeposit = (amount: number) => initiateSecureTransaction({ type: 'buy_sukuk', amount, symbol: 'HAJJ' });
  const handleETFInvest = (amount: number, etf: string) => initiateSecureTransaction({ type: 'buy_sukuk', amount, symbol: etf });
  const handleKametiContribute = (amount: number) => initiateSecureTransaction({ type: 'buy_sukuk', amount, symbol: 'KAMETI' });

  const executeReset = () => {
    localStorage.clear();
    sessionStorage.clear();
    setCurrentUser(null);
    setIsAuthorized(false);
    setBalance(5000000);
    setHoldings([]);
    setTransactions([]);
    setZakatHistory([]);
    setShowResetModal(false);
    window.location.href = '/dashboard';
  };

  return (
    <>
      <ToastContainer />
      {showLangSelect && <LanguageSelect onSelect={handleLangSelect} />}
      {showOnboarding && !showLangSelect && <Onboarding lang={lang} onComplete={() => { setShowOnboarding(false); localStorage.setItem('roziq_onboarded', '1'); }} />}
      {!isAuthorized && !showLangSelect && !showOnboarding && <AuthGate onSuccess={handleAuthSuccess} />}
      {showTradingPin && <PinSecurity mode="trading" onSuccess={handleTradingPinSuccess} onCancel={() => { setShowTradingPin(false); setPendingTrade(null); }} />}
      
      {/* Custom Reset Confirmation Modal */}
      {showResetModal && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[5000] bg-slate-900/80 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-4 animate-in fade-in duration-200" dir={lang === 'ur' ? 'rtl' : 'ltr'}>
          <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 max-w-sm w-full text-center shadow-2xl border border-slate-100 space-y-4 animate-in zoom-in-95 duration-200">
            <div className="w-16 h-16 bg-red-100 text-red-600 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center text-3xl mx-auto shadow-inner border border-red-200">🗑️</div>
            <h3 className="text-lg font-black text-slate-800 tracking-tight">{t('Reset All Demo Data?', 'کیا آپ واقعی تمام فرضی ڈیٹا ری سیٹ کرنا چاہتے ہیں؟')}</h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 leading-relaxed font-medium">
              {t('This will permanently delete your CNIC account, virtual cash balance, and mock transaction history. You will be redirected to the onboarding screen.', 'اس سے آپ کا CNIC اکاؤنٹ، ورچوئل کیش بیلنس اور فرضی ٹرانزیکشن ہسٹری مکمل طور پر ڈیلیٹ ہو جائے گی۔ آپ کو دوبارہ لاگ ان کرنا ہوگا۔')}
            </p>
            <div className="flex gap-3 pt-2">
              <button 
                onClick={() => setShowResetModal(false)} 
                className="flex-1 py-3 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 font-bold rounded-xl text-xs transition-all active:scale-95 shadow-sm"
              >
                {t('Cancel', 'کینسل')}
              </button>
              <button 
                onClick={executeReset} 
                className="flex-1 py-3 bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-700 text-white font-black rounded-xl text-xs shadow-md transition-all active:scale-95"
              >
                {t('Yes, Delete All', 'ہاں، ڈیلیٹ کریں')}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Master AI Agent (Voice + Write + Linked Accounts) */}
      <RoziqAI balance={balance} holdings={holdings} transactions={transactions} lang={lang} />

      <div className="min-h-screen text-slate-900 selection:bg-emerald-100 selection:text-emerald-900 overflow-x-hidden pb-32" style={{ background: 'var(--bg-primary)' }} dir={lang === 'ur' ? 'rtl' : 'ltr'}>
        {/* Top Header */}
        <header className="sticky top-0 z-50 backdrop-blur-xl border-b px-4 sm:px-6 py-3 flex justify-between items-center shadow-sm" style={{ background: 'var(--bg-primary)', borderColor: 'var(--border-color)' }}>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 sm:gap-3">
            <div className="w-8 h-8 sm:w-10 sm:h-10 bg-gradient-to-br from-emerald-500 to-teal-600 rounded-xl sm:rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center text-base sm:text-lg shadow-md shadow-emerald-900/20">🇵🇰</div>
            <div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <h1 className="text-sm sm:text-base font-black tracking-tighter" style={{ color: 'var(--text-primary)' }}>ROZIQ</h1>
                <div className="hidden sm:flex items-center gap-1 bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-100 rounded-lg px-2 py-0.5">
                  <div className="w-1.5 h-1.5 bg-emerald-500 rounded-full animate-pulse" />
                  <span className="text-[8px] font-black text-emerald-600 uppercase tracking-wider">Live</span>
                </div>
              </div>
              <p className="text-[8px] sm:text-[9px] font-black text-emerald-600 uppercase tracking-[0.2em]">
                {currentUser ? currentUser.name : t('Wealth OS', 'ویلتھ او ایس')}
              </p>
            </div>
            {/* Desktop tab name breadcrumb */}
            <div className="hidden lg:flex items-center gap-1.5 ml-2" style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>
              <span className="text-xs">/</span>
              <span className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest capitalize" style={{ color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }}>
                {activeTab === 'home' ? 'Dashboard' : activeTab === 'portfolio' ? 'Halal Stocks' : activeTab === 'assets' ? 'Sovereign Assets' : activeTab === 'kameti' ? 'Family & Takaful' : activeTab === 'zakat' ? 'Zakat & Impact' : 'Settings'}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            {currentUser?.isOverseas && (
              <span className="px-2.5 py-1 bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border border-emerald-200 rounded-xl text-[9px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest hidden sm:inline-flex items-center gap-1">
                🌍 {currentUser.country || 'Overseas'}
              </span>
            )}

            {isPremium && (
              <span className="hidden md:inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 to-yellow-500 text-amber-900 rounded-xl text-[9px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest shadow-sm">
                ⭐ Elite
              </span>
            )}

            {/* Reset Demo Data */}
            <button
              onClick={() => setShowResetModal(true)}
              title={t('Delete Account & Reset Demo Data', 'اکاؤنٹ ڈیلیٹ کریں اور ڈیٹا ری سیٹ کریں')}
              className="px-2.5 py-1.5 sm:px-3 sm:py-2 bg-red-50 hover:bg-red-500 text-red-600 hover:text-white border border-red-200 hover:border-red-500 rounded-xl text-[10px] sm:text-xs font-black transition-all active:scale-95 flex items-center gap-1 shadow-sm"
            >
              <span>🗑️</span> <span className="hidden sm:inline">{t('Reset Demo', 'ری سیٹ')}</span>
            </button>

            <button className="w-8 h-8 sm:w-9 sm:h-9 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center border border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50 transition-all active:scale-90 text-xs sm:text-sm shadow-sm" style={{ background: 'var(--bg-card)' }}>
              🔔
            </button>

            <button
              onClick={() => setActiveTab('settings')}
              className={`w-8 h-8 sm:w-9 sm:h-9 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-br from-emerald-500 to-teal-600 border-2 shadow-md transition-all active:scale-90 ${activeTab === 'settings' ? 'border-emerald-400 scale-105 shadow-emerald-500/30' : 'border-white'}`}
            >
              <div className="w-full h-full rounded-xl border border-black/10 flex items-center justify-center text-white text-xs font-black">
                {currentUser ? currentUser.name.charAt(0).toUpperCase() : '⚙'}
              </div>
            </button>
          </div>
        </header>

        {/* Widescreen Responsive Layout Grid (max-w-7xl) */}
        <main className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-3 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-4 sm:py-6 animate-in fade-in duration-500">
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-4 sm:gap-6 lg:gap-8 items-start">
            
            {/* Left Column: Wallet Overview & Navigation */}
            <div className="lg:col-span-4 space-y-4 sm:space-y-6 sticky top-20 z-10">
              <BalanceCard balance={balance} portfolioValue={totalPortfolioValue} onDeposit={() => setModal('deposit')} onWithdraw={() => setModal('withdraw')} />
              
              {isPremium && (
                <div className="hidden lg:block">
                  <WaliAutopilot lang={lang} />
                </div>
              )}

              {/* Desktop Sidebar Navigation */}
              <div className="hidden lg:block rounded-3xl p-4 border shadow-sm space-y-1" style={{ background: 'var(--bg-card)', borderColor: 'var(--border-color)' }}>
                <p className="text-[9px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest px-3 mb-3" style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>
                  {t('Navigation', 'نیویگیشن')}
                </p>
                {[
                  { id: 'home', icon: '🏠', labelEn: 'Dashboard', labelUr: 'ڈیش بورڈ', sub: t('Overview & Activity', 'جائزہ') },
                  { id: 'portfolio', icon: '📈', labelEn: 'Halal Stocks', labelUr: 'حلال اسٹاکس', sub: t('PSX Screened', 'PSX اسکریند') },
                  { id: 'assets', icon: '🏛️', labelEn: 'Sovereign Assets', labelUr: 'خود مختار اثاثے', sub: t('Sukuk, Hajj, Agri', 'سکوک، حج') },
                  { id: 'kameti', icon: '🤝', labelEn: 'Family & Takaful', labelUr: 'فیملی ہیلتھ تکافل', sub: t('Health & Kameti', 'صحت') },
                  { id: 'zakat', icon: '🕋', labelEn: 'Zakat & Impact', labelUr: 'زکوٰۃ اور اثر', sub: t('Social Finance', 'سماجی مالیات') },
                ].map((tab) => (
                  <button
                    key={tab.id}
                    onClick={() => setActiveTab(tab.id as any)}
                    className={`w-full flex items-center gap-3 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-2xl text-left transition-all duration-200 group/nav ${
                      activeTab === tab.id
                        ? 'bg-emerald-600 text-white shadow-md shadow-emerald-900/20'
                        : 'hover:bg-emerald-50'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <div className={`w-9 h-9 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center text-base flex-shrink-0 transition-all ${
                      activeTab === tab.id ? 'bg-white/20' : 'bg-slate-100 group-hover/nav:bg-emerald-100'
                    }`}>
                      {tab.icon}
                    </div>
                    <div className="min-w-0">
                      <div className={`text-xs font-black tracking-tight ${activeTab === tab.id ? 'text-white' : ''}`} style={activeTab !== tab.id ? { color: 'var(--text-primary)' } : {}}>
                        {lang === 'ur' ? tab.labelUr : tab.labelEn}
                      </div>
                      <div className={`text-[9px] font-medium truncate ${activeTab === tab.id ? 'text-emerald-100' : ''}`} style={activeTab !== tab.id ? { color: 'var(--text-muted)' } : {}}>
                        {tab.sub}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    {activeTab === tab.id && <span className="ml-auto text-white/60 text-xs">→</span>}
                  </button>
                ))}

                {/* Zakat Eligibility Indicator */}
                {isZakatEligible && (
                  <div className="mx-1 mt-2 p-3 bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 rounded-2xl">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <span className="text-base">🕋</span>
                      <div>
                        <div className="text-[10px] font-black text-amber-800">{t('Zakat Due', 'زکوٰۃ واجب')}</div>
                        <div className="text-[9px] text-amber-600 font-medium">{t('You are Nisab eligible', 'آپ نصاب کی شرط پوری کرتے ہیں')}</div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}

                <div className="pt-3 mt-2 border-t px-1" style={{ borderColor: 'var(--border-color)' }}>
                  <button 
                    onClick={() => setShowResetModal(true)}
                    className="w-full py-2.5 px-3 bg-red-50 hover:bg-red-500 text-red-600 hover:text-white border border-red-200 hover:border-red-500 rounded-xl text-xs font-black transition-all active:scale-95 flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 shadow-sm"
                  >
                    <span>🗑️</span> {t('Delete Account & Demo Data', 'اکاؤنٹ اور فرضی ڈیٹا ڈیلیٹ کریں')}
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Right Column: Dynamic Tab Panels */}
            <div className="lg:col-span-8 space-y-4 sm:space-y-6">
              {/* HOME TAB */}
              {activeTab === 'home' && (
                <div className="space-y-4 sm:space-y-6 pb-20">
                  <div className="block lg:hidden">
                    {isPremium && <WaliAutopilot lang={lang} />}
                  </div>

                  <PortfolioChart balance={balance} holdings={holdings} totalValue={totalPortfolioValue} />

                  <section className="bg-white rounded-2xl sm:rounded-3xl p-4 sm:p-6 border border-slate-200 shadow-sm">
                    <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-3 sm:mb-4">
                      <h2 className="text-sm sm:text-base font-black text-slate-800 tracking-tight">{t('Recent Activity', 'حالیہ سرگرمی')}</h2>
                      <button className="text-[11px] sm:text-xs font-bold text-emerald-600 hover:underline">{t('View All', 'سب دیکھیں')}</button>
                    </div>
                    <div className="space-y-2">
                      {transactions.length === 0 ? (
                        <div className="p-6 text-center bg-slate-50 rounded-2xl border border-slate-100">
                          <p className="text-slate-400 text-xs font-medium">{t('No activity recorded yet.', 'ابھی تک کوئی سرگرمی ریکارڈ نہیں ہوئی۔')}</p>
                        </div>
                      ) : (
                        transactions.slice(0, 5).map((tx) => (
                          <div key={tx.id} className="group flex justify-between items-center p-3 sm:p-3.5 bg-slate-50/50 rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 hover:border-emerald-100 hover:bg-white hover:shadow-md transition-all duration-200">
                            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                              <div className={`w-9 h-9 sm:w-10 sm:h-10 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center text-sm sm:text-base shadow-sm ${tx.type === 'deposit' ? 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600' : tx.type === 'withdraw' ? 'bg-slate-50 text-slate-600' : tx.type === 'zakat_vault' ? 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-600' : 'bg-blue-50 text-blue-600'}`}>
                                {tx.type === 'deposit' ? '➕' : tx.type === 'withdraw' ? '➖' : tx.type === 'zakat_vault' ? '🏛️' : '📈'}
                              </div>
                              <div>
                                <div className="font-black text-slate-800 text-xs tracking-tight">
                                  {tx.type === 'deposit' ? t('Deposit', 'ڈیپازٹ') : tx.type === 'withdraw' ? t('نکالنا', 'نکالنا') : tx.type === 'zakat_vault' ? t('National Vault', 'قومی والٹ') : tx.symbol}
                                </div>
                                <div className="text-[8px] sm:text-[9px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase mt-0.5">
                                  {new Date(tx.date).toLocaleDateString(lang === 'ur' ? 'ur-PK' : 'en-US', { day: 'numeric', month: 'short' })}
                                </div>
                              </div>
                            </div>
                            <div className={`font-black text-xs ${tx.type === 'deposit' ? 'text-emerald-600' : 'text-slate-800'}`}>
                              {tx.type === 'deposit' ? '+' : '-'} {tx.amount.toLocaleString()}
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        ))
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </section>
                </div>
              )}

              {/* ASSETS TAB */}
              {activeTab === 'assets' && (
                <div className="space-y-4 sm:space-y-6 pb-20 animate-in fade-in duration-300">
                  <div className="mb-1">
                    <h2 className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-black text-slate-800 tracking-tight">{t('Sovereign Assets', 'خود مختار اثاثے')}</h2>
                    <p className="text-slate-500 text-xs mt-0.5">{t('Invest in land, Hajj, Sukuks, and Overseas funds.', 'زمین، حج، سکوک، اور اوورسیز فنڈز میں سرمایہ کاری کریں۔')}</p>
                  </div>
                  {currentUser?.isOverseas && (
                    <OverseasInvest balance={balance} onInvest={handleOverseasInvest} lang={lang} cnic={currentUser.cnic} country={currentUser.country} />
                  )}
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4 sm:gap-6 items-stretch">
                    <HajjVault onDeposit={handleHajjDeposit} />
                    <AgriFinancing balance={balance} onInvest={(amount) => initiateSecureTransaction({ type: 'buy_sukuk', amount, symbol: 'AGRI' })} />
                  </div>
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4 sm:gap-6 items-stretch">
                    <FractionalSukuk balance={balance} onBuy={handleBuySukuk} />
                    <GlobalHalalETFs onInvest={handleETFInvest} />
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}

              {/* PORTFOLIO TAB */}
              {activeTab === 'portfolio' && (
                <div className="space-y-4 sm:space-y-6 pb-20 animate-in fade-in duration-300">
                  <div className="mb-1">
                    <h2 className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-black text-slate-800 tracking-tight">{t('My Portfolio', 'میرا پورٹ فولیو')}</h2>
                    <p className="text-slate-500 text-xs mt-0.5">{t('Real-time tracking of your Halal wealth.', 'آپ کی حلال دولت کی اصل وقت میں ٹریکنگ۔')}</p>
                  </div>
                  <PortfolioDashboard holdings={holdings} totalValue={totalPortfolioValue} dailyPnl={dailyPnl} />
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-12 gap-4 sm:gap-6 items-start">
                    <div className="md:col-span-7">
                      <PortfolioChart balance={balance} holdings={holdings} totalValue={totalPortfolioValue} />
                    </div>
                    <div className="md:col-span-5 space-y-2.5 bg-white rounded-2xl sm:rounded-3xl p-4 sm:p-6 border border-slate-200 shadow-sm">
                      <h3 className="text-xs font-black text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest px-1">{t('Halal Marketplace', 'حلال مارکیٹ پلیس')}</h3>
                      <HalalStockScanner balance={balance} onBuy={initiateBuyStock} />
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}

              {/* KAMETI & HEALTH TAB */}
              {activeTab === 'kameti' && (
                <div className="space-y-4 sm:space-y-6 pb-20 animate-in fade-in duration-300">
                  <FamilyHealthSaving balance={balance} onSave={handleHealthSave} lang={lang} cnic={currentUser?.cnic} />
                  <DigitalCommittee onContribute={handleKametiContribute} />
                  <WirasatCalculator />
                </div>
              )}

              {/* ZAKAT TAB */}
              {activeTab === 'zakat' && (
                <div className="space-y-4 sm:space-y-6 pb-20 animate-in fade-in duration-300">
                  <div className="mb-1">
                    <h2 className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-black text-slate-800 tracking-tight">{t('Social Impact', 'سماجی اثر')}</h2>
                    <p className="text-slate-500 text-xs mt-0.5">{t('Purify wealth and empower the Ummah.', 'دولت کو پاک کریں اور امت کو بااختیار بنائیں۔')}</p>
                  </div>
                  <ZakatDistribution balance={balance} portfolioValue={totalPortfolioValue} />
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4 sm:gap-6 items-stretch">
                    <PurificationTracker onPurify={(amount) => handleZakatDonation(amount, 'Dividend Purification')} />
                    <FinancialHealthScore balance={balance} portfolioValue={totalPortfolioValue} holdings={holdings} transactions={transactions} zakatHistory={zakatHistory} isZakatEligible={isZakatEligible} />
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}

              {/* SETTINGS TAB */}
              {activeTab === 'settings' && (
                <div className="space-y-4 sm:space-y-6 pb-20 animate-in fade-in duration-300">
                  <SettingsTab lang={lang} darkMode={darkMode} onLangChange={handleLangChange} onDarkToggle={toggleDark} onLock={() => setIsAuthorized(false)} isPremium={isPremium} eligibility={{ totalInvested: totalInvestedHistory, totalProfit: totalRealizedProfit, reqInvest: 250000000, reqProfit: 500000000 }} />
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </main>

        {modal && (
          <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-slate-900/40 backdrop-blur-md flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-0 sm:p-4 z-[60] animate-in fade-in">
            <div className="relative w-full max-w-md bg-white rounded-t-[2.5rem] sm:rounded-[2.5rem] p-5 sm:p-6 shadow-2xl animate-in slide-in-from-bottom-full duration-300">
              <button onClick={() => setModal(null)} className="absolute top-5 right-6 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 transition-colors">✕</button>
              <div className="mt-1">
                {modal === 'deposit' ? <DepositFlow onSuccess={handleTransactionSuccess} /> : <WithdrawFlow onSuccess={handleTransactionSuccess} />}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Bottom Navigation Bar (Visible only on mobile/tablet) */}
        <div className="fixed bottom-4 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 w-[96%] max-w-lg z-50 block lg:hidden">
          <nav className="glass-dark rounded-[2rem] p-1.5 flex justify-between items-center shadow-2xl border-white/10 overflow-hidden bg-slate-900/90 backdrop-blur-lg text-white">
            {[
              { id: 'home', icon: '🏠', labelEn: 'Home', labelUr: 'ہوم' },
              { id: 'portfolio', icon: '💼', labelEn: 'Stocks', labelUr: 'اسٹاکس' },
              { id: 'assets', icon: '🚜', labelEn: 'Assets', labelUr: 'اثاثے' },
              { id: 'kameti', icon: '🤝', labelEn: 'Family', labelUr: 'خاندان' },
              { id: 'zakat', icon: '🕋', labelEn: 'Zakat', labelUr: 'زکوٰۃ' },
            ].map((tab) => (
              <button key={tab.id} onClick={() => setActiveTab(tab.id as any)}
                className={`relative flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-2 px-3 rounded-2xl transition-all duration-300 group ${activeTab === tab.id ? 'bg-emerald-600 text-white shadow-md shadow-emerald-900/20' : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-white'}`}>
                <span className={`text-base sm:text-lg transition-transform duration-300 ${activeTab === tab.id ? 'scale-110 -translate-y-0.5' : 'group-hover:scale-110'}`}>{tab.icon}</span>
                <span className={`text-[7px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest absolute transition-all duration-300 ${activeTab === tab.id ? 'opacity-100 translate-y-2.5' : 'opacity-0 translate-y-5'}`}>
                  {lang === 'ur' ? tab.labelUr : tab.labelEn}
                </span>
              </button>
            ))}
          </nav>
        </div>

        <SovereignScan isOpen={isScanning} onComplete={onScanComplete} />
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

}

  --bg-muted: #1e293b;

  --text-secondary: #94a3b8;

  --shadow-color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.4);
  --glass-bg: rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.85);
  --glass-border: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
}

@layer base {
  html {
    scroll-behavior: smooth;
    -webkit-tap-highlight-color: transparent;
    font-feature-settings: "cv02", "cv03", "cv04", "cv11";
  }
  body {
    font-family: "Inter", -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", sans-serif;
    background-color: var(--bg-primary);
    color: var(--text-primary);
    background-image:
      radial-gradient(at 0% 0%, rgba(34, 197, 94, 0.04) 0px, transparent 50%),

/* FILE: src\components\AiMuftiChatbot.tsx */
/* ================================================================ */

'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';

export default function AiMuftiChatbot() {
  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);
  const [messages, setMessages] = useState<{role: 'user'|'mufti', text: string}[]>([
    { role: 'mufti', text: 'Assalamu Alaikum! I am your AI Shariah Advisor. Ask me anything about Halal investing, Zakat, or AAOIFI standards.' }
  ]);
  const [input, setInput] = useState('');

  const handleSend = () => {
    if (!input.trim()) return;
    setMessages(prev => [...prev, { role: 'user', text: input }]);
    const currentInput = input;
    setInput('');
    
    // Simulate AI response based on keywords
    setTimeout(() => {
      let response = 'According to AAOIFI standards, please ensure the core business is halal and interest-bearing debt is less than 33% of market cap.';
      if (currentInput.toLowerCase().includes('purification') || currentInput.toLowerCase().includes('impure')) {
        response = 'If a company earns a small amount from interest (under 5%), you must purify your dividends by donating that exact percentage to charity without expecting spiritual reward.';
      } else if (currentInput.toLowerCase().includes('sukuk')) {
        response = 'Sukuk are Islamic financial certificates, similar to bonds in Western finance, but they comply with Shariah. They represent ownership in an underlying asset rather than a debt obligation.';
      }
      setMessages(prev => [...prev, { role: 'mufti', text: response }]);
    }, 1000);
  };

  return (
    <>
      {/* Floating Action Button */}
      <button 
        onClick={() => setIsOpen(true)}
        className="fixed bottom-6 right-6 bg-emerald-700 text-white p-4 rounded-full shadow-2xl hover:bg-emerald-800 transition-transform hover:scale-105 z-50 flex items-center justify-center"
      >
        <span className="text-2xl mr-2">👳🏽‍♂️</span>
        <span className="font-bold hidden md:inline">Ask AI Mufti</span>
      </button>

      {/* Chat Window */}
      {isOpen && (
        <div className="fixed bottom-24 right-6 w-80 md:w-96 bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-2xl border border-gray-200 z-50 flex flex-col overflow-hidden">
          <div className="bg-emerald-700 text-white p-4 flex justify-between items-center">
            <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
              <span className="text-2xl">👳🏽‍♂️</span>
              <h3 className="font-bold">AI Shariah Advisor</h3>
            </div>
            <button onClick={() => setIsOpen(false)} className="text-white hover:text-gray-200 text-xl">✕</button>
          </div>
          
          <div className="flex-1 p-4 h-80 overflow-y-auto bg-gray-50 space-y-4">
            {messages.map((msg, idx) => (
              <div key={idx} className={`flex ${msg.role === 'user' ? 'justify-end' : 'justify-start'}`}>
                <div className={`max-w-[80%] p-3 rounded-2xl text-sm ${
                  msg.role === 'user' 
                    ? 'bg-emerald-600 text-white rounded-tr-none' 
                    : 'bg-white text-gray-800 border border-gray-200 rounded-tl-none shadow-sm'
                }`}>
                  {msg.text}
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>

          <div className="p-3 bg-white border-t border-gray-100 flex items-center space-x-2">
            <input 
              type="text" 
              value={input}
              onChange={(e) => setInput(e.target.value)}
              onKeyPress={(e) => e.key === 'Enter' && handleSend()}
              placeholder="Type your question..."
              className="flex-1 p-2 bg-gray-100 rounded-xl text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-500"
            />
            <button 
              onClick={handleSend}
              className="bg-emerald-700 text-white p-2 rounded-xl hover:bg-emerald-800"
            >
              Send
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </>
  );
}



/* ================================================================ */
/* FILE: src\components\AuthGate.tsx */
/* ================================================================ */

'use client';
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';

interface AuthGateProps {
  onSuccess: (user: UserProfile) => void;
}

export interface UserProfile {
  name: string;
  cnic: string;
  mobile: string;
  location?: string;
  isOverseas: boolean;
  country?: string;
  tradingPin: string;
}

type Screen = 'login' | 'register' | 'biometric' | 'trading-pin';

const COUNTRIES = [
  'Saudi Arabia', 'UAE', 'UK', 'USA', 'Canada', 'Australia',
  'Qatar', 'Kuwait', 'Bahrain', 'Oman', 'Germany', 'Italy', 'Other'
];

export default function AuthGate({ onSuccess }: AuthGateProps) {
  const [screen, setScreen] = useState<Screen>('login');
  const [cnic, setCnic] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [mobile, setMobile] = useState('');
  const [location, setLocation] = useState('');
  const [isLocating, setIsLocating] = useState(false);
  const [confirmPassword, setConfirmPassword] = useState('');
  const [isOverseas, setIsOverseas] = useState(false);
  const [country, setCountry] = useState('');
  const [tradingPin, setTradingPin] = useState('');
  const [confirmTradingPin, setConfirmTradingPin] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [biometricAvailable, setBiometricAvailable] = useState(false);
  const [pendingUser, setPendingUser] = useState<UserProfile | null>(null);

  // Format CNIC as 42101-1234567-1
  const formatCnic = (val: string) => {
    const digits = val.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 13);
    if (digits.length <= 5) return digits;
    if (digits.length <= 12) return `${digits.slice(0,5)}-${digits.slice(5)}`;
    return `${digits.slice(0,5)}-${digits.slice(5,12)}-${digits.slice(12)}`;
  };

  // Format Mobile as 0300-1234567
  const formatMobile = (val: string) => {
    const digits = val.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 11);
    if (digits.length <= 4) return digits;
    return `${digits.slice(0,4)}-${digits.slice(4)}`;
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (window.PublicKeyCredential) {
      PublicKeyCredential.isUserVerifyingPlatformAuthenticatorAvailable()
        .then(ok => setBiometricAvailable(ok))
        .catch(() => {});
    }
    // Auto-login check
    const saved = localStorage.getItem('roziq_user');
    const savedPw = localStorage.getItem('roziq_pw');
    if (saved && savedPw) {
      const u: UserProfile = JSON.parse(saved);
      setPendingUser(u);
      if (biometricAvailable) setScreen('biometric');
    }
  }, [biometricAvailable]);

  const getUsers = (): Record<string, { pw: string; user: UserProfile }> => {
    try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('roziq_users') || '{}'); } catch { return {}; }
  };

  const handleGetLiveLocation = () => {
    setIsLocating(true);
    setError('');
    if (navigator.geolocation) {
      navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition(
        () => {
          // Simulate clean reverse geocoding for Pakistan / Hackathon demo
          const cities = ['Karachi, Sindh', 'Lahore, Punjab', 'Islamabad, Capital Territory', 'Peshawar, KPK'];
          const randomCity = cities[Math.floor(Math.random() * cities.length)];
          setLocation(`${randomCity} (GPS Verified 📍)`);
          setIsLocating(false);
        },
        () => {
          setLocation('Karachi, Sindh (Default GPS 📍)');
          setIsLocating(false);
        },
        { timeout: 5000 }
      );
    } else {
      setLocation('Karachi, Sindh (Default GPS 📍)');
      setIsLocating(false);
    }
  };

  const handleLogin = () => {
    setError('');
    const cnicRaw = cnic.replace(/\D/g, '');
    if (cnicRaw.length !== 13) { setError('Enter valid 13-digit CNIC'); return; }
    if (!password) { setError('Enter your password'); return; }
    const users = getUsers();
    const entry = users[cnicRaw];
    if (!entry || entry.pw !== btoa(password)) { setError('Invalid CNIC or password'); return; }
    localStorage.setItem('roziq_user', JSON.stringify(entry.user));
    localStorage.setItem('roziq_pw', entry.pw);
    onSuccess(entry.user);
  };

  const handleRegister = () => {
    setError('');
    const cnicRaw = cnic.replace(/\D/g, '');
    const mobileRaw = mobile.replace(/\D/g, '');
    if (!name.trim()) { setError('Enter your full name'); return; }
    if (cnicRaw.length !== 13) { setError('Enter valid 13-digit CNIC'); return; }
    if (mobileRaw.length < 10) { setError('Enter valid Mobile Number'); return; }
    if (!location.trim()) { setError('Please fetch your Live Location'); return; }
    if (password.length < 6) { setError('Password must be at least 6 characters'); return; }
    if (password !== confirmPassword) { setError('Passwords do not match'); return; }
    if (isOverseas && !country) { setError('Select your country of residence'); return; }

    const users = getUsers();
    if (users[cnicRaw]) { setError('Account with this CNIC already exists. Please login.'); return; }

    const newUser: UserProfile = {
      name: name.trim(),
      cnic: cnicRaw,
      mobile: mobileRaw,
      location: location.trim(),
      isOverseas,
      country: isOverseas ? country : undefined,
      tradingPin: ''
    };
    setPendingUser(newUser);
    users[cnicRaw] = { pw: btoa(password), user: newUser };
    localStorage.setItem('roziq_users', JSON.stringify(users));
    localStorage.setItem('roziq_user', JSON.stringify(newUser));
    localStorage.setItem('roziq_pw', btoa(password));
    // Initialize user balance
    if (!localStorage.getItem(`roziq_balance_${cnicRaw}`)) {
      localStorage.setItem(`roziq_balance_${cnicRaw}`, '5000000'); // Seed 5 Million PKR for Hackathon demo
      localStorage.setItem(`roziq_tx_${cnicRaw}`, '[]');
      localStorage.setItem(`roziq_holdings_${cnicRaw}`, '[]');
    }
    setScreen('trading-pin');
  };

  const handleTradingPin = () => {
    setError('');
    if (tradingPin.length !== 4 || !/^\d{4}$/.test(tradingPin)) { setError('Trading PIN must be 4 digits'); return; }
    if (tradingPin !== confirmTradingPin) { setError('PINs do not match'); return; }
    if (!pendingUser) return;
    const updated = { ...pendingUser, tradingPin };
    const users = getUsers();
    users[updated.cnic] = { pw: users[updated.cnic]?.pw || '', user: updated };
    localStorage.setItem('roziq_users', JSON.stringify(users));
    localStorage.setItem('roziq_user', JSON.stringify(updated));
    localStorage.setItem('roziq_pin', tradingPin);
    localStorage.setItem('roziq_trading_pin', tradingPin);
    onSuccess(updated);
  };

  const handleBiometricLogin = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const challenge = new Uint8Array(32);
      crypto.getRandomValues(challenge);
      await navigator.credentials.get({
        publicKey: { challenge, timeout: 60000, userVerification: 'required', rpId: window.location.hostname, allowCredentials: [] }
      });
      if (pendingUser) onSuccess(pendingUser);
    } catch {
      // Fallback: auto-approve for devices without registered credential
      if (pendingUser) onSuccess(pendingUser);
    } finally { setLoading(false); }
  };

  const handleGoogleLogin = () => {
    // For hackathon demo: simulate Google OAuth success
    const googleUser: UserProfile = {
      name: 'Muhammad Ahsan (Demo)',
      cnic: '4210112345671',
      mobile: '03001234567',
      location: 'Karachi, Sindh (GPS Verified 📍)',
      isOverseas: false,
      tradingPin: '1234'
    };
    localStorage.setItem('roziq_user', JSON.stringify(googleUser));
    localStorage.setItem('roziq_pin', '1234');
    localStorage.setItem('roziq_trading_pin', '1234');
    onSuccess(googleUser);
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[2000] bg-slate-950 flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-4 overflow-y-auto">
      {/* Background glow */}
      <div className="absolute top-0 right-0 w-72 h-72 bg-emerald-500/15 rounded-full blur-[100px] pointer-events-none" />
      <div className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 w-56 h-56 bg-teal-500/10 rounded-full blur-[80px] pointer-events-none" />

      <div className="relative z-10 w-full max-w-sm my-auto py-6">
        {/* Logo */}
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center mb-6">
          <div className="w-14 h-14 bg-emerald-600 rounded-3xl flex items-center justify-center text-3xl shadow-2xl shadow-emerald-900/50 mb-3">🇵🇰</div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-black text-white tracking-tighter">Roziq</h1>
          <p className="text-emerald-400 text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-[0.25em] mt-1">Pakistan's Halal Wealth OS</p>
        </div>

        {/* ── LOGIN SCREEN ── */}
        {screen === 'login' && (
          <div className="bg-slate-900 border border-white/10 rounded-3xl p-6 space-y-4 shadow-xl">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-black text-white text-center">Welcome Back</h2>
            <p className="text-slate-400 text-xs text-center font-medium">Login with your CNIC &amp; Password</p>

            {error && <p className="text-red-400 text-xs font-bold text-center bg-red-900/20 py-2 px-3 rounded-xl">{error}</p>}

            <div>
              <label className="text-[10px] font-black text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest block mb-1.5">CNIC</label>
              <input
                type="text" inputMode="numeric" value={cnic}
                onChange={e => setCnic(formatCnic(e.target.value))}
                placeholder="42101-1234567-1"
                className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-slate-800 border border-white/10 rounded-2xl text-white font-mono text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-emerald-500 transition-colors"
              />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="text-[10px] font-black text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest block mb-1.5">Password</label>
              <input
                type="password" value={password} onChange={e => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                onKeyDown={e => e.key === 'Enter' && handleLogin()}
                placeholder="••••••••"
                className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-slate-800 border border-white/10 rounded-2xl text-white text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-emerald-500 transition-colors"
              />
            </div>
            <button onClick={handleLogin} className="w-full py-3 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white font-black rounded-2xl transition-all active:scale-95 text-sm shadow-md">
              Login
            </button>

            {biometricAvailable && (
              <button onClick={handleBiometricLogin}
                className="w-full py-3 bg-slate-800 border border-white/10 text-slate-300 font-bold rounded-2xl transition-all active:scale-95 text-sm flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-sm">
                <span>🫱</span> Fingerprint / Face ID
              </button>
            )}
            <button onClick={handleGoogleLogin}
              className="w-full py-3 bg-slate-800 border border-white/10 text-slate-300 font-bold rounded-2xl transition-all active:scale-95 text-sm flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-sm">
              <span>🔵</span> Continue with Google (Demo)
            </button>

            <p className="text-center text-xs text-slate-500 font-medium pt-1">
              Don't have an account?{' '}
              <button onClick={() => { setScreen('register'); setError(''); }}
                className="text-emerald-400 font-black hover:text-emerald-300 transition-colors">Register</button>
            </p>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* ── REGISTER SCREEN ── */}
        {screen === 'register' && (
          <div className="bg-slate-900 border border-white/10 rounded-3xl p-6 space-y-4 shadow-xl">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-black text-white text-center">Create Sovereign Account</h2>
            {error && <p className="text-red-400 text-xs font-bold text-center bg-red-900/20 py-2 px-3 rounded-xl">{error}</p>}

            <div>
              <label className="text-[10px] font-black text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest block mb-1">Full Name</label>
              <input type="text" value={name} onChange={e => setName(e.target.value)} placeholder="Muhammad Ali"
                className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 bg-slate-800 border border-white/10 rounded-xl text-white text-xs focus:outline-none focus:border-emerald-500 transition-colors" />
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
              <div>
                <label className="text-[10px] font-black text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest block mb-1">CNIC Number</label>
                <input type="text" inputMode="numeric" value={cnic} onChange={e => setCnic(formatCnic(e.target.value))} placeholder="42101-1234567-1"
                  className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 bg-slate-800 border border-white/10 rounded-xl text-white font-mono text-xs focus:outline-none focus:border-emerald-500 transition-colors" />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="text-[10px] font-black text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest block mb-1">Mobile Number</label>
                <input type="text" inputMode="numeric" value={mobile} onChange={e => setMobile(formatMobile(e.target.value))} placeholder="0300-1234567"
                  className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 bg-slate-800 border border-white/10 rounded-xl text-white font-mono text-xs focus:outline-none focus:border-emerald-500 transition-colors" />
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Live Location Picker */}
            <div>
              <label className="text-[10px] font-black text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest block mb-1">Live GPS Location</label>
              <div className="flex gap-2 items-center">
                <input type="text" readOnly value={location} placeholder="Click button to fetch location..."
                  className="flex-1 px-3.5 py-2.5 bg-slate-800/60 border border-white/10 rounded-xl text-emerald-300 font-bold text-xs outline-none" />
                <button 
                  onClick={handleGetLiveLocation} 
                  disabled={isLocating}
                  className="px-3 py-2.5 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white rounded-xl text-xs font-black transition-all active:scale-95 flex items-center gap-1 flex-shrink-0 shadow-md disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  {isLocating ? '⏳ Locating...' : '📍 Get Live GPS'}
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
              <div>
                <label className="text-[10px] font-black text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest block mb-1">Password</label>
                <input type="password" value={password} onChange={e => setPassword(e.target.value)} placeholder="••••••••"
                  className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 bg-slate-800 border border-white/10 rounded-xl text-white text-xs focus:outline-none focus:border-emerald-500 transition-colors" />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="text-[10px] font-black text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest block mb-1">Confirm Password</label>
                <input type="password" value={confirmPassword} onChange={e => setConfirmPassword(e.target.value)} placeholder="••••••••"
                  className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 bg-slate-800 border border-white/10 rounded-xl text-white text-xs focus:outline-none focus:border-emerald-500 transition-colors" />
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Overseas toggle */}
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-3 bg-slate-800 rounded-xl border border-white/10">
              <div>
                <p className="text-white font-bold text-xs">🌍 Overseas Pakistani?</p>
                <p className="text-slate-400 text-[10px] mt-0.5">Living or working abroad</p>
              </div>
              <button onClick={() => setIsOverseas(v => !v)}
                className={`w-10 h-5 rounded-full transition-colors flex items-center px-1 ${isOverseas ? 'bg-emerald-500' : 'bg-slate-600'}`}>
                <div className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 rounded-full bg-white transition-transform ${isOverseas ? 'translate-x-5' : ''}`} />
              </button>
            </div>

            {isOverseas && (
              <div>
                <label className="text-[10px] font-black text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest block mb-1">Country of Residence</label>
                <select value={country} onChange={e => setCountry(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 bg-slate-800 border border-white/10 rounded-xl text-white text-xs focus:outline-none focus:border-emerald-500">
                  <option value="">Select Country</option>
                  {COUNTRIES.map(c => <option key={c} value={c}>{c}</option>)}
                </select>
              </div>
            )}

            <button onClick={handleRegister} className="w-full py-3 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white font-black rounded-xl transition-all active:scale-95 text-xs shadow-md">
              Continue to PIN Setup →
            </button>
            <p className="text-center text-xs text-slate-500 font-medium">
              Already registered?{' '}
              <button onClick={() => { setScreen('login'); setError(''); }} className="text-emerald-400 font-black hover:text-emerald-300">Login</button>
            </p>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* ── TRADING PIN SETUP ── */}
        {screen === 'trading-pin' && (
          <div className="bg-slate-900 border border-white/10 rounded-3xl p-6 space-y-4 text-center shadow-xl">
            <div className="text-3xl mb-1">🔑</div>
            <h2 className="text-lg font-black text-white">Set Your 4-Digit Security PIN</h2>
            <p className="text-slate-400 text-xs font-medium leading-relaxed">
              This 4-digit PIN is required every time you <strong className="text-white">buy stocks, Sukuks, or transfer assets</strong>.
            </p>
            {error && <p className="text-red-400 text-xs font-bold bg-red-900/20 py-2 px-3 rounded-xl">{error}</p>}
            <div>
              <label className="text-[10px] font-black text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest block mb-1.5">Enter 4-Digit PIN</label>
              <input type="password" inputMode="numeric" maxLength={4} value={tradingPin}
                onChange={e => setTradingPin(e.target.value.replace(/\D/g,'').slice(0,4))}
                placeholder="••••"
                className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-slate-800 border border-white/10 rounded-xl text-white text-center text-2xl tracking-[1em] focus:outline-none focus:border-emerald-500 transition-colors"
              />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="text-[10px] font-black text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest block mb-1.5">Confirm 4-Digit PIN</label>
              <input type="password" inputMode="numeric" maxLength={4} value={confirmTradingPin}
                onChange={e => setConfirmTradingPin(e.target.value.replace(/\D/g,'').slice(0,4))}
                placeholder="••••"
                className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-slate-800 border border-white/10 rounded-xl text-white text-center text-2xl tracking-[1em] focus:outline-none focus:border-emerald-500 transition-colors"
              />
            </div>
            <button onClick={handleTradingPin} className="w-full py-3 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white font-black rounded-xl transition-all active:scale-95 text-xs shadow-md">
              Save PIN &amp; Enter Dashboard →
            </button>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* ── BIOMETRIC QUICK LOGIN ── */}
        {screen === 'biometric' && pendingUser && (
          <div className="bg-slate-900 border border-white/10 rounded-3xl p-8 text-center space-y-6 shadow-xl">
            <div className="text-5xl">👋</div>
            <h2 className="text-lg font-black text-white">Welcome back,<br/><span className="text-emerald-400">{pendingUser.name}</span></h2>
            <button onClick={handleBiometricLogin} disabled={loading}
              className="w-full py-4 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white font-black rounded-xl transition-all active:scale-95 text-sm flex items-center justify-center gap-3 shadow-md">
              <span className="text-2xl">{loading ? '⏳' : '🫱'}</span>
              {loading ? 'Verifying...' : 'Use Fingerprint / Face ID'}
            </button>
            <button onClick={() => { setScreen('login'); setPendingUser(null); localStorage.removeItem('roziq_user'); localStorage.removeItem('roziq_pw'); }}
              className="text-slate-500 text-xs font-bold hover:text-slate-300 transition-colors">
              Use CNIC &amp; Password instead
            </button>
          </div>
        )}

        <p className="text-center text-slate-600 text-[10px] font-bold mt-6 uppercase tracking-widest">
          🔒 End-to-end encrypted · CNIC &amp; GPS verified · AAOIFI Halal Certified
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* FILE: src\components\BalanceCard.tsx */

﻿"use client";
import { useState } from "react";

interface BalanceCardProps {
  balance: number;
  onDeposit: () => void;
  onWithdraw: () => void;
  portfolioValue?: number;
}

export default function BalanceCard({ balance, onDeposit, onWithdraw, portfolioValue = 0 }: BalanceCardProps) {
  const [showBalance, setShowBalance] = useState(true);
  const totalWealth = balance + portfolioValue;

  const formatPKR = (n: number) =>
    n >= 10_000_000
      ? `${(n / 10_000_000).toFixed(2)} Cr`
      : n >= 100_000
      ? `${(n / 100_000).toFixed(1)} L`
      : n.toLocaleString("en-PK");

  return (
    <div className="relative overflow-hidden bg-gradient-to-br from-emerald-600 via-emerald-700 to-teal-800 rounded-[2.5rem] p-6 sm:p-8 text-white shadow-2xl shadow-emerald-900/30 group transition-all duration-500 hover:shadow-emerald-900/40">
      {/* Decorative orbs */}
      <div className="absolute -top-16 -right-16 w-48 h-48 sm:w-64 sm:h-64 bg-white/10 rounded-full blur-3xl group-hover:bg-white/15 transition-all duration-700" />
      <div className="absolute -bottom-16 -left-16 w-40 h-40 sm:w-52 sm:h-52 bg-emerald-400/20 rounded-full blur-2xl group-hover:bg-emerald-400/30 transition-all duration-700" />
      <div className="absolute top-1/2 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-32 h-32 bg-teal-300/10 rounded-full blur-2xl" />

      <div className="relative z-10">
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-5 sm:mb-6">
          <div>
            <p className="text-emerald-100/70 text-[10px] sm:text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-[0.2em] mb-0.5">
              Total Cash Balance
            </p>
            <div className="flex items-baseline gap-2">
              <h2 className="text-3xl sm:text-4xl font-black tracking-tight leading-none">
                {showBalance ? (
                  <>
                    <span className="text-xl sm:text-2xl mr-1 opacity-60 font-bold">PKR</span>
                    {balance.toLocaleString("en-PK")}
                  </>
                ) : (
                  <span className="text-xl tracking-[0.3em] font-black">••••••••</span>
                )}
              </h2>
            </div>
            {portfolioValue > 0 && showBalance && (
              <p className="text-emerald-200/70 text-[10px] sm:text-xs font-bold mt-1">
                + ₨{formatPKR(portfolioValue)} invested • Total: ₨{formatPKR(totalWealth)}
              </p>
            )}
          </div>
          <div className="flex flex-col items-end gap-2">
            <div className="w-11 h-11 sm:w-12 sm:h-12 bg-white/10 backdrop-blur-xl rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center border border-white/20 shadow-inner">
              <span className="text-xl sm:text-2xl">🕌</span>
            </div>
            <button
              onClick={() => setShowBalance(!showBalance)}
              className="text-emerald-200/70 hover:text-white transition-colors text-xs font-bold"
            >
              {showBalance ? "👁️" : "🙈"}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Shariah Badge */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-5 sm:mb-6">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-white/10 border border-white/20 rounded-xl px-3 py-1.5">
            <div className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-300 animate-pulse" />
            <span className="text-[10px] font-black text-emerald-100 uppercase tracking-wider">Shariah Certified</span>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-white/10 border border-white/20 rounded-xl px-3 py-1.5">
            <span className="text-[10px] font-black text-emerald-100 uppercase tracking-wider">0% Commission</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Action Buttons */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
          <button
            onClick={onDeposit}
            className="relative py-3.5 sm:py-4 bg-white text-emerald-800 rounded-2xl font-black text-sm shadow-lg shadow-black/10 hover:bg-emerald-50 transition-all duration-200 active:scale-95 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 overflow-hidden group/btn"
          >
            <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-50 to-white opacity-0 group-hover/btn:opacity-100 transition-opacity" />
            <span className="relative z-10">💰</span>
            <span className="relative z-10">Deposit</span>
          </button>

          <button
            onClick={onWithdraw}
            className="py-3.5 sm:py-4 bg-emerald-900/40 border border-white/20 text-white rounded-2xl font-black text-sm backdrop-blur-md hover:bg-emerald-900/60 transition-all duration-200 active:scale-95 flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
          >
            <span>💸</span>
            <span>Withdraw</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}



/* ================================================================ */
/* FILE: src\components\CommunityChallenges.tsx */
/* ================================================================ */

'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';

interface Challenge {
  id: number;
  title: string;
  target: number;
  current: number;
  reward: string;
  icon: string;
}

const INITIAL_CHALLENGES: Challenge[] = [
  { id: 1, title: '100,000 Pakistanis Invest PKR 1,000', target: 100000, current: 84231, reward: 'Platform donates PKR 10M to Flood Relief', icon: '🇵🇰' },
  { id: 2, title: 'Collective PKR 1B in Green Sukuk', target: 1000000000, current: 650000000, reward: 'Platform plants 1 Million Trees', icon: '🌱' },
  { id: 3, title: '10,000 Users Complete Financial Course', target: 10000, current: 8940, reward: 'Free Hajj Draw for 10 Participants', icon: '📚' },
];

export default function CommunityChallenges() {
  const [challenges, setChallenges] = useState<Challenge[]>(INITIAL_CHALLENGES);
  const [joined, setJoined] = useState<Set<number>>(new Set());

  const handleJoin = (id: number) => {
    if (joined.has(id)) return;
    setJoined(prev => new Set([...prev, id]));
    setChallenges(prev =>
      prev.map(c => c.id === id ? { ...c, current: c.current + 1 } : c)
    );
  };

  const formatNumber = (n: number) => {
    if (n >= 1000000000) return `${(n / 1000000000).toFixed(1)}B`;
    if (n >= 1000000) return `${(n / 1000000).toFixed(1)}M`;
    return n.toLocaleString();
  };

  return (
    <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 p-4 mb-6">
      <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
        <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800">🤝 Community Challenges</h2>
        <span className="text-xs bg-blue-100 text-blue-800 px-2 py-1 rounded-full">Nation Building</span>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-4">
        {challenges.map((c) => {
          const progress = Math.min(100, (c.current / c.target) * 100);
          const isJoined = joined.has(c.id);
          return (
            <div key={c.id} className={`p-4 rounded-xl border transition ${isJoined ? 'bg-green-50 border-green-200' : 'bg-gray-50 border-gray-200 hover:border-blue-300'}`}>
              <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                <span className="text-2xl">{c.icon}</span>
                <div className="flex-1">
                  <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-800 text-sm md:text-base">{c.title}</h3>
                  <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-1"> Reward: {c.reward}</p>
                  
                  <div className="mt-3">
                    <div className="flex justify-between text-xs mb-1">
                      <span className="text-gray-600">{formatNumber(c.current)} / {formatNumber(c.target)}</span>
                      <span className="font-bold text-blue-600">{progress.toFixed(1)}%</span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="w-full h-2 bg-gray-200 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                      <div 
                        className={`h-full rounded-full transition-all duration-1000 ${isJoined ? 'bg-green-500' : 'bg-blue-500'}`} 
                        style={{ width: `${progress}%` }}
                      ></div>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {!isJoined && (
                    <button 
                      onClick={() => handleJoin(c.id)}
                      className="mt-3 w-full py-2 bg-blue-600 text-white text-sm font-medium rounded-lg hover:bg-blue-700 transition"
                    >
                      Join This Challenge
                    </button>
                  )}
                  {isJoined && (
                    <div className="mt-2 text-center text-green-700 text-xs font-semibold">
                      ✅ You Joined! Together we grow.
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}



/* ================================================================ */
/* FILE: src\components\DepositFlow.tsx */
/* ================================================================ */

'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';

const PAYMENT_METHODS = [
  { id: 'easypaisa', name: 'EasyPaisa', icon: '💚', color: 'bg-green-50 border-green-200', activeColor: 'bg-green-600', type: 'mobile' },
  { id: 'jazzcash', name: 'JazzCash', icon: '🔴', color: 'bg-red-50 border-red-200', activeColor: 'bg-red-600', type: 'mobile' },
  { id: 'meezan', name: 'Meezan Bank', icon: '🏦', color: 'bg-blue-50 border-blue-200', activeColor: 'bg-blue-600', type: 'bank' },
  { id: 'hbl', name: 'HBL', icon: '🏛️', color: 'bg-emerald-50 border-emerald-200', activeColor: 'bg-emerald-600', type: 'bank' },
  { id: 'ubl', name: 'UBL', icon: '💳', color: 'bg-indigo-50 border-indigo-200', activeColor: 'bg-indigo-600', type: 'bank' },
];

const QUICK_AMOUNTS = [5000, 10000, 25000, 50000, 100000, 500000];

export default function DepositFlow({ onSuccess }: { onSuccess: (data: any) => void }) {
  const [step, setStep] = useState<'method' | 'amount' | 'confirm' | 'processing' | 'success'>('method');
  const [method, setMethod] = useState<any>(null);
  const [amount, setAmount] = useState('');
  const [mobileNumber, setMobileNumber] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [txRef, setTxRef] = useState('');
  const [instructions, setInstructions] = useState('');
  const [bankDetails, setBankDetails] = useState<any>(null);

  const selectedMethod = PAYMENT_METHODS.find(m => m.id === method);

  const handleMethodSelect = (id: string) => {
    setMethod(id);
    setError('');
    setStep('amount');
  };

  const handleAmountNext = () => {
    const num = parseFloat(amount);
    if (!num || num < 100) { setError('Minimum deposit is PKR 100'); return; }
    if (num > 5000000) { setError('Maximum deposit is PKR 50 Lakh'); return; }
    setError('');
    setStep('confirm');
  };

  const handleConfirm = async () => {
    setStep('processing');
    setError('');
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/payment/deposit', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ amount: parseFloat(amount), method, mobileNumber }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (data.success) {
        setTxRef(data.txRef);
        setInstructions(data.instructions || '');
        setBankDetails(data.bankDetails || null);
        // Now commit to our wallet
        await onSuccess({ type: 'deposit', amount: parseFloat(amount), method: selectedMethod?.name, txRef: data.txRef });
        setStep('success');
      } else {
        setError(data.message || 'Payment failed');
        setStep('confirm');
      }
    } catch {
      setError('Network error. Please try again.');
      setStep('confirm');
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* STEP: Method Selection */}
      {step === 'method' && (
        <div>
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-black text-slate-900 mb-2">Add Funds</h2>
          <p className="text-slate-500 text-sm mb-6">Choose your payment method</p>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3">
            {PAYMENT_METHODS.map(m => (
              <button
                key={m.id}
                onClick={() => handleMethodSelect(m.id)}
                className={`flex items-center gap-4 p-4 rounded-2xl border-2 transition-all active:scale-95 hover:shadow-md ${m.color}`}
              >
                <span className="text-3xl">{m.icon}</span>
                <div className="text-left">
                  <div className="font-black text-slate-800">{m.name}</div>
                  <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">{m.type === 'mobile' ? 'Mobile Wallet · Instant' : 'Bank Transfer · 1-2 hrs'}</div>
                </div>
                <span className="ml-auto text-slate-400">›</span>
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* STEP: Amount */}
      {step === 'amount' && (
        <div>
          <button onClick={() => setStep('method')} className="text-slate-400 text-sm mb-4 flex items-center gap-1">‹ Back</button>
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-black text-slate-900 mb-1">Enter Amount</h2>
          <p className="text-slate-500 text-sm mb-6">via <span className="font-bold text-slate-700">{selectedMethod?.name}</span></p>

          {selectedMethod?.type === 'mobile' && (
            <div className="mb-4">
              <label className="text-xs font-black text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest">Mobile Number</label>
              <input
                type="tel"
                placeholder="03XX-XXXXXXX"
                value={mobileNumber}
                onChange={e => setMobileNumber(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full mt-2 p-4 rounded-2xl border-2 border-slate-200 focus:border-emerald-400 outline-none font-bold text-slate-800 text-lg"
              />
            </div>
          )}

          <label className="text-xs font-black text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest">Amount (PKR)</label>
          <div className="relative mt-2 mb-4">
            <span className="absolute left-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400 font-black text-xl">₨</span>
            <input
              type="number"
              placeholder="0"
              value={amount}
              onChange={e => setAmount(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-4 rounded-2xl border-2 border-slate-200 focus:border-emerald-400 outline-none font-black text-slate-800 text-2xl"
            />
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 mb-6">
            {QUICK_AMOUNTS.map(a => (
              <button key={a} onClick={() => setAmount(String(a))} className="py-2 px-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 hover:bg-emerald-50 hover:text-emerald-700 text-slate-600 font-bold text-xs transition-all">
                {a >= 100000 ? `${a/100000}L` : `${a/1000}K`}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>

          {error && <p className="text-red-500 text-sm mb-3 font-medium">{error}</p>}
          <button onClick={handleAmountNext} className="w-full py-4 bg-emerald-600 text-white rounded-2xl font-black text-lg hover:bg-emerald-700 active:scale-95 transition-all shadow-lg shadow-emerald-900/20">
            Continue →
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* STEP: Confirm */}
      {step === 'confirm' && (
        <div>
          <button onClick={() => setStep('amount')} className="text-slate-400 text-sm mb-4 flex items-center gap-1">‹ Back</button>
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-black text-slate-900 mb-6">Confirm Deposit</h2>
          <div className="bg-slate-50 rounded-3xl p-6 space-y-3 mb-6">
            <div className="flex justify-between"><span className="text-slate-500">Method</span><span className="font-black">{selectedMethod?.icon} {selectedMethod?.name}</span></div>
            {mobileNumber && <div className="flex justify-between"><span className="text-slate-500">Mobile</span><span className="font-bold">{mobileNumber}</span></div>}
            <div className="flex justify-between text-lg"><span className="text-slate-500">Amount</span><span className="font-black text-emerald-600">₨ {parseFloat(amount).toLocaleString()}</span></div>
          </div>
          {error && <p className="text-red-500 text-sm mb-3 font-medium">{error}</p>}
          <button onClick={handleConfirm} className="w-full py-4 bg-emerald-600 text-white rounded-2xl font-black text-lg hover:bg-emerald-700 active:scale-95 transition-all shadow-lg shadow-emerald-900/20">
            Confirm & Pay
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* STEP: Processing */}
      {step === 'processing' && (
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-16 space-y-4">
          <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-full border-4 border-emerald-200 border-t-emerald-600 animate-spin" />
          <p className="font-black text-slate-800 text-xl">Processing Payment...</p>
          <p className="text-slate-400 text-sm">Connecting to {selectedMethod?.name}</p>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* STEP: Success */}
      {step === 'success' && (
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center text-center py-8 space-y-4">
          <div className="w-20 h-20 rounded-full bg-emerald-100 flex items-center justify-center text-4xl">✅</div>
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-black text-slate-900">Deposit Successful!</h2>
          <p className="text-slate-500">₨ {parseFloat(amount).toLocaleString()} added to your Halal Wallet</p>
          {txRef && <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 font-mono bg-slate-100 px-4 py-2 rounded-xl">Ref: {txRef}</p>}
          {instructions && <p className="text-sm text-slate-600 bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-100 rounded-2xl p-4">{instructions}</p>}
          {bankDetails && (
            <div className="w-full text-left bg-slate-50 rounded-2xl p-4 space-y-2 text-sm">
              <p className="font-black text-slate-700 mb-2">Bank Transfer Details:</p>
              <p><span className="text-slate-400">Account:</span> <span className="font-bold">{bankDetails.accountTitle}</span></p>
              <p><span className="text-slate-400">Number:</span> <span className="font-mono font-bold">{bankDetails.accountNumber}</span></p>
              <p><span className="text-slate-400">IBAN:</span> <span className="font-mono text-xs">{bankDetails.iban}</span></p>
              <p><span className="text-slate-400">Bank:</span> <span className="font-bold">{bankDetails.bank}</span></p>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
