            Invite Family
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}



/* ================================================================ */
/* FILE: src\components\FamilyHealthSaving.tsx */
/* ================================================================ */

'use client';
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';

interface FamilyHealthSavingProps {
  balance: number;
  onSave: (amount: number) => void;
  lang?: 'en' | 'ur';
  cnic?: string;
}

interface Member {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  relation: string;
  age: number;
  monthlyAllocation: number;
  claimed: number;
}

export default function FamilyHealthSaving({ balance, onSave, lang = 'en', cnic = 'default' }: FamilyHealthSavingProps) {
  const isUrdu = lang === 'ur';
  const t = (en: string, ur: string) => isUrdu ? ur : en;

  const storageKey = `roziq_health_pool_${cnic}`;
  const membersKey = `roziq_health_members_${cnic}`;

  const [poolBalance, setPoolBalance] = useState(150000);
  const [members, setMembers] = useState<Member[]>([
    { id: '1', name: 'Ammi Jan', relation: 'Mother', age: 62, monthlyAllocation: 10000, claimed: 25000 },
    { id: '2', name: 'Abbu Jan', relation: 'Father', age: 68, monthlyAllocation: 15000, claimed: 40000 },
    { id: '3', name: 'Ayesha', relation: 'Spouse', age: 31, monthlyAllocation: 8000, claimed: 5000 },
    { id: '4', name: 'Ibrahim', relation: 'Child', age: 6, monthlyAllocation: 5000, claimed: 0 }
  ]);

  const [showAdd, setShowAdd] = useState(false);
  const [showClaim, setShowClaim] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [showDeposit, setShowDeposit] = useState(false);
  const [depositAmount, setDepositAmount] = useState('');
  const [claimAmount, setClaimAmount] = useState('');
  const [claimReason, setClaimReason] = useState('');

  // Add member form
  const [newName, setNewName] = useState('');
  const [newRelation, setNewRelation] = useState('');
  const [newAge, setNewAge] = useState('');
  const [newAlloc, setNewAlloc] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    const savedPool = localStorage.getItem(storageKey);
    const savedMembers = localStorage.getItem(membersKey);
    if (savedPool) setPoolBalance(parseFloat(savedPool));
    if (savedMembers) setMembers(JSON.parse(savedMembers));
  }, [storageKey, membersKey]);

  const updateStorage = (pool: number, mems: Member[]) => {
    localStorage.setItem(storageKey, pool.toString());
    localStorage.setItem(membersKey, JSON.stringify(mems));
  };

  const handleAddDeposit = () => {
    setError('');
    const amt = parseFloat(depositAmount);
    if (isNaN(amt) || amt <= 0) { setError(t('Enter a valid amount', 'درست رقم درج کریں')); return; }
    if (amt > balance) { setError(t('Insufficient main wallet balance', 'مین والٹ میں ناکافی بیلنس')); return; }

    const newPool = poolBalance + amt;
    setPoolBalance(newPool);
    onSave(amt); // Protected by PIN in dashboard
    updateStorage(newPool, members);
    setDepositAmount('');
    setShowDeposit(false);
  };

  const handleAddMember = () => {
    setError('');
    if (!newName || !newRelation || !newAge || !newAlloc) {
      setError(t('Please fill all fields', 'براہ کرم تمام خانے پُر کریں'));
      return;
    }
    const newM: Member = {
      id: Date.now().toString(),
      name: newName,
      relation: newRelation,
      age: parseInt(newAge) || 25,
      monthlyAllocation: parseFloat(newAlloc) || 5000,
      claimed: 0
    };
    const updated = [...members, newM];
    setMembers(updated);
    updateStorage(poolBalance, updated);
    setNewName(''); setNewRelation(''); setNewAge(''); setNewAlloc('');
    setShowAdd(false);
  };

  const handleClaim = (memberId: string) => {
    setError('');
    const amt = parseFloat(claimAmount);
    if (isNaN(amt) || amt <= 0) { setError(t('Enter a valid claim amount', 'درست کلیم کی رقم درج کریں')); return; }
    if (amt > poolBalance) { setError(t('Insufficient funds in Health Pool', 'ہیلتھ پول میں ناکافی فنڈز')); return; }

    const updatedMems = members.map(m => m.id === memberId ? { ...m, claimed: m.claimed + amt } : m);
    const newPool = poolBalance - amt;

    setPoolBalance(newPool);
    setMembers(updatedMems);
    updateStorage(newPool, updatedMems);
    setClaimAmount(''); setClaimReason(''); setShowClaim(null);
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-2.5 sm:space-y-4 animate-in fade-in duration-500 mb-3 sm:mb-6" dir={isUrdu ? 'rtl' : 'ltr'}>
      {/* Header */}
      <div>
        <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 sm:gap-2 px-2.5 py-0.5 bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 rounded-full text-[9px] sm:text-xs font-bold mb-1 border border-emerald-100 shadow-inner">
          <span>🩺</span> {t('Takaful Protection', 'تکافل تحفظ')}
        </div>
        <h2 className="text-sm sm:text-2xl font-black text-slate-800 tracking-tight">
          {t('Family Health Savings', 'فیملی ہیلتھ سیونگز')}
        </h2>
        <p className="text-slate-500 text-[10px] sm:text-xs mt-0.5 leading-relaxed max-w-2xl">
          {t('A Shariah-compliant emergency medical fund for your family. Earn Halal dividends while keeping funds liquid.', 'آپ کے خاندان کے لیے شریعہ کے مطابق ہنگامی طبی فنڈ۔ صحت کی دیکھ بھال کی ضروریات کے لیے فنڈز کو مائع رکھتے ہوئے حلال منافع کمائیں۔')}
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Overview Card — Compact Mobile Padding */}
      <div className="bg-gradient-to-br from-emerald-600 to-teal-700 rounded-2xl sm:rounded-[2.5rem] p-3 sm:p-5 text-white shadow-lg shadow-emerald-900/20 relative overflow-hidden border border-emerald-500/20">
        <div className="absolute top-0 right-0 w-24 h-24 sm:w-48 sm:h-48 bg-white/10 rounded-full blur-[30px] pointer-events-none" />
        <div className="relative z-10 flex flex-col sm:flex-row justify-between items-start sm:items-end gap-2 sm:gap-0 mb-2.5 sm:mb-5">
          <div>
            <p className="text-[8px] sm:text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-emerald-200 mb-0.5">
              {t('Available Health Pool', 'دستیاب ہیلتھ پول')}
            </p>
            <h3 className="text-base sm:text-3xl font-black tracking-tight">Rs {poolBalance.toLocaleString()}</h3>
          </div>
          <button onClick={() => setShowDeposit(true)}
            className="w-full sm:w-auto px-3 py-1.5 sm:px-5 sm:py-2.5 bg-white text-emerald-700 rounded-xl sm:rounded-2xl font-black text-[10px] sm:text-xs shadow-md hover:bg-emerald-50 transition-all active:scale-95 text-center flex-shrink-0">
            + {t('Add Funds (PIN Locked)', 'فنڈز شامل کریں')}
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 sm:gap-4 pt-2 sm:pt-4 border-t border-white/15 text-[10px] sm:text-xs">
          <div>
            <p className="text-emerald-200 font-medium">{t('Active Covered Members', 'فعال زیر کفالت ارکان')}</p>
            <p className="font-bold text-xs sm:text-base mt-0.5">{members.length} {t('Members', 'ارکان')}</p>
          </div>
          <div>
            <p className="text-emerald-200 font-medium">{t('Total Medical Claims', 'کل طبی کلیمز')}</p>
            <p className="font-bold text-xs sm:text-base mt-0.5">Rs {members.reduce((s,m) => s + m.claimed, 0).toLocaleString()}</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Deposit Modal */}
      {showDeposit && (
        <div className="p-3 sm:p-5 bg-white rounded-2xl sm:rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xl space-y-2.5 sm:space-y-4 animate-in slide-in-from-top-2 duration-200">
          <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
            <h4 className="font-bold text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-800">{t('Deposit to Health Pool', 'ہیلتھ پول میں جمع کریں')}</h4>
            <button onClick={() => setShowDeposit(false)} className="text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 text-xs sm:text-sm">✕</button>
          </div>
          {error && <p className="text-[10px] sm:text-xs font-bold text-red-500 bg-red-50 p-2 rounded-xl">{error}</p>}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-2 sm:gap-3 items-end">
            <div>
              <label className="text-[8px] sm:text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest block mb-1">
                {t('Amount (PKR)', 'رقم (روپے)')}
              </label>
              <input type="number" value={depositAmount} onChange={e => setDepositAmount(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="50000"
                className="w-full px-3 py-1.5 sm:px-4 sm:py-2.5 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl sm:rounded-2xl text-slate-800 font-bold focus:outline-none focus:border-emerald-500 text-xs sm:text-sm outline-none" />
              <p className="text-[8px] sm:text-[10px] text-slate-400 mt-1">{t('Current Wallet Balance:', 'موجودہ والٹ بیلنس:')} Rs {balance.toLocaleString()}</p>
            </div>
            <button onClick={handleAddDeposit} className="w-full py-2 sm:py-2.5 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white font-black rounded-xl sm:rounded-2xl transition-all text-xs sm:text-sm shadow-md active:scale-95 flex-shrink-0">
              {t('Confirm Transfer (PIN Locked)', 'ٹرانسفر کی تصدیق کریں')}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Member Management Section */}
      <div className="space-y-2 sm:space-y-3">
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center px-1">
          <h3 className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-black text-slate-800 tracking-tight">{t('Covered Family Members', 'زیر کفالت خاندانی ارکان')}</h3>
          <button onClick={() => setShowAdd(true)} className="text-[10px] sm:text-xs font-bold text-emerald-600 hover:text-emerald-700 flex items-center gap-1">
            <span>+</span> {t('Add Member', 'رکن شامل کریں')}
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Add Member Form */}
        {showAdd && (
          <div className="p-3 sm:p-5 bg-slate-100 rounded-2xl sm:rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 space-y-2.5 sm:space-y-4 animate-in fade-in slide-in-from-top-2 duration-200 shadow-sm">
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
              <h4 className="font-bold text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-800">{t('Add Family Member', 'خاندانی رکن شامل کریں')}</h4>
              <button onClick={() => setShowAdd(false)} className="text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 text-xs sm:text-sm">✕</button>
            </div>
            {error && <p className="text-[10px] sm:text-xs font-bold text-red-50 bg-red-50 p-2 rounded-xl">{error}</p>}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-2 sm:gap-3 items-end">
              <div>
                <label className="text-[8px] sm:text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-500 block mb-1">{t('Full Name', 'پورا نام')}</label>
                <input type="text" value={newName} onChange={e => setNewName(e.target.value)} placeholder="Ammi Jan"
                  className="w-full p-2 sm:p-2.5 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl sm:rounded-2xl text-xs outline-none focus:border-emerald-500" />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="text-[8px] sm:text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-500 block mb-1">{t('Relation', 'رشتہ')}</label>
                <input type="text" value={newRelation} onChange={e => setNewRelation(e.target.value)} placeholder="Mother"
                  className="w-full p-2 sm:p-2.5 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl sm:rounded-2xl text-xs outline-none focus:border-emerald-500" />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="text-[8px] sm:text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-500 block mb-1">{t('Age', 'عمر')}</label>
                <input type="number" value={newAge} onChange={e => setNewAge(e.target.value)} placeholder="62"
                  className="w-full p-2 sm:p-2.5 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl sm:rounded-2xl text-xs outline-none focus:border-emerald-500" />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="text-[8px] sm:text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-500 block mb-1">{t('Monthly Alloc (PKR)', 'ماہانہ مختص')}</label>
                <input type="number" value={newAlloc} onChange={e => setNewAlloc(e.target.value)} placeholder="10000"
                  className="w-full p-2 sm:p-2.5 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl sm:rounded-2xl text-xs outline-none focus:border-emerald-500" />
              </div>
            </div>
            <button onClick={handleAddMember} className="w-full py-2 sm:py-2.5 bg-emerald-600 text-white font-bold rounded-xl sm:rounded-2xl text-xs hover:bg-emerald-500 shadow-md active:scale-95 transition-all">
              {t('Save Member', 'رکن محفوظ کریں')}
            </button>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Member List - Responsive 2-Column Grid on Desktop */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-2 sm:gap-4 items-start">
          {members.map(m => (
            <div key={m.id} className="p-2.5 sm:p-4 bg-white rounded-xl sm:rounded-3xl border border-slate-100 shadow-sm hover:border-emerald-200 transition-all space-y-2 flex flex-col justify-between h-full">
              <div>
                <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-1.5 sm:mb-2">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 sm:gap-3">
                    <div className="w-7 h-7 sm:w-9 sm:h-9 rounded-xl bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600 flex items-center justify-center text-xs sm:text-base font-bold flex-shrink-0 border border-emerald-100">
                      {m.name.charAt(0)}
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <h4 className="font-bold text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-800 tracking-tight">{m.name}</h4>
                      <p className="text-[9px] sm:text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-medium">{m.relation} · {m.age} {t('years old', 'سال عمر')}</p>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <button onClick={() => setShowClaim(m.id)}
                    className="px-2 py-1 sm:px-3.5 sm:py-1.5 bg-slate-50 hover:bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 rounded-xl font-bold text-[9px] sm:text-xs border border-slate-100 hover:border-emerald-200 transition-all flex-shrink-0 active:scale-95 shadow-sm">
                    {t('File Claim', 'کلیم کریں')}
                  </button>
                </div>

                {/* Claim Form */}
                {showClaim === m.id && (
                  <div className="pt-2 sm:pt-2.5 border-t border-slate-100 space-y-2 animate-in fade-in duration-200 mb-1.5 sm:mb-2">
                    <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
                      <p className="text-[10px] sm:text-xs font-bold text-slate-700">{t('File Medical Claim', 'طبی کلیم جمع کریں')}</p>
                      <button onClick={() => setShowClaim(null)} className="text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 text-xs">✕</button>
                    </div>
                    {error && <p className="text-[10px] sm:text-xs font-bold text-red-500 bg-red-50 p-1.5 rounded-xl">{error}</p>}
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-1.5">
                      <div>
                        <label className="text-[8px] font-bold text-slate-400 block mb-0.5">{t('Claim Amount', 'کلیم کی رقم')}</label>
                        <input type="number" value={claimAmount} onChange={e => setClaimAmount(e.target.value)} placeholder="15000"
                          className="w-full p-1.5 sm:p-2 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold outline-none focus:border-emerald-500" />
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <label className="text-[8px] font-bold text-slate-400 block mb-0.5">{t('Medical Reason', 'طبی وجہ')}</label>
                        <input type="text" value={claimReason} onChange={e => setClaimReason(e.target.value)} placeholder="Doctor Visit / Meds"
                          className="w-full p-1.5 sm:p-2 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-xs outline-none focus:border-emerald-500" />
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <button onClick={() => handleClaim(m.id)} className="w-full py-1.5 sm:py-2 bg-emerald-600 text-white font-bold rounded-xl text-xs hover:bg-emerald-500 shadow-md active:scale-95 transition-all">
                      {t('Submit Claim Request', 'کلیم کی درخواست جمع کریں')}
                    </button>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>

              <div className="flex justify-between items-center pt-1.5 sm:pt-2 border-t border-slate-50 text-[9px] sm:text-[10px]">
                <span className="text-slate-400">{t('Monthly Alloc:', 'ماہانہ مختص:')} <strong className="text-slate-700 font-bold">Rs {m.monthlyAllocation.toLocaleString()}</strong></span>
                <span className="text-slate-400">{t('Claimed:', 'کلیم کردہ:')} <strong className="text-emerald-600 font-bold">Rs {m.claimed.toLocaleString()}</strong></span>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* FILE: src\components\FinancialHealthScore.tsx */
/* ================================================================ */

'use client';
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';

interface HealthScoreProps {
  balance: number;
  portfolioValue: number;
  holdings: any[];
  transactions: any[];
  zakatHistory: any[];
  isZakatEligible: boolean;
}

interface Badge {
  id: string;
  icon: string;
  title: string;
  desc: string;
  unlocked: boolean;
}

export default function FinancialHealthScore({
  balance,
  portfolioValue,
  holdings,
  transactions,
  zakatHistory,
  isZakatEligible
}: HealthScoreProps) {
  const [score, setScore] = useState(0);
  const [badges, setBadges] = useState<Badge[]>([]);
  const [streak, setStreak] = useState(0);
  const [tips, setTips] = useState<string[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    // 1. Calculate Score (0-100)
    let s = 0;
    
    // Emergency Fund (30 pts)
    const emergencyTarget = Math.max(portfolioValue * 0.2, 5000);
    s += Math.min(30, (balance / emergencyTarget) * 30);

    // Diversification (25 pts)
    s += Math.min(25, (holdings.length / 4) * 25);

    // Consistency (20 pts)
    s += Math.min(20, (transactions.length / 5) * 20);

    // Zakat Compliance (15 pts)
    if (!isZakatEligible || (isZakatEligible && zakatHistory.length > 0)) s += 15;

    // Activity (10 pts)
    const lastTx = transactions[0]?.date ? new Date(transactions[0].date) : new Date(0);
    const daysSince = (Date.now() - lastTx.getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24);
    s += daysSince <= 7 ? 10 : 5;

    setScore(Math.min(100, Math.round(s)));

    // 2. Generate Tips based on weak areas
    const t: string[] = [];
    if (balance < emergencyTarget) t.push('💡 Add PKR 500 to your emergency fund for +5 points');
    if (holdings.length < 3) t.push('📊 Buy 1 more sector (e.g., Tech or Energy) to diversify');
    if (isZakatEligible && zakatHistory.length === 0) t.push('🤲 Pay Zakat to unlock the "Zakat Champion" badge');
    if (daysSince > 3) t.push('🔥 Make a small investment or deposit to keep your streak alive');
    setTips(t);

    // 3. Determine Badges
    const b: Badge[] = [
      { id: 'first', icon: '🌱', title: 'First Seed', desc: 'Made your first investment', unlocked: transactions.some(t => t.type.includes('buy')) },
      { id: 'safety', icon: '🛡️', title: 'Safety Net', desc: 'Emergency fund > 20% of portfolio', unlocked: balance >= emergencyTarget },
      { id: 'diverse', icon: '📊', title: 'Diversifier', desc: 'Hold 3+ different assets', unlocked: holdings.length >= 3 },
      { id: 'zakat', icon: '🤲', title: 'Zakat Champion', desc: 'Paid Zakat when eligible', unlocked: isZakatEligible && zakatHistory.length > 0 },
      { id: 'streak', icon: '🔥', title: '7-Day Streak', desc: 'Active for 7 days', unlocked: daysSince <= 1 && transactions.length >= 7 },
    ];
    setBadges(b);
    setStreak(Math.min(7, Math.floor(transactions.length / 2))); // Mock streak for demo
  }, [balance, portfolioValue, holdings, transactions, zakatHistory, isZakatEligible]);

  const getScoreColor = () => {
    if (score >= 80) return 'text-green-600 stroke-green-600';
    if (score >= 60) return 'text-yellow-600 stroke-yellow-600';
    return 'text-red-600 stroke-red-600';
  };

  const circumference = 2 * Math.PI * 40;
  const strokeDashoffset = circumference - (score / 100) * circumference;

  return (
    <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 p-4 sm:p-5 mb-4 sm:mb-6">
      <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-3 sm:mb-4">
        <h2 className="text-base sm:text-lg font-bold text-gray-800 tracking-tight">📈 Financial Health</h2>
        <span className={`text-[10px] sm:text-xs px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full font-medium ${
          score >= 80 ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-800' : score >= 60 ? 'bg-yellow-100 text-yellow-800' : 'bg-red-100 text-red-800'
        }`}>
          {score >= 80 ? 'Excellent' : score >= 60 ? 'Good' : 'Needs Work'}
        </span>
      </div>

      {/* Score Circle + Streak */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4 sm:mb-6">
        <div className="relative w-20 h-20 sm:w-24 sm:h-24 flex-shrink-0">
          <svg className="w-full h-full transform -rotate-90" viewBox="0 0 100 100">
            <circle cx="50" cy="50" r="40" stroke="#e5e7eb" strokeWidth="8" fill="none" />
            <circle 
              cx="50" cy="50" r="40" 
              stroke="currentColor" 
              strokeWidth="8" 
              fill="none" 
              strokeDasharray={circumference} 
              strokeDashoffset={strokeDashoffset}
              strokeLinecap="round"
              className={`transition-all duration-1000 ease-out ${getScoreColor()}`}
            />
          </svg>
          <div className="absolute inset-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center">
            <span className={`text-xl sm:text-2xl font-black tracking-tight ${getScoreColor()}`}>{score}</span>
            <span className="text-[9px] sm:text-[10px] text-gray-500 font-bold">/ 100</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="text-right flex-shrink-0">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1 sm:gap-1.5 mb-0.5 sm:mb-1 justify-end">
            <span className="text-lg sm:text-xl">🔥</span>
            <span className="text-sm sm:text-base font-black text-orange-600 tracking-tight">{streak} Day Streak</span>
          </div>
          <p className="text-[10px] sm:text-xs text-gray-500">Keep investing daily to grow your streak</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Metric Breakdown */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 sm:gap-3 mb-4 sm:mb-6">
        <div className="p-2.5 sm:p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-xl">
          <div className="text-[10px] sm:text-xs text-gray-500">Emergency Fund</div>
          <div className="font-semibold text-xs sm:text-sm text-gray-800 mt-0.5">{Math.min(100, Math.round((balance / Math.max(portfolioValue * 0.2, 5000)) * 100))}%</div>
        </div>
        <div className="p-2.5 sm:p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-xl">
          <div className="text-[10px] sm:text-xs text-gray-500">Diversification</div>
          <div className="font-semibold text-xs sm:text-sm text-gray-800 mt-0.5">{Math.min(100, Math.round((holdings.length / 4) * 100))}%</div>
        </div>
        <div className="p-2.5 sm:p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-xl">
          <div className="text-[10px] sm:text-xs text-gray-500">Consistency</div>
          <div className="font-semibold text-xs sm:text-sm text-gray-800 mt-0.5">{Math.min(100, Math.round((transactions.length / 5) * 100))}%</div>
        </div>
        <div className="p-2.5 sm:p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-xl">
          <div className="text-[10px] sm:text-xs text-gray-500">Zakat Status</div>
          <div className="font-semibold text-xs sm:text-sm text-gray-800 mt-0.5">{(!isZakatEligible || zakatHistory.length > 0) ? '✅ Compliant' : '⏳ Pending'}</div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Badges */}
      <div className="mb-3 sm:mb-4">
        <h3 className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 mb-2 px-0.5">🏆 Badges</h3>
        <div className="flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto pb-1.5 scrollbar-none">
          {badges.map(b => (
            <div key={b.id} className={`flex-shrink-0 w-16 sm:w-20 p-2 rounded-xl text-center border transition-all ${
              b.unlocked ? 'border-green-300 bg-green-50 shadow-sm' : 'border-gray-200 bg-gray-50 opacity-40'
            }`}>
              <div className="text-lg sm:text-xl mb-1">{b.icon}</div>
              <div className="text-[9px] sm:text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-700 leading-tight truncate">{b.title}</div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Actionable Tips */}
      {tips.length > 0 && (
        <div className="p-3 bg-blue-50 border border-blue-100 rounded-xl shadow-inner">
          <h4 className="text-[10px] sm:text-xs font-bold text-blue-800 mb-1">💡 How to Improve</h4>
          <ul className="space-y-1">
            {tips.map((tip, i) => (
              <li key={i} className="text-[10px] sm:text-xs text-blue-700 flex items-start gap-1 font-medium">
                <span>•</span> {tip}
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

/* FILE: src\components\FractionalSukuk.tsx */
/* ================================================================ */

'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';

const SUKUKS = [
  { id: 'gop-green', name: 'GoP Green Ijarah Sukuk', yield: '18.5%', maturity: '1 Year', type: 'Sovereign', risk: 'Low', minInvestment: 500 },
  { id: 'wapda-energy', name: 'WAPDA Energy Sukuk II', yield: '19.2%', maturity: '3 Years', type: 'Corporate', risk: 'Medium', minInvestment: 1000 },
];

export default function FractionalSukuk({ balance, onBuy }: { balance: number, onBuy: (amount: number) => void }) {
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState(SUKUKS[0]);
  const [amount, setAmount] = useState<number | ''>('');

  const handleBuy = () => {
    if (typeof amount === 'number' && amount >= selected.minInvestment && balance >= amount) {
      onBuy(amount);
      setAmount('');
      alert('Sukuk Purchased Successfully!');
    } else {
      alert('Invalid amount or insufficient balance.');
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl sm:rounded-3xl p-4 sm:p-6 shadow-sm border border-gray-100 mb-4 sm:mb-6">
      <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4 sm:mb-6">
        <div>
          <h2 className="text-lg sm:text-xl font-bold text-gray-800 tracking-tight">📜 Fractional Sukuk</h2>
          <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-gray-500 mt-0.5">Government & Corporate Islamic Bonds</p>
        </div>
        <span className="px-2.5 py-1 bg-blue-100 text-blue-700 text-[10px] sm:text-xs font-bold rounded-full flex-shrink-0">Stable Yield</span>
      </div>

      <div className="flex space-x-2.5 sm:space-x-3 overflow-x-auto pb-3 sm:pb-4 mb-3 sm:mb-4 scrollbar-none">
        {SUKUKS.map(s => (
          <button 
            key={s.id}
            onClick={() => setSelected(s)}
            className={`flex-shrink-0 p-3.5 sm:p-4 rounded-xl sm:rounded-2xl border-2 text-left min-w-[170px] sm:min-w-[200px] transition-all ${selected.id === s.id ? 'border-emerald-600 bg-emerald-50 shadow-sm' : 'border-gray-100 hover:border-emerald-200'}`}
          >
            <div className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold text-gray-800 truncate">{s.name}</div>
            <div className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-black text-emerald-700 mt-1 sm:mt-2">{s.yield}</div>
            <div className="text-[10px] sm:text-xs text-gray-500 mt-1">{s.maturity} • {s.risk} Risk</div>
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="bg-gray-50 p-3.5 sm:p-5 rounded-xl sm:rounded-2xl">
        <div className="flex justify-between text-xs sm:text-sm mb-3 sm:mb-4">
          <span className="text-gray-600">Minimum Investment</span>
          <span className="font-bold">PKR {selected.minInvestment}</span>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center space-x-2 sm:space-x-4">
          <div className="relative flex-1">
            <span className="absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-500 font-bold text-xs sm:text-sm">PKR</span>
            <input 
              type="number" 
              value={amount}
              onChange={(e) => setAmount(Number(e.target.value))}
              placeholder={`Min ${selected.minInvestment}`}
              className="w-full pl-11 pr-3 py-2.5 sm:pl-14 sm:pr-4 sm:py-3 bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-500 font-bold text-xs sm:text-sm outline-none"
            />
          </div>
          <button 
            onClick={handleBuy}
            className="bg-emerald-700 text-white px-6 py-2.5 sm:px-8 sm:py-3 rounded-xl font-bold hover:bg-emerald-800 shadow-md text-xs sm:text-sm flex-shrink-0"
          >
            Invest
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ================================================================ */
/* FILE: src\components\HalalStockScanner.tsx */
/* ================================================================ */

'use client';
import { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react';

interface Stock {
  symbol: string;
  name: string;
  sector: string;
  price: number;
  change: number;
  changePercent: number;
  shariahStatus: string;
  isLive: boolean;
}

export default function HalalStockScanner({ balance, onBuy }: { balance: number; onBuy: (symbol: string, amount: number) => void }) {
  const [stocks, setStocks] = useState<Stock[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');
  const [selectedStock, setSelectedStock] = useState<Stock | null>(null);
  const [buyAmount, setBuyAmount] = useState('');
  const [buying, setBuying] = useState(false);
  const [isLive, setIsLive] = useState(false);
  const [lastUpdated, setLastUpdated] = useState('');

  const fetchStocks = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/psx');
      const data = await res.json();
      if (data.stocks) {
        setStocks(data.stocks);
        setIsLive(data.stocks.some((s: Stock) => s.isLive));
        setLastUpdated(new Date(data.lastUpdated).toLocaleTimeString());
      }
    } catch {
      setError('Could not load market data. Showing cached prices.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchStocks();
    const interval = setInterval(fetchStocks, 60000); // refresh every 60s
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, [fetchStocks]);

  const filtered = stocks.filter(s =>
    s.name.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) ||
    s.symbol.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) ||
    s.sector.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase())
  );

  const handleBuy = async () => {
    if (!selectedStock || !buyAmount) return;
    const amount = parseFloat(buyAmount);
    if (amount > balance) { return; }
    setBuying(true);
    await onBuy(selectedStock.symbol, amount);
    setBuying(false);
    setSelectedStock(null);
    setBuyAmount('');
  };

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="space-y-2.5 sm:space-y-3">
        {[1,2,3,4,5].map(i => (
          <div key={i} className="bg-white rounded-2xl sm:rounded-3xl p-3 sm:p-4 animate-pulse">
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
              <div className="space-y-2">
                <div className="h-3.5 bg-slate-100 rounded w-24" />
                <div className="h-2.5 bg-slate-100 rounded w-32" />
              </div>
              <div className="h-5 bg-slate-100 rounded w-20" />
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-3 sm:space-y-4">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 sm:gap-2">
          <div className={`w-2 h-2 rounded-full ${isLive ? 'bg-emerald-500 animate-pulse' : 'bg-amber-400'}`} />
          <span className="text-[10px] sm:text-xs font-bold text-slate-400">{isLive ? `Live · ${lastUpdated}` : `Simulated · ${lastUpdated}`}</span>
        </div>
        <button onClick={fetchStocks} className="text-[10px] sm:text-xs text-emerald-600 font-bold hover:underline">↻ Refresh</button>
      </div>

      {/* Search */}
      <div className="relative">
        <span className="absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400 text-xs sm:text-sm">🔍</span>
        <input
          type="text"
          placeholder="Search stocks..."
          value={search}
          onChange={e => setSearch(e.target.value)}
          className="w-full pl-9 pr-4 py-2.5 sm:py-3 rounded-xl sm:rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 focus:border-emerald-400 outline-none text-xs sm:text-sm font-medium shadow-inner"
        />
      </div>

      {error && <p className="text-amber-600 text-[11px] sm:text-xs font-medium bg-amber-50 px-3 py-2 rounded-xl">{error}</p>}

      {/* Stock List — Contained in a beautiful 340px scrollable box */}
      <div className="space-y-2 max-h-[340px] overflow-y-auto pr-1.5 scrollbar-thin scrollbar-thumb-slate-200">
        {filtered.map(stock => (
          <div key={stock.symbol}>
            <button
              onClick={() => setSelectedStock(selectedStock?.symbol === stock.symbol ? null : stock)}
              className={`w-full flex justify-between items-center p-3 sm:p-4 rounded-2xl sm:rounded-3xl border transition-all duration-200 active:scale-95 ${
                selectedStock?.symbol === stock.symbol
                  ? 'border-emerald-300 bg-emerald-50 shadow-sm shadow-emerald-100'
                  : 'border-slate-100 bg-white hover:border-emerald-200 hover:shadow-sm'
              }`}
            >
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 sm:gap-3 text-left">
                <div className="w-9 h-9 sm:w-10 sm:h-10 rounded-xl sm:rounded-2xl bg-slate-100 flex items-center justify-center font-black text-xs text-slate-700 flex-shrink-0">
                  {stock.symbol.slice(0,3)}
                </div>
                <div>
                  <div className="font-black text-slate-800 text-xs sm:text-sm tracking-tight">{stock.symbol}</div>
                  <div className="text-[10px] sm:text-xs text-slate-400">{stock.name} · {stock.sector}</div>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="text-right flex-shrink-0">
                <div className="font-black text-slate-800 text-xs sm:text-sm">₨{stock.price.toLocaleString()}</div>
                <div className={`text-[10px] sm:text-xs font-bold ${stock.changePercent >= 0 ? 'text-emerald-600' : 'text-red-500'}`}>
                  {stock.changePercent >= 0 ? '▲' : '▼'} {Math.abs(stock.changePercent).toFixed(2)}%
                </div>
              </div>
            </button>

            {/* Inline Buy Panel */}
            {selectedStock?.symbol === stock.symbol && (
              <div className="mt-1 mx-1 bg-white border border-emerald-200 rounded-2xl sm:rounded-3xl p-4 sm:p-5 space-y-3 sm:space-y-4 animate-in slide-in-from-top-2 duration-200 shadow-md">
                <div className="flex justify-between text-xs sm:text-sm">
                  <span className="text-slate-500">Your Balance</span>
                  <span className="font-black text-emerald-600">₨{balance.toLocaleString()}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex justify-between text-xs sm:text-sm">
                  <span className="text-slate-500">Price/Share</span>
                  <span className="font-black">₨{stock.price.toLocaleString()}</span>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <div className="relative">
                    <span className="absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400 font-black text-xs sm:text-sm">₨</span>
                    <input
                      type="number"
                      placeholder="Investment amount"
                      value={buyAmount}
                      onChange={e => setBuyAmount(e.target.value)}
                      className="w-full pl-7 pr-4 py-2.5 sm:py-3 rounded-xl sm:rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 focus:border-emerald-400 outline-none font-bold text-slate-800 text-xs sm:text-sm"
                    />
                  </div>
                  {buyAmount && parseFloat(buyAmount) > 0 && (
                    <p className="text-[10px] sm:text-xs text-slate-400 mt-1 pl-1 font-medium">
                      ≈ {(parseFloat(buyAmount) / stock.price).toFixed(2)} shares
                    </p>
                  )}
                </div>
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 sm:gap-3">
                  <button onClick={() => { setSelectedStock(null); setBuyAmount(''); }} className="py-2 sm:py-2.5 rounded-xl sm:rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 font-black text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-50 active:scale-95 transition-all text-xs sm:text-sm">Cancel</button>
                  <button
                    onClick={handleBuy}
                    disabled={buying || !buyAmount || parseFloat(buyAmount) > balance}
                    className="py-2 sm:py-2.5 rounded-xl sm:rounded-2xl bg-emerald-600 text-white font-black hover:bg-emerald-700 disabled:opacity-50 active:scale-95 transition-all text-xs sm:text-sm shadow-md"
                  >
                    {buying ? 'Buying...' : 'Buy ✓'}
                  </button>
                </div>
                <p className="text-center text-[10px] sm:text-xs text-emerald-600 font-bold tracking-widest uppercase">✓ AAOIFI Shariah Certified</p>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}



/* ================================================================ */
/* FILE: src\components\LanguageSelect.tsx */
/* ================================================================ */

'use client';

interface LanguageSelectProps {
  onSelect: (lang: 'en' | 'ur') => void;
}

export default function LanguageSelect({ onSelect }: LanguageSelectProps) {
  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[9999] bg-slate-950 flex flex-col items-center justify-center px-8 overflow-hidden">
      {/* Ambient Orbs */}
      <div className="absolute top-0 right-0 w-96 h-96 bg-emerald-500/10 rounded-full blur-[120px] -translate-y-1/2 translate-x-1/2 pointer-events-none" />
      <div className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 w-80 h-80 bg-teal-500/10 rounded-full blur-[100px] translate-y-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 pointer-events-none" />

            dir="rtl"

/* ================================================================ */
/* FILE: src\components\MarketAlerts.tsx */
/* ================================================================ */

'use client';
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { MOCK_PSX_STOCKS } from '@/lib/halal-stock-data';

interface Alert {
  id: number;
  symbol: string;
  name: string;
  targetPrice: number;
  direction: 'above' | 'below';
  currentPrice: number;
  triggered: boolean;
  createdAt: string;
}

export default function MarketAlerts() {
  const [alerts, setAlerts] = useState<Alert[]>([]);
  const [showAdd, setShowAdd] = useState(false);
  const [selectedSymbol, setSelectedSymbol] = useState('MEBL');
  const [targetPrice, setTargetPrice] = useState('');
  const [direction, setDirection] = useState<'above' | 'below'>('above');
  const [saved, setSaved] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const stored = localStorage.getItem('roziq_alerts');
    if (stored) setAlerts(JSON.parse(stored));
  }, []);

  const saveAlerts = (updated: Alert[]) => {
    setAlerts(updated);
    localStorage.setItem('roziq_alerts', JSON.stringify(updated));
  };

  const addAlert = () => {
    if (!targetPrice || isNaN(parseFloat(targetPrice))) return;
    const stock = MOCK_PSX_STOCKS.find(s => s.symbol === selectedSymbol);
    if (!stock) return;
    const newAlert: Alert = {
      id: Date.now(),
      symbol: selectedSymbol,
      name: stock.name,
      targetPrice: parseFloat(targetPrice),
      direction,
      currentPrice: stock.price,
      triggered: Math.abs(stock.price - parseFloat(targetPrice)) < 1,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    };
    saveAlerts([newAlert, ...alerts]);
    setTargetPrice('');
    setShowAdd(false);
    setSaved(true);
    setTimeout(() => setSaved(false), 2000);

    // Browser notification
    if ('Notification' in window && Notification.permission === 'granted') {
      new Notification(`🕌 Roziq Alert Set`, {
        body: `Alert: ${stock.name} ${direction} ₨${parseFloat(targetPrice).toLocaleString()}`,
        icon: '/icon.png',
      });
    } else if ('Notification' in window && Notification.permission !== 'denied') {
      Notification.requestPermission().then(perm => {
        if (perm === 'granted') {
          new Notification(`🕌 Roziq Alert Set`, {
            body: `${stock.name} ${direction} ₨${parseFloat(targetPrice).toLocaleString()}`,
            icon: '/icon.png',
          });
        }
      });
    }
  };

  const removeAlert = (id: number) => saveAlerts(alerts.filter(a => a.id !== id));

  const halalStocks = MOCK_PSX_STOCKS.filter(s => s.halalScore >= 6);

  return (
    <div className="premium-card p-6">
      <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-6">
        <div>
          <h3 className="text-xs font-black text-emerald-600 uppercase tracking-widest mb-1">Price Alerts</h3>
          <p className="text-xl font-black text-slate-800 tracking-tight">Market Alerts</p>
        </div>
        <button
          onClick={() => setShowAdd(v => !v)}
          className="w-10 h-10 bg-emerald-600 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center text-white text-xl hover:bg-emerald-700 active:scale-95 transition-all shadow-lg shadow-emerald-200"
        >
          {showAdd ? '✕' : '+'}
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Add Alert Form */}
      {showAdd && (
        <div className="bg-slate-50 rounded-3xl p-5 mb-6 space-y-4 animate-in slide-in-from-top-2 duration-300">
          <div>
            <label className="text-[10px] font-black text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-widest block mb-2">Stock</label>
            <select
              value={selectedSymbol}
              onChange={e => setSelectedSymbol(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl font-bold text-slate-800 focus:outline-none focus:border-emerald-400 text-sm"
            >
              {halalStocks.map(s => (
                <option key={s.symbol} value={s.symbol}>{s.symbol} — {s.name} (₨{s.price})</option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            <div>
              <label className="text-[10px] font-black text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-widest block mb-2">Direction</label>
              <div className="flex gap-2">
                {(['above', 'below'] as const).map(d => (
                  <button
                    key={d}
                    onClick={() => setDirection(d)}
                    className={`flex-1 py-2.5 rounded-2xl font-black text-xs transition-all ${
                      direction === d ? 'bg-emerald-600 text-white' : 'bg-white text-slate-600 border border-slate-200'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {d === 'above' ? '▲ Above' : '▼ Below'}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="text-[10px] font-black text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-widest block mb-2">Target (₨)</label>
              <input
                type="number"
                value={targetPrice}
                onChange={e => setTargetPrice(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="e.g. 150"
                className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl font-bold text-slate-800 focus:outline-none focus:border-emerald-400 text-sm"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          <button
            onClick={addAlert}
            disabled={!targetPrice}
            className="w-full py-3.5 bg-emerald-600 text-white font-black rounded-2xl hover:bg-emerald-700 active:scale-95 transition-all disabled:opacity-40"
          >
            🔔 Set Alert
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      {saved && (
        <div className="mb-4 p-3 bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-100 rounded-2xl text-emerald-700 text-xs font-black text-center animate-in fade-in">
          ✅ Alert set! You'll be notified when the price is reached.
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Alert List */}
      {alerts.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="text-center py-10">
          <p className="text-4xl mb-3">🔔</p>
          <p className="text-slate-400 text-sm font-medium">No alerts yet.</p>
          <p className="text-slate-300 text-xs mt-1">Tap + to set a price alert for any halal stock.</p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-3">
          {alerts.map(alert => {
            const liveStock = MOCK_PSX_STOCKS.find(s => s.symbol === alert.symbol);
            const current = liveStock?.price ?? alert.currentPrice;
            const isTriggered = alert.direction === 'above' ? current >= alert.targetPrice : current <= alert.targetPrice;
            const diff = ((current - alert.targetPrice) / alert.targetPrice * 100).toFixed(1);

            return (
              <div key={alert.id} className={`flex items-center gap-4 p-4 rounded-3xl border transition-all ${
                isTriggered ? 'bg-emerald-50 border-emerald-200' : 'bg-white border-slate-100'
              }`}>
                <div className={`w-11 h-11 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center text-lg flex-shrink-0 ${
                  isTriggered ? 'bg-emerald-100' : 'bg-slate-50'
                }`}>
                  {isTriggered ? '🔔' : '⏳'}
                </div>
                <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <span className="font-black text-slate-800 text-sm">{alert.symbol}</span>
                    <span className={`text-[9px] font-black px-2 py-0.5 rounded-lg ${
                      alert.direction === 'above' ? 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700' : 'bg-red-100 text-red-700'
                    }`}>
                      {alert.direction === 'above' ? '▲' : '▼'} ₨{alert.targetPrice.toLocaleString()}
                    </span>
                    {isTriggered && <span className="text-[9px] font-black px-2 py-0.5 bg-emerald-600 text-white rounded-lg">TRIGGERED</span>}
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
                    Current: ₨{current.toLocaleString()} &nbsp;·&nbsp;
                    <span className={parseFloat(diff) >= 0 ? 'text-emerald-500' : 'text-red-400'}>
                      {parseFloat(diff) >= 0 ? '+' : ''}{diff}% from target
                    </span>
                  </p>
                </div>
                <button
                  onClick={() => removeAlert(alert.id)}
                  className="text-slate-300 hover:text-red-400 transition-colors text-lg flex-shrink-0"
                >
                  ✕
                </button>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

/* ================================================================ */
/* FILE: src\components\MuftiAI.tsx */
/* ================================================================ */

'use client';
import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';

interface MuftiAIProps {
  lang: 'en' | 'ur';
}

export default function MuftiAI({ lang }: MuftiAIProps) {
  const [isListening, setIsListening] = useState(false);
  const [isSpeaking, setIsSpeaking] = useState(false);
  const [inputText, setInputText] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  
  const recognitionRef = useRef<any>(null);
  const t = (en: string, ur: string) => (lang === 'ur' ? ur : en);

  useEffect(() => {
    const SpeechRecognition = (window as any).SpeechRecognition || (window as any).webkitSpeechRecognition;
    if (SpeechRecognition) {
      recognitionRef.current = new SpeechRecognition();
      recognitionRef.current.continuous = false;
      recognitionRef.current.interimResults = false;
      recognitionRef.current.lang = lang === 'ur' ? 'ur-PK' : 'en-US';

      recognitionRef.current.onresult = (event: any) => {
        const text = event.results[0][0]?.transcript;
        if (text) askMufti(text);
      };

      recognitionRef.current.onend = () => setIsListening(false);
      recognitionRef.current.onerror = () => setIsListening(false);
    }
  }, [lang]);

  const [messages, setMessages] = useState<{ role: 'user' | 'ai', text: string }[]>([
    { role: 'ai', text: lang === 'ur' ? 'السلام علیکم! میں مفتی اے آئی ہوں۔ آپ مجھ سے زکوٰۃ، حلال اسٹاکس، یا سود (ربا) کے بارے میں شرعی رہنمائی لے سکتے ہیں۔' : 'Assalam-o-Alaikum! I am Mufti AI. You can ask me for Shariah guidance regarding Zakat, Halal stocks, or Riba (interest).' }
  ]);

  const toggleListening = () => {
    if (isListening) {
      recognitionRef.current?.stop();
    } else {
      if ('vibrate' in navigator) navigator.vibrate(50);
      setIsListening(true);
      try {
        recognitionRef.current?.start();
      } catch {
        setIsListening(false);
        alert(lang === 'ur' ? 'مائیکروفون کی اجازت نہیں ہے، براہ کرم لکھ کر سوال پوچھیں۔' : 'Microphone permission denied. Please type your question.');
      }
    }
  };

  const speak = (text: string) => {
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && 'speechSynthesis' in window) {
      window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
      const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(text);
      utterance.lang = lang === 'ur' ? 'ur-PK' : 'en-US';
      utterance.rate = 0.9;
      utterance.onstart = () => setIsSpeaking(true);
      utterance.onend = () => setIsSpeaking(false);
      window.speechSynthesis.speak(utterance);
    }
  };

  const handleSendText = () => {
    if (!inputText.trim()) return;
    const query = inputText;
    setInputText('');
    askMufti(query);
  };

  const askMufti = async (message: string) => {
    setLoading(true);
    setMessages(prev => [...prev, { role: 'user', text: message }]);
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/agent/mufti', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ message, lang })
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      setMessages(prev => [...prev, { role: 'ai', text: data.text }]);
      speak(data.audioText || data.text);
    } catch (err) {
      const fallbackMsg = lang === 'ur' 
        ? "AAOIFI شریعہ معیارات کے مطابق، آپ کی سرمایہ کاری اس وقت تک جائز ہے جب تک کمپنی کا قرض 33 فیصد سے کم اور غیر شرعی آمدنی 5 فیصد سے کم ہو۔ منافع کی باقاعدہ تطہیر یقینی بنائیں۔" 
        : "Based on AAOIFI Shariah standards, your investment is permitted as long as the company's debt-to-equity ratio is under 33% and illicit income is under 5%. Ensure regular dividend purification.";
      setMessages(prev => [...prev, { role: 'ai', text: fallbackMsg }]);
      speak(fallbackMsg);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="p-5 sm:p-6 rounded-[2.5rem] bg-slate-950 border border-amber-500/20 shadow-2xl relative overflow-hidden transition-all duration-500 min-h-[380px] max-h-[500px] flex flex-col" dir={lang === 'ur' ? 'rtl' : 'ltr'}>
      {/* Spiritual Aura */}
      <div className="absolute top-0 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 w-48 h-24 bg-amber-500/10 rounded-full blur-[60px]" />
      
      <div className="relative z-10 flex flex-col flex-1 h-full">
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-4 border-b border-white/10 pb-4">
          <div className={`w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center text-2xl shadow-lg transition-all duration-500 ${isListening ? 'bg-amber-500 rotate-12 scale-110' : isSpeaking ? 'bg-amber-600 animate-bounce' : 'bg-slate-900 border border-white/5'}`}>
            👳‍♂️
          </div>
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-lg font-black text-amber-500 tracking-tight uppercase flex items-center gap-1.5">
              MUFTI <span className="text-white">AI</span>
              <span className="text-[9px] px-2 py-0.5 bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-300 rounded-full border border-amber-500/30">AAOIFI</span>
            </h2>
            <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400">{t('Certified Shariah Advisor', 'تصدیق شدہ شرعی مشیر')}</p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Interaction Area - Chat Thread */}
        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto space-y-3 mb-4 pr-1 max-h-[220px] scrollbar-hide flex flex-col">
          {messages.map((m, i) => (
            <div key={i} className={`flex ${m.role === 'user' ? 'justify-end' : 'justify-start'} animate-in fade-in duration-300`}>
              <div className={`max-w-[85%] p-3.5 rounded-2xl text-xs font-bold leading-relaxed tracking-tight shadow-sm ${
                m.role === 'user' 
                  ? 'bg-amber-500 text-white rounded-tr-none' 
                  : 'bg-slate-900 text-slate-200 border border-white/10 rounded-tl-none'
              }`}>
                {m.text}
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
          {loading && (
            <div className="flex justify-start">
              <div className="bg-slate-900 p-3.5 rounded-2xl rounded-tl-none border border-white/10 flex gap-1.5">
                {[1,2,3].map(i => <div key={i} className="w-1.5 h-1.5 bg-amber-500 rounded-full animate-bounce" style={{ animationDelay: `${i*0.2}s` }} />)}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Direct Text Input & Controls */}
        <div className="mt-auto pt-2 border-t border-white/10 flex items-center gap-2">
          <button 
            onClick={toggleListening}
            title={t('Voice Input', 'وائس ان پٹ')}
            className={`w-11 h-11 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center text-lg transition-all active:scale-90 flex-shrink-0 ${
              isListening ? 'bg-red-500 text-white animate-pulse shadow-lg shadow-red-500/40' : 'bg-slate-900 text-amber-500 border border-white/10 hover:bg-slate-800'
            }`}
          >
            {isListening ? '⏹️' : '🎤'}
          </button>
          
          <input
            type="text"
            value={inputText}
            onChange={e => setInputText(e.target.value)}
            onKeyPress={e => e.key === 'Enter' && handleSendText()}
            placeholder={t('Ask about Zakat, Riba, Stocks...', 'زکوٰۃ، حلال اسٹاکس، یا سود کے بارے میں پوچھیں...')}
            className="flex-1 bg-slate-900 border border-white/10 rounded-2xl px-3.5 py-3 text-xs text-white placeholder-slate-500 outline-none focus:border-amber-500 transition-colors"
          />

          <button 
            onClick={handleSendText}
            disabled={loading || !inputText.trim()}
            className="w-11 h-11 rounded-2xl bg-amber-500 text-slate-950 flex items-center justify-center text-base font-black hover:bg-amber-400 active:scale-95 transition-all disabled:opacity-50 flex-shrink-0"
          >
            ↑
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Quick Suggestion Chips */}
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 mt-3 pt-2 border-t border-white/5 justify-center">
          {(lang === 'ur' 
            ? ['کیا میزان بینک حلال ہے؟', 'زکوٰۃ کا نصاب کیا ہے؟', 'سکوک کیا ہیں؟', 'تطہیر کیسے کریں؟']
            : ['Is Meezan Bank halal?', 'What is Zakat Nisab?', 'What are Sukuks?', 'How to purify dividends?']
          ).map(q => (
            <button 
              key={q} 
              onClick={() => { setInputText(q); }}
              className="px-2.5 py-1 bg-slate-900 hover:bg-amber-500 hover:text-slate-950 text-slate-400 rounded-xl text-[10px] font-bold border border-white/5 transition-all"
            >
              {q}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* FILE: src\components\Onboarding.tsx */
/* ================================================================ */

'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';

const steps = [
  {
    icon: '🕌',
    color: 'from-emerald-500 to-teal-600',
    titleEn: 'Welcome to Roziq',
    titleUr: 'روزق میں خوش آمدید',
    descEn: 'Pakistan\'s first Sovereign Halal Wealth OS. Built for the modern Muslim investor — Shariah-compliant, intelligent, and yours.',
    descUr: 'پاکستان کا پہلا خودمختار حلال ویلتھ OS۔ جدید مسلمان سرمایہ کار کے لیے بنایا گیا — شریعت کے مطابق، ذہین اور آپ کا۔',
    badge: 'Powered by Shariah',
  },
  {
    icon: '📈',
    color: 'from-blue-500 to-indigo-600',
    titleEn: 'Invest with Confidence',
    titleUr: 'اعتماد سے سرمایہ کاری کریں',
    descEn: 'Every stock, sukuk, and fund is screened by AAOIFI Shariah standards. No riba. No haram. Pure halal returns.',
    descUr: 'ہر اسٹاک، سکوک اور فنڈ AAOIFI شریعت معیارات کے مطابق جانچا جاتا ہے۔ کوئی سود نہیں۔ کوئی حرام نہیں۔ خالص حلال منافع۔',
    badge: 'AAOIFI Certified',
  },
  {
    icon: '🤲',
    color: 'from-amber-500 to-orange-600',
    titleEn: 'Purify & Give Back',
    titleUr: 'پاک کریں اور واپس دیں',
    descEn: 'Automatically calculate your Zakat Al-Mal. Distribute to verified charities with one tap. Grow wealth. Purify the Ummah.',
    descUr: 'خود بخود اپنی زکوٰۃ المال کا حساب لگائیں۔ ایک ٹچ سے تصدیق شدہ خیراتی اداروں کو دیں۔ دولت بڑھائیں۔ امت کو پاک کریں۔',
    badge: 'Zakat Engine',
  },
];

interface OnboardingProps {
  onComplete: () => void;
  lang: 'en' | 'ur';
}

export default function Onboarding({ onComplete, lang }: OnboardingProps) {
  const [step, setStep] = useState(0);
  const [exiting, setExiting] = useState(false);
  const current = steps[step];
  const isLast = step === steps.length - 1;
  const isRtl = lang === 'ur';

  const next = () => {
    if (isLast) {
      setExiting(true);
      setTimeout(() => {
        localStorage.setItem('roziq_onboarded', 'true');
        onComplete();
      }, 400);
    } else {
      setStep(s => s + 1);
    }
  };

  const skip = () => {
    localStorage.setItem('roziq_onboarded', 'true');
    onComplete();
  };

  return (
    <div
      className={`fixed inset-0 z-[9998] bg-slate-950 flex flex-col transition-opacity duration-400 ${exiting ? 'opacity-0' : 'opacity-100'}`}
      dir={isRtl ? 'rtl' : 'ltr'}
    >
      {/* Ambient background */}
      <div className="absolute inset-0 overflow-hidden pointer-events-none">
        <div className={`absolute top-0 right-0 w-[500px] h-[500px] bg-gradient-to-br ${current.color} opacity-10 rounded-full blur-[150px] -translate-y-1/3 translate-x-1/3`} />
        <div className={`absolute bottom-0 left-0 w-[400px] h-[400px] bg-gradient-to-tr ${current.color} opacity-5 rounded-full blur-[120px] translate-y-1/3 -translate-x-1/3`} />
      </div>

      {/* Skip button */}
      <div className="relative z-10 flex justify-end p-6">
        <button onClick={skip} className="text-white/30 text-sm font-bold hover:text-white/60 transition-colors px-4 py-2">
          {isRtl ? 'چھوڑیں' : 'Skip'}
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Content */}
      <div className="relative z-10 flex-1 flex flex-col items-center justify-center px-8 pb-8">
        {/* Icon */}
        <div className={`w-28 h-28 bg-gradient-to-br ${current.color} rounded-[2.5rem] flex items-center justify-center text-6xl mb-8 shadow-2xl transition-all duration-500`}>
          {current.icon}
        </div>

        {/* Badge */}
        <div className="mb-6 px-4 py-1.5 bg-white/5 border border-white/10 rounded-full">
          <span className="text-white/50 text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-[0.25em]">{current.badge}</span>
        </div>

        {/* Title */}
        <h2 className="text-3xl font-black text-white text-center tracking-tight mb-4 leading-tight">
          {isRtl ? current.titleUr : current.titleEn}
        </h2>

        {/* Description */}
        <p className="text-white/50 text-center text-sm font-medium leading-relaxed max-w-xs">
          {isRtl ? current.descUr : current.descEn}
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Bottom controls */}
      <div className="relative z-10 px-8 pb-12">
        {/* Progress dots */}
        <div className="flex justify-center gap-2 mb-8">
          {steps.map((_, i) => (
            <button
              key={i}
              onClick={() => setStep(i)}
              className={`rounded-full transition-all duration-300 ${
                i === step ? 'w-6 h-2 bg-emerald-500' : 'w-2 h-2 bg-white/20'
              }`}
            />
          ))}
        </div>

        {/* Next button */}
        <button
          onClick={next}
          className={`w-full py-5 rounded-3xl font-black text-base tracking-wide transition-all duration-300 active:scale-95 bg-gradient-to-r ${current.color} text-white shadow-2xl hover:shadow-emerald-900/40 hover:scale-[1.02]`}
        >
          {isLast
            ? (isRtl ? 'شروع کریں 🚀' : 'Get Started 🚀')
            : (isRtl ? 'آگے →' : 'Next →')}
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
