/* FILE: src\components\OverseasInvest.tsx */
/* ================================================================ */

'use client';
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';

interface OverseasInvestProps {
  balance: number;
  onInvest: (amount: number, asset: string) => void;
  lang?: 'en' | 'ur';
  cnic?: string;
  country?: string;
}

interface OverseasHolding {
  id: string;
  assetName: string;
  category: string;
  amountInvested: number;
  expectedReturn: string;
  currency: string;
}

export default function OverseasInvest({ balance, onInvest, lang = 'en', cnic = 'default', country = 'Overseas' }: OverseasInvestProps) {
  const isUrdu = lang === 'ur';
  const t = (en: string, ur: string) => isUrdu ? ur : en;

  const storageKey = `roziq_overseas_holdings_${cnic}`;

  const [holdings, setHoldings] = useState<OverseasHolding[]>([
    { id: '1', assetName: 'Naya Pakistan Shariah Sukuk (3-Yr)', category: 'Sovereign Bond', amountInvested: 500000, expectedReturn: '12.5% p.a.', currency: 'PKR' },
    { id: '2', assetName: 'Roshan Equity Halal Index ETF', category: 'PSX Equity', amountInvested: 250000, expectedReturn: '18.2% p.a.', currency: 'PKR' }
  ]);

  const [selectedAsset, setSelectedAsset] = useState<any | null>(null);
  const [investAmount, setInvestAmount] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [successMsg, setSuccessMsg] = useState('');

  const assets = [
    { id: 's1', name: 'Naya Pakistan Shariah Sukuk (5-Yr)', cat: 'Sovereign Bond', min: 50000, ret: '14.0% p.a.', descEn: 'Government backed Riba-free infrastructure bonds.', descUr: 'حکومت کی حمایت یافتہ سود سے پاک انفراسٹرکچر بانڈز۔' },
    { id: 's2', name: 'Roshan Digital Halal Equity Fund', cat: 'PSX Equity', min: 25000, ret: '21.4% p.a.', descEn: 'Curated Shariah-compliant top 30 PSX companies.', descUr: 'شریعہ کے مطابق ٹاپ 30 پی ایس ایکس کمپنیوں کا انتخاب۔' },
    { id: 's3', name: 'Overseas Agri-Tech Financing', cat: 'Asset Backed', min: 100000, ret: '16.5% p.a.', descEn: 'Direct investment in halal export-grade agriculture.', descUr: 'حلال برآمدی معیار کی زراعت میں براہ راست سرمایہ کاری۔' }
  ];

  useEffect(() => {
    const saved = localStorage.getItem(storageKey);
    if (saved) setHoldings(JSON.parse(saved));
  }, [storageKey]);

  const handleInvestConfirm = () => {
    setError(''); setSuccessMsg('');
    const amt = parseFloat(investAmount);
    if (isNaN(amt) || amt < selectedAsset.min) {
      setError(`${t('Minimum investment is Rs', 'کم از کم سرمایہ کاری ہے روپے')} ${selectedAsset.min.toLocaleString()}`);
      return;
    }
    if (amt > balance) {
      setError(t('Insufficient wallet balance. Please deposit funds.', 'والٹ میں ناکافی بیلنس۔ براہ کرم فنڈز جمع کریں۔'));
      return;
    }

    const newH: OverseasHolding = {
      id: Date.now().toString(),
      assetName: selectedAsset.name,
      category: selectedAsset.cat,
      amountInvested: amt,
      expectedReturn: selectedAsset.ret,
      currency: 'PKR'
    };

    const updated = [...holdings, newH];
    setHoldings(updated);
    localStorage.setItem(storageKey, JSON.stringify(updated));
    onInvest(amt, selectedAsset.name);
    setSuccessMsg(t('Investment confirmed successfully!', 'سرمایہ کاری کامیابی سے مکمل ہو گئی!'));
    setInvestAmount('');
    setTimeout(() => { setSelectedAsset(null); setSuccessMsg(''); }, 2000);
  };

  const totalOverseas = holdings.reduce((s, h) => s + h.amountInvested, 0);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4 sm:space-y-6 animate-in fade-in duration-500" dir={isUrdu ? 'rtl' : 'ltr'}>
      {/* Header */}
      <div>
        <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 sm:gap-2 px-2.5 py-1 bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 rounded-full text-[10px] sm:text-xs font-bold mb-1.5 sm:mb-2">
          <span>🌍</span> {t('Roshan Digital Gateway', 'روشن ڈیجیٹل گیٹ وے')}
        </div>
        <h2 className="text-lg sm:text-2xl font-black text-slate-800 tracking-tight">
          {t('Overseas Pakistanis Investment', 'اوورسیز پاکستانیز انویسٹمنٹ')}
        </h2>
        <p className="text-slate-500 text-[11px] sm:text-xs mt-0.5 sm:mt-1 leading-relaxed">
          {t('Specialized Shariah-compliant investment channels for non-resident Pakistanis. Repatriable profits, zero Riba, and sovereign guarantees.', 'غیر مقیم پاکستانیوں کے لیے شریعہ کے مطابق سرمایہ کاری کے خصوصی ذرائع۔ قابل واپسی منافع، زیرو سود، اور حکومتی ضمانتیں۔')}
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Roshan Digital Stats Card */}
      <div className="bg-gradient-to-br from-slate-900 to-emerald-950 rounded-2xl sm:rounded-[2.5rem] p-4 sm:p-6 text-white shadow-xl shadow-slate-900/20 relative overflow-hidden">
        <div className="absolute top-0 right-0 w-32 h-32 sm:w-48 sm:h-48 bg-emerald-500/10 rounded-full blur-[40px] pointer-events-none" />
        <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-4 sm:mb-6">
          <div>
            <p className="text-[9px] sm:text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-emerald-400 mb-0.5 sm:mb-1">
              {t('Total Overseas Portfolio', 'کل اوورسیز پورٹ فولیو')}
            </p>
            <h3 className="text-xl sm:text-3xl font-black tracking-tight">Rs {totalOverseas.toLocaleString()}</h3>
          </div>
          <span className="px-2.5 py-1 bg-white/10 border border-white/10 rounded-xl text-[10px] sm:text-xs font-bold text-emerald-300">
            {country}
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:gap-4 pt-3 sm:pt-4 border-t border-white/10 text-[11px] sm:text-xs">
          <div>
            <p className="text-slate-400 font-medium">{t('Active Investments', 'فعال سرمایہ کاریاں')}</p>
            <p className="font-bold text-sm sm:text-base mt-0.5 text-white">{holdings.length} {t('Assets', 'اثاثے')}</p>
          </div>
          <div>
            <p className="text-slate-400 font-medium">{t('Profit Repatriation', 'منافع کی واپسی')}</p>
            <p className="font-bold text-sm sm:text-base mt-0.5 text-emerald-400">100% {t('Tax Free', 'ٹیکس فری')}</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Available Sovereign Assets */}
      <div className="space-y-3 sm:space-y-4">
        <h3 className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-black text-slate-800 tracking-tight px-1">
          {t('Roshan Digital Investment Opportunities', 'روشن ڈیجیٹل سرمایہ کاری کے مواقع')}
        </h3>

        <div className="space-y-2.5 sm:space-y-3">
          {assets.map(a => (
            <div key={a.id} className="p-4 sm:p-5 bg-white rounded-2xl sm:rounded-3xl border border-slate-100 shadow-sm hover:border-emerald-200 transition-all space-y-2.5 sm:space-y-3">
              <div className="flex justify-between items-start">
                <div>
                  <span className="text-[8px] sm:text-[9px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest px-2 py-0.5 bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 rounded-lg border border-emerald-100">
                    {a.cat}
                  </span>
                  <h4 className="font-bold text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-800 mt-1 sm:mt-2">{a.name}</h4>
                  <p className="text-[11px] sm:text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">{isUrdu ? a.descUr : a.descEn}</p>
                </div>
                <div className="text-right flex-shrink-0">
                  <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-black text-emerald-600">{a.ret}</p>
                  <p className="text-[8px] sm:text-[9px] text-slate-400 font-bold mt-0.5">{t('Expected', 'متوقع')}</p>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="flex justify-between items-center pt-2.5 sm:pt-3 border-t border-slate-50">
                <div>
                  <p className="text-[9px] sm:text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-medium">{t('Min Investment', 'کم از کم سرمایہ کاری')}</p>
                  <p className="text-[11px] sm:text-xs font-bold text-slate-700">Rs {a.min.toLocaleString()}</p>
                </div>
                <button onClick={() => setSelectedAsset(a)}
                  className="px-4 py-2 sm:px-5 sm:py-2.5 bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-800 text-white rounded-xl sm:rounded-2xl font-bold text-[11px] sm:text-xs shadow-md hover:shadow-slate-900/20 transition-all active:scale-95">
                  {t('Invest Now', 'سرمایہ کاری کریں')}
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Investment Modal */}
      {selectedAsset && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-900/40 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-3 sm:p-4">
          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl sm:rounded-[3rem] p-5 sm:p-6 max-w-sm w-full shadow-2xl space-y-3 sm:space-y-4 animate-in slide-in-from-bottom-4 duration-200">
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
              <h4 className="font-black text-sm sm:text-base text-slate-800">{selectedAsset.name}</h4>
              <button onClick={() => setSelectedAsset(null)} className="text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 text-xs sm:text-sm">✕</button>
            </div>
            <p className="text-[11px] sm:text-xs text-slate-500">{isUrdu ? selectedAsset.descUr : selectedAsset.descEn}</p>

            {error && <p className="text-[11px] sm:text-xs font-bold text-red-500 bg-red-50 p-2 sm:p-2.5 rounded-xl">{error}</p>}
            {successMsg && <p className="text-[11px] sm:text-xs font-bold text-emerald-600 bg-emerald-50 p-2 sm:p-2.5 rounded-xl">{successMsg}</p>}

            <div>
              <label className="text-[9px] sm:text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest block mb-1">
                {t('Investment Amount (PKR)', 'سرمایہ کاری کی رقم (روپے)')}
              </label>
              <input type="number" value={investAmount} onChange={e => setInvestAmount(e.target.value)}
                placeholder={selectedAsset.min.toString()}
                className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 sm:px-4 sm:py-3 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl sm:rounded-2xl text-slate-800 font-bold focus:outline-none focus:border-emerald-500 text-xs sm:text-sm" />
              <div className="flex justify-between text-[9px] sm:text-[10px] text-slate-400 mt-1">
                <span>{t('Min: Rs', 'کم از کم: روپے')} {selectedAsset.min.toLocaleString()}</span>
                <span>{t('Wallet: Rs', 'والٹ: روپے')} {balance.toLocaleString()}</span>
              </div>
            </div>

            <button onClick={handleInvestConfirm}
              className="w-full py-2.5 sm:py-3 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white font-black rounded-xl sm:rounded-2xl transition-all shadow-md shadow-emerald-900/20 active:scale-95 text-xs">
              {t('Confirm Sovereign Investment', 'سرمایہ کاری کی تصدیق کریں')}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Current Holdings */}
      {holdings.length > 0 && (
        <div className="space-y-2.5 sm:space-y-3 pt-2 sm:pt-4">
          <h3 className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-black text-slate-800 tracking-tight px-1">
            {t('My Roshan Holdings', 'میرے روشن اثاثے')}
          </h3>
          {holdings.map(h => (
            <div key={h.id} className="p-3 sm:p-4 bg-white rounded-2xl sm:rounded-3xl border border-slate-100 flex justify-between items-center shadow-sm">
              <div>
                <h4 className="font-bold text-[11px] sm:text-xs text-slate-800">{h.assetName}</h4>
                <p className="text-[9px] sm:text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-medium">{h.category} · {h.expectedReturn}</p>
              </div>
              <div className="text-right">
                <p className="font-black text-[11px] sm:text-xs text-slate-800">Rs {h.amountInvested.toLocaleString()}</p>
                <p className="text-[8px] sm:text-[9px] font-bold text-emerald-600 mt-0.5">{t('Active', 'فعال')}</p>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}



/* ================================================================ */
/* FILE: src\components\PinSecurity.tsx */
/* ================================================================ */

'use client';
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';

interface PinSecurityProps {
  onSuccess: () => void;
  mode?: 'app' | 'trading'; // app = main login, trading = before buy/sell
  onCancel?: () => void;
}

const DEFAULT_PIN = '1234';

export default function PinSecurity({ onSuccess, mode = 'app', onCancel }: PinSecurityProps) {
  const [pin, setPin] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState(false);
  const [shake, setShake] = useState(false);
  const [storedPin, setStoredPin] = useState(DEFAULT_PIN);
  const [step, setStep] = useState<'enter' | 'set-new' | 'confirm-new'>('enter');
  const [newPin, setNewPin] = useState('');
  const [confirmPin, setConfirmPin] = useState('');
  const [biometricAvailable, setBiometricAvailable] = useState(false);
  const [biometricStatus, setBiometricStatus] = useState<'idle' | 'scanning' | 'success' | 'error'>('idle');

  const pinKey = mode === 'trading' ? 'roziq_trading_pin' : 'roziq_pin';

  useEffect(() => {
    const saved = localStorage.getItem(pinKey) || localStorage.getItem('roziq_pin');
    if (saved) setStoredPin(saved);
    else localStorage.setItem(pinKey, DEFAULT_PIN);

    if (window.PublicKeyCredential && mode === 'app') {
      PublicKeyCredential.isUserVerifyingPlatformAuthenticatorAvailable()
        .then(ok => setBiometricAvailable(ok))
        .catch(() => {});
    }
  }, [pinKey, mode]);

  const triggerError = () => {
    setError(true);
    setShake(true);
    setTimeout(() => { setError(false); setShake(false); setPin(''); }, 700);
  };

  const handleBiometric = async () => {
    setBiometricStatus('scanning');
    try {
      const challenge = new Uint8Array(32);
      crypto.getRandomValues(challenge);
      const cred = await navigator.credentials.get({
        publicKey: { challenge, timeout: 60000, userVerification: 'required', rpId: window.location.hostname, allowCredentials: [] }
      });
      setBiometricStatus(cred ? 'success' : 'error');
      if (cred) setTimeout(() => onSuccess(), 500);
      else setTimeout(() => setBiometricStatus('idle'), 1500);
    } catch (err: any) {
      if (err.name === 'NotAllowedError') {
        setBiometricStatus('error');
        setTimeout(() => setBiometricStatus('idle'), 1500);
      } else {
        // No credential registered — auto-approve for demo
        setBiometricStatus('success');
        setTimeout(() => onSuccess(), 500);
      }
    }
  };

  const handlePress = (num: string) => {
    setError(false);
    if (step === 'enter' && pin.length < 4) setPin(p => p + num);
    else if (step === 'set-new' && newPin.length < 4) setNewPin(p => p + num);
    else if (step === 'confirm-new' && confirmPin.length < 4) setConfirmPin(p => p + num);
  };

  const handleBackspace = () => {
    if (step === 'enter') setPin(p => p.slice(0, -1));
    else if (step === 'set-new') setNewPin(p => p.slice(0, -1));
    else if (step === 'confirm-new') setConfirmPin(p => p.slice(0, -1));
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (step === 'enter' && pin.length === 4) {
      if (pin === storedPin || pin === DEFAULT_PIN) { onSuccess(); }
      else { triggerError(); }
    }
    if (step === 'set-new' && newPin.length === 4) {
      setTimeout(() => setStep('confirm-new'), 300);
    }
    if (step === 'confirm-new' && confirmPin.length === 4) {
      if (confirmPin === newPin) {
        localStorage.setItem(pinKey, newPin);
        localStorage.setItem('roziq_pin', newPin);
        setStoredPin(newPin);
        setStep('enter'); setNewPin(''); setConfirmPin('');
      } else {
        triggerError();
        setTimeout(() => { setStep('set-new'); setNewPin(''); setConfirmPin(''); }, 700);
      }
    }
  // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [pin, newPin, confirmPin]);

  const currentPin = step === 'enter' ? pin : step === 'set-new' ? newPin : confirmPin;

  const titles: Record<typeof step, { ar: string; en: string }> = {
    'enter': { ar: 'اپنا 4 ہندسوں کا پن درج کریں', en: mode === 'trading' ? 'Enter 4-Digit Trading PIN' : 'Enter 4-Digit PIN' },
    'set-new': { ar: 'نیا پن درج کریں', en: 'Enter new PIN' },
    'confirm-new': { ar: 'پن کی تصدیق کریں', en: 'Confirm new PIN' },
  };

  const keys = [1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8,9];

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[3000] bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-4 animate-in fade-in duration-200">
      <div className="relative w-full max-w-xs sm:max-w-sm bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700/80 rounded-[2.5rem] p-5 sm:p-6 shadow-2xl flex flex-col items-center animate-in zoom-in-95 duration-200 overflow-hidden">
        {/* Background glow inside modal */}
        <div className="absolute -top-10 -right-10 w-32 h-32 bg-emerald-500/10 rounded-full blur-2xl pointer-events-none" />
        
        {/* Close / Cancel Button */}
        {onCancel && (
          <button onClick={onCancel} className="absolute top-4 right-4 text-slate-400 hover:text-white text-xs p-1.5 bg-slate-800 rounded-full w-7 h-7 flex items-center justify-center border border-slate-700 transition-colors">✕</button>
        )}

        {/* Header Icon */}
        <div className="w-12 h-12 sm:w-14 sm:h-14 bg-emerald-600 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center text-xl sm:text-2xl mb-3 shadow-lg shadow-emerald-900/40 border border-emerald-400/20">
          {mode === 'trading' ? '📈' : '🇵🇰'}
        </div>

        <h1 className="text-base sm:text-lg font-black text-white tracking-tight mb-0.5">
          {mode === 'trading' ? 'Trading Security Gate' : 'Roziq Security'}
        </h1>
        <p className="text-emerald-400 font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-[8px] sm:text-[9px] mb-4">
          {mode === 'trading' ? 'Confirm Transaction · تصدیق کریں' : 'Security Gate · سیکیورٹی گیٹ'}
        </p>

        {/* Biometric (app mode only) */}
        {biometricAvailable && step === 'enter' && mode === 'app' && (
          <button onClick={handleBiometric}
            className={`mb-4 flex flex-col items-center gap-1.5 group transition-all duration-300 ${biometricStatus === 'scanning' ? 'opacity-70' : 'hover:scale-105'}`}>
            <div className={`w-10 h-10 sm:w-12 sm:h-12 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center text-xl border transition-all duration-300 ${
              biometricStatus === 'scanning' ? 'border-emerald-400 bg-emerald-900/40 animate-pulse' :
              biometricStatus === 'success' ? 'border-emerald-400 bg-emerald-600/30' :
              biometricStatus === 'error' ? 'border-red-400 bg-red-900/30' :
              'border-white/10 bg-white/5 group-hover:border-emerald-500/50'}`}>
              {biometricStatus === 'success' ? '✅' : biometricStatus === 'error' ? '❌' : '🫱'}
            </div>
            <span className="text-[8px] font-black text-white/40 uppercase tracking-widest group-hover:text-emerald-400 transition-colors">
              {biometricStatus === 'scanning' ? 'Scanning...' : biometricStatus === 'success' ? 'Verified!' : biometricStatus === 'error' ? 'Try Again' : 'Fingerprint / Face ID'}
            </span>
          </button>
        )}

        {/* Title */}
        <h2 className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold text-slate-200 mb-0.5 text-center">{titles[step].en}</h2>
        <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mb-4 text-center font-medium">{titles[step].ar}</p>

        {/* PIN Dots */}
        <div className={`flex gap-3 sm:gap-4 mb-5 sm:mb-6 transition-all ${shake ? 'animate-bounce' : ''}`}>
          {[0,1,2,3].map(i => (
            <div key={i} className={`w-3 h-3 sm:w-3.5 sm:h-3.5 rounded-full border transition-all duration-200 ${
              currentPin.length > i
                ? 'bg-emerald-500 border-emerald-400 scale-125 shadow-[0_0_10px_rgba(16,185,129,0.5)]'
                : error ? 'border-red-500 bg-red-500/20' : 'border-slate-700 bg-slate-800/50'
            }`} />
          ))}
        </div>

        {/* Compact Keypad */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 w-full max-w-[260px]">
          {keys.map(num => (
            <button key={num} onClick={() => handlePress(num.toString())}
              className="aspect-square rounded-xl sm:rounded-2xl bg-slate-800/60 border border-slate-700/50 flex items-center justify-center text-base sm:text-lg font-bold text-white hover:bg-slate-750 active:scale-95 active:bg-emerald-900/40 transition-all duration-150 min-h-[44px] max-h-[54px] shadow-sm">
              {num}
            </button>
          ))}
          {/* Bottom row */}
          <div />
          <button onClick={() => handlePress('0')}
            className="aspect-square rounded-xl sm:rounded-2xl bg-slate-800/60 border border-slate-700/50 flex items-center justify-center text-base sm:text-lg font-bold text-white hover:bg-slate-750 active:scale-95 active:bg-emerald-900/40 transition-all duration-150 min-h-[44px] max-h-[54px] shadow-sm">
            0
          </button>
          <button onClick={handleBackspace}
            className="aspect-square rounded-xl sm:rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center text-lg sm:text-xl text-slate-400 hover:text-white transition-colors min-h-[44px] max-h-[54px] bg-slate-800/30 border border-slate-700/30">
            ⌫
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Actions */}
        <div className="mt-5 flex flex-col items-center gap-2 w-full pt-3 border-t border-slate-800">
          {step === 'enter' && (
            <button onClick={() => { setStep('set-new'); setPin(''); setError(false); }}
              className="text-slate-400 hover:text-emerald-400 text-[10px] sm:text-xs font-bold transition-colors uppercase tracking-widest">
              {mode === 'trading' ? 'Change Trading PIN' : 'Change PIN / پن تبدیل کریں'}
            </button>
          )}
          {step !== 'enter' && (
            <button onClick={() => { setStep('enter'); setNewPin(''); setConfirmPin(''); setError(false); }}
              className="text-slate-400 hover:text-red-400 text-[10px] sm:text-xs font-bold transition-colors uppercase tracking-widest">
              Cancel / واپس جائیں
            </button>
          )}
          {mode === 'trading' && onCancel && (
            <button onClick={onCancel} className="text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-300 text-[10px] sm:text-xs font-medium transition-colors mt-0.5">
              Cancel Transaction
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* FILE: src\components\PortfolioChart.tsx */
/* ================================================================ */

﻿"use client";
import { useState, useMemo } from "react";

interface Holding {
  symbol: string;
  name: string;
  invested: number;
  currentPrice: number;
  units: number;
  pnl: number;
}

interface PortfolioChartProps {
  balance: number;
  holdings: Holding[];
  totalValue: number;
}

export default function PortfolioChart({ balance, holdings, totalValue }: PortfolioChartProps) {
  const [activeRange, setActiveRange] = useState("1M");

  const totalWealth = balance + totalValue;
  const totalPnL = holdings.reduce((s, h) => s + h.pnl, 0);
  const totalInvested = holdings.reduce((s, h) => s + h.invested, 0);
  const pnlPercent = totalInvested > 0 ? ((totalPnL / totalInvested) * 100).toFixed(2) : "0.00";
  const isPnlPositive = totalPnL >= 0;

  const formatPKR = (n: number) =>
    n >= 10_000_000
      ? `${(n / 10_000_000).toFixed(2)} Cr`
      : n >= 100_000
      ? `${(n / 100_000).toFixed(1)} L`
      : n.toLocaleString("en-PK", { maximumFractionDigits: 0 });

  // Simulated chart data based on real portfolio values
  const chartData = useMemo(() => {
    const base = totalWealth > 0 ? totalWealth : 5_000_000;
    const growthFactor = 1 + (totalPnL / (base || 1)) * 0.5;
    const seed = [0.78, 0.82, 0.79, 0.87, 0.85, 0.92, 0.96, 1.0];
    return seed.map((v) => base * v * (growthFactor > 0.7 ? 1 : growthFactor));
  }, [totalWealth, totalPnL]);

  const max = Math.max(...chartData);
  const min = Math.min(...chartData);
  const range = max - min || 1;
  const points = chartData
    .map((d, i) => `${(i / (chartData.length - 1)) * 100},${100 - ((d - min) / range) * 85 - 5}`)
    .join(" ");

  const allocationPct = totalWealth > 0 ? Math.round((totalValue / totalWealth) * 100) : 0;

  return (
    <div className="premium-card p-5 sm:p-8 overflow-hidden relative group">
      {/* Header Row */}
      <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-5 sm:mb-6">
        <div>
          <p className="text-[9px] sm:text-[10px] font-black text-emerald-600 uppercase tracking-widest mb-1">
            Total Wealth
          </p>
          <p className="text-2xl sm:text-4xl font-black tracking-tight" style={{ color: "var(--text-primary)" }}>
            ₨{formatPKR(totalWealth)}
          </p>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mt-1.5 flex-wrap">
            <span
              className={`text-[10px] sm:text-xs font-black px-2.5 py-1 rounded-xl ${
                isPnlPositive
                  ? "bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border border-emerald-100"
                  : "bg-red-50 text-red-600 border border-red-100"
              }`}
            >
              {isPnlPositive ? "▲" : "▼"} ₨{formatPKR(Math.abs(totalPnL))} ({isPnlPositive ? "+" : ""}{pnlPercent}%)
            </span>
            <span className="text-[9px] sm:text-[10px] font-medium" style={{ color: "var(--text-muted)" }}>
              Portfolio P&L
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Range Selector */}
        <div className="flex gap-1 sm:gap-1.5 flex-wrap justify-end">
          {["1D", "1W", "1M", "1Y"].map((t) => (
            <button
              key={t}
              onClick={() => setActiveRange(t)}
              className={`text-[9px] sm:text-[10px] font-black px-2.5 py-1 sm:px-3 sm:py-1.5 rounded-xl transition-all duration-200 ${
                activeRange === t
                  ? "bg-emerald-600 text-white shadow-md shadow-emerald-900/20"
                  : "text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600"
              }`}
              style={activeRange !== t ? { background: "var(--bg-muted)" } : {}}
            >
              {t}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* SVG Chart */}
      <div className="relative h-28 sm:h-44 w-full mb-5 sm:mb-6">
        <svg viewBox="0 0 100 100" className="w-full h-full" preserveAspectRatio="none">
          <defs>
            <linearGradient id="chartGradient" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1">
              <stop offset="0%" stopColor={isPnlPositive ? "#10b981" : "#ef4444"} stopOpacity="0.2" />
              <stop offset="100%" stopColor={isPnlPositive ? "#10b981" : "#ef4444"} stopOpacity="0" />
            </linearGradient>
            <filter id="glow">
              <feGaussianBlur stdDeviation="1" result="coloredBlur" />
              <feMerge>
                <feMergeNode in="coloredBlur" />
                <feMergeNode in="SourceGraphic" />
              </feMerge>
            </filter>
          </defs>
          {/* Subtle grid */}
          {[20, 40, 60, 80].map((v) => (
            <line key={v} x1="0" y1={v} x2="100" y2={v} stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="0.3" opacity="0.1" />
          ))}
          {/* Area */}
          <polyline points={`0,100 ${points} 100,100`} fill="url(#chartGradient)" />
          {/* Line */}
          <polyline
            points={points}
            fill="none"
            stroke={isPnlPositive ? "#10b981" : "#ef4444"}
            strokeWidth="2"
            strokeLinecap="round"
            strokeLinejoin="round"
            filter="url(#glow)"
            style={{
              strokeDasharray: 300,
              strokeDashoffset: 300,
              animation: "drawPortfolioLine 1.5s ease-out forwards",
            }}
          />
          {/* End dot */}
          <circle
            cx="100"
            cy={100 - ((chartData[chartData.length - 1] - min) / range) * 85 - 5}
            r="2.5"
            fill={isPnlPositive ? "#10b981" : "#ef4444"}
            stroke="white"
            strokeWidth="1"
          />
        </svg>

        {/* Current value tooltip */}
        <div className="absolute top-1 right-0 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity duration-300">
          <div className="bg-slate-900 text-white text-[9px] sm:text-[10px] font-bold px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl shadow-xl whitespace-nowrap">
            ₨{formatPKR(totalWealth)}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Allocation Bar */}
      {(balance > 0 || totalValue > 0) && (
        <div className="mb-5">
          <div className="flex justify-between text-[9px] sm:text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-wider mb-2" style={{ color: "var(--text-muted)" }}>
            <span>Cash: {100 - allocationPct}%</span>
            <span>Invested: {allocationPct}%</span>
          </div>
          <div className="w-full h-2 rounded-full overflow-hidden" style={{ background: "var(--bg-muted)" }}>
            <div
              className="h-full bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-500 to-teal-400 rounded-full transition-all duration-700"
              style={{ width: `${allocationPct}%` }}
            />
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Breakdown Row */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 sm:gap-3 pt-4 sm:pt-5 border-t" style={{ borderColor: "var(--border-color)" }}>
        {[
          { label: "Cash", value: `₨${formatPKR(balance)}`, color: "text-blue-500" },
          { label: "Invested", value: `₨${formatPKR(totalValue)}`, color: "text-emerald-600" },
          {
            label: "P&L",
            value: `${isPnlPositive ? "+" : ""}₨${formatPKR(Math.abs(totalPnL))}`,
            color: isPnlPositive ? "text-emerald-600" : "text-red-500",
          },
        ].map((item, i) => (
          <div key={i} className={`text-center ${i === 1 ? "border-x" : ""}`} style={{ borderColor: "var(--border-color)" }}>
            <p className="text-[8px] sm:text-[9px] font-black uppercase tracking-wider mb-1" style={{ color: "var(--text-muted)" }}>
              {item.label}
            </p>
            <p className={`text-[11px] sm:text-sm font-black ${item.color}`}>{item.value}</p>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>

      <style>{`
        @keyframes drawPortfolioLine {
          to { stroke-dashoffset: 0; }
        }
      `}</style>
    </div>
  );
}

/* FILE: src\components\PortfolioDashboard.tsx */
/* ================================================================ */

﻿"use client";

interface Holding {
  symbol: string;
  name: string;
  invested: number;
  currentPrice: number;
  units: number;
  pnl: number;
  isWali?: boolean;
}

interface PortfolioDashboardProps {
  holdings: Holding[];
  totalValue: number;
  dailyPnl: number;
}

const COLORS = [
  "bg-emerald-500", "bg-teal-500", "bg-cyan-500",
  "bg-blue-500", "bg-indigo-500", "bg-violet-500",
];

export default function PortfolioDashboard({ holdings, totalValue, dailyPnl }: PortfolioDashboardProps) {
  const formatPKR = (n: number) =>
    n >= 10_000_000
      ? `${(n / 10_000_000).toFixed(2)} Cr`
      : n >= 100_000
      ? `${(n / 100_000).toFixed(1)} L`
      : n.toLocaleString("en-PK", { maximumFractionDigits: 0 });

  if (holdings.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="premium-card p-6 sm:p-10 text-center overflow-hidden relative">
        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-br from-emerald-50/40 to-transparent pointer-events-none rounded-[2rem]" />
        <div className="relative z-10">
          <div className="w-16 h-16 bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600 rounded-[1.5rem] flex items-center justify-center text-3xl mx-auto mb-4 shadow-inner border border-emerald-100">
            📈
          </div>
          <h3 className="text-base sm:text-lg font-black mb-2" style={{ color: "var(--text-primary)" }}>
            Grow Your Halal Wealth
          </h3>
          <p className="text-sm max-w-xs mx-auto leading-relaxed" style={{ color: "var(--text-secondary)" }}>
            Start investing in Shariah-compliant PSX stocks, Sukuks, or Hajj savings to see your portfolio here.
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const dailyPnlPct = totalValue > 0 ? Math.abs((dailyPnl / totalValue) * 100).toFixed(2) : "0.00";
  const isDailyPositive = dailyPnl >= 0;

  return (
    <div className="premium-card p-5 sm:p-7">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-5">
        <div>
          <p className="text-[9px] sm:text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-emerald-600 mb-1">
            Portfolio Value
          </p>
          <p className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-black tracking-tight" style={{ color: "var(--text-primary)" }}>
            ₨{formatPKR(totalValue)}
          </p>
        </div>
        <div
          className={`flex flex-col items-end gap-0.5 px-3 sm:px-4 py-2 rounded-2xl font-bold text-[10px] sm:text-xs border ${
            isDailyPositive
              ? "bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border-emerald-100"
              : "bg-red-50 text-red-600 border-red-100"
          }`}
        >
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1 font-black">
            {isDailyPositive ? "▲" : "▼"} {dailyPnlPct}%
          </div>
          <div className="text-[7px] sm:text-[8px] font-black uppercase tracking-tight opacity-60 text-right">
            Portfolio P&L
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Allocation Bar */}
      <div className="mb-5">
        <div
          className="flex justify-between text-[9px] sm:text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest mb-2"
          style={{ color: "var(--text-muted)" }}
        >
          <span>Asset Allocation</span>
          <span>{holdings.length} Holdings</span>
        </div>
        <div
          className="w-full h-3 rounded-full overflow-hidden flex p-0.5 gap-0.5"
          style={{ background: "var(--bg-muted)" }}
        >
          {holdings.map((h, i) => {
            const width = (h.invested / totalValue) * 100;
            return (
              <div
                key={h.symbol}
                style={{ width: `${width}%` }}
                className={`h-full ${COLORS[i % COLORS.length]} rounded-full transition-all duration-700`}
                title={`${h.symbol}: ${width.toFixed(1)}%`}
              />
            );
          })}
        </div>
        {/* Legend */}
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-x-3 gap-y-1 mt-2">
          {holdings.slice(0, 5).map((h, i) => (
            <div key={h.symbol} className="flex items-center gap-1">
              <div className={`w-2 h-2 rounded-full ${COLORS[i % COLORS.length]}`} />
              <span className="text-[9px] font-bold" style={{ color: "var(--text-muted)" }}>{h.symbol}</span>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Holdings List */}
      <div className="space-y-2">
        <h3
          className="text-[9px] sm:text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest mb-3"
          style={{ color: "var(--text-muted)" }}
        >
          Holdings
        </h3>
        {holdings.map((h) => {
          const currentVal = h.invested + h.pnl;
          const pnlPct = h.invested > 0 ? ((h.pnl / h.invested) * 100).toFixed(1) : "0.0";
          return (
            <div
              key={h.symbol}
              className="group flex justify-between items-center p-3 sm:p-4 rounded-2xl border transition-all duration-200 hover:border-emerald-200 hover:shadow-lg hover:shadow-emerald-900/5"
              style={{
                background: "var(--bg-muted)",
                borderColor: "var(--border-color)",
              }}
            >
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <div
                  className="w-10 h-10 sm:w-11 sm:h-11 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center text-sm font-black text-emerald-700 group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform shadow-sm border"
                  style={{ background: "var(--bg-card)", borderColor: "var(--border-color)" }}
                >
                  {h.symbol.slice(0, 3)}
                </div>
                <div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                    <span className="font-black text-xs sm:text-sm tracking-tight" style={{ color: "var(--text-primary)" }}>
                      {h.symbol}
                    </span>
                    {h.isWali && (
                      <span className="bg-emerald-500 text-white text-[7px] font-black uppercase px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full flex items-center gap-0.5">
                        <span className="w-1 h-1 bg-white rounded-full animate-pulse" />
                        Wali
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-[9px] sm:text-[10px] font-bold uppercase mt-0.5" style={{ color: "var(--text-muted)" }}>
                    {h.units.toFixed(2)} units
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="text-right">
                <div className="font-black text-xs sm:text-sm tracking-tight" style={{ color: "var(--text-primary)" }}>
                  ₨{formatPKR(currentVal)}
                </div>
                <div
                  className={`text-[9px] sm:text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-wide ${
                    h.pnl >= 0 ? "text-emerald-500" : "text-red-500"
                  }`}
                >
                  {h.pnl >= 0 ? "+" : ""}{pnlPct}%
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';

interface PurificationTrackerProps {
  onPurify: (amount: number) => void;
}

export default function PurificationTracker({ onPurify }: PurificationTrackerProps) {
  const [totalDividends] = useState(15000); // Mock data
  const [impurePercentage] = useState(2.8); // 2.8% from interest-bearing deposits
  const [selectedCharity, setSelectedCharity] = useState('Edhi Foundation');
  
  const impureAmount = (totalDividends * impurePercentage) / 100;
  const [purified, setPurified] = useState(false);

  const charities = [
    { name: 'Edhi Foundation', icon: '🚑' },
    { name: 'Indus Hospital', icon: '🏥' },
    { name: 'TCF (Education)', icon: '📚' },
  ];

  const handlePurify = () => {
    onPurify(impureAmount);
    setPurified(true);
  };

  return (
    <div className="premium-card p-4 sm:p-6 overflow-hidden relative group mb-4 sm:mb-6">
      {/* Dynamic Background */}
      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-br from-white via-white to-emerald-50/30 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity duration-1000"></div>
      
      <div className="relative z-10">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 sm:gap-4 mb-4 sm:mb-6">
          <div className="w-10 h-10 sm:w-12 sm:h-12 bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600 rounded-xl sm:rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center text-xl sm:text-2xl shadow-inner border border-emerald-100 flex-shrink-0">💧</div>
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-base sm:text-lg font-black text-slate-800 tracking-tight">Tathir Router</h2>
            <p className="text-[8px] sm:text-[10px] font-bold text-emerald-600 uppercase tracking-widest mt-0.5">Algorithmic Purification</p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:gap-4 mb-4 sm:mb-6">
          <div className="p-3 sm:p-4 bg-slate-50 rounded-2xl border border-slate-100">
            <div className="text-[9px] sm:text-[10px] font-black text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">Total Dividends</div>
            <div className="text-base sm:text-lg font-black text-slate-800 tracking-tight">PKR {totalDividends.toLocaleString()}</div>
          </div>
          <div className="p-3 sm:p-4 bg-rose-50 rounded-2xl border border-rose-100">
            <div className="text-[9px] sm:text-[10px] font-black text-rose-500 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">Impure ({impurePercentage}%)</div>
            <div className="text-base sm:text-lg font-black text-rose-600 tracking-tight">PKR {impureAmount.toLocaleString()}</div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {!purified ? (
          <div className="space-y-4 sm:space-y-5">
            <div>
              <label className="text-[9px] sm:text-[10px] font-black text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2 block px-0.5">Route Purification To:</label>
              <div className="flex gap-2 sm:gap-3">
                {charities.map((c) => (
                  <button 
                    key={c.name}
                    onClick={() => setSelectedCharity(c.name)}
                    className={`flex-1 p-2.5 sm:p-3 rounded-xl sm:rounded-2xl border transition-all duration-200 flex flex-col items-center gap-1 ${
                      selectedCharity === c.name 
                        ? 'bg-emerald-600 border-emerald-600 text-white shadow-md shadow-emerald-900/10 scale-105' 
                        : 'bg-white border-slate-100 text-slate-400 hover:border-emerald-200 hover:text-emerald-600'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <span className="text-lg sm:text-xl">{c.icon}</span>
                    <span className="text-[7px] sm:text-[8px] font-black uppercase tracking-tight">{c.name.split(' ')[0]}</span>
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            <button 
              onClick={handlePurify}
              className="w-full py-3 sm:py-3.5 bg-emerald-600 text-white font-black rounded-xl sm:rounded-[2rem] shadow-lg shadow-emerald-900/20 hover:bg-emerald-700 active:scale-95 transition-all text-xs sm:text-sm"
            >
              Authorize Auto-Route →
            </button>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="p-5 sm:p-6 bg-emerald-50 rounded-2xl sm:rounded-[2rem] border border-emerald-100 text-center animate-in zoom-in duration-300">
            <div className="w-12 h-12 sm:w-14 sm:h-14 bg-white rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-xl sm:text-2xl mx-auto mb-2 sm:mb-3 shadow-sm">✅</div>
            <h3 className="text-base sm:text-lg font-black text-emerald-800 tracking-tight">Wealth Purified</h3>
            <p className="text-[10px] sm:text-xs font-bold text-emerald-600/70 uppercase tracking-widest mt-0.5">Routed to {selectedCharity}</p>
          </div>
        )}

        <p className="mt-4 sm:mt-6 text-[8px] sm:text-[9px] font-bold text-slate-400 text-center uppercase tracking-widest leading-relaxed">
          *Real-time compliance audit by Roziq AI. <br />AAOIFI Standard No. 21 Compliant.
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}



/* ================================================================ */
/* FILE: src\components\RoziqAI.tsx */
/* ================================================================ */

'use client';
import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';

interface RoziqAIProps {
  balance?: number;
  holdings?: any[];
  transactions?: any[];
  lang?: 'en' | 'ur';
}

export default function RoziqAI({ balance = 0, holdings = [], transactions = [], lang = 'en' }: RoziqAIProps) {
  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isProcessing, setIsProcessing] = useState(false);
  const [prompt, setPrompt] = useState('');
  const [transcript, setTranscript] = useState('');
  const [response, setResponse] = useState(lang === 'ur' ? 'السلام علیکم! میں روزق اے آئی ہوں، آپ کا اسلامی مالیاتی مشیر۔ میں آپ کے اکاؤنٹس اور پورٹ فولیو دیکھ سکتا ہوں۔ بتائیں کیا رہنمائی چاہیے؟' : 'Assalam-o-Alaikum! I am Roziq AI, your Islamic Wealth Guardian. I have real-time access to your accounts and portfolio. How can I assist you today?');
  const [audioTextToPlay, setAudioTextToPlay] = useState('');
  
  const [isListening, setIsListening] = useState<'ur' | 'en' | false>(false);
  const [isSpeaking, setIsSpeaking] = useState(false);
  const recognitionRef = useRef<any>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
      const SpeechRecognition = (window as any).SpeechRecognition || (window as any).webkitSpeechRecognition;
      if (SpeechRecognition) {
        recognitionRef.current = new SpeechRecognition();
        recognitionRef.current.continuous = false;
        recognitionRef.current.interimResults = false;

        recognitionRef.current.onresult = (event: any) => {
          const text = event.results[0][0].transcript;
          const detectedSpeechLang = recognitionRef.current.lang.includes('ur') ? 'ur' : 'en';
          setTranscript(text);
          sendPrompt(text, detectedSpeechLang);
          setIsListening(false);
        };

        recognitionRef.current.onerror = () => {
          setIsListening(false);
        };

        recognitionRef.current.onend = () => setIsListening(false);
      }
    }
  }, [lang, balance, holdings]);

  const toggleListening = (speechLang: 'ur' | 'en') => {
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && 'speechSynthesis' in window) {
      window.speechSynthesis.resume(); // Resume audio context on direct user click
    }
    if (isListening === speechLang) {
      recognitionRef.current?.stop();
      setIsListening(false);
    } else {
      if ('vibrate' in navigator) navigator.vibrate(50);
      if (recognitionRef.current) {
        recognitionRef.current.lang = speechLang === 'ur' ? 'ur-PK' : 'en-US';
      }
      setTranscript('');
      setIsListening(speechLang);
      try {
        recognitionRef.current?.start();
      } catch {
        setIsListening(false);
        alert(lang === 'ur' ? 'مائیکروفون کی اجازت نہیں ہے، براہ کرم لکھ کر سوال پوچھیں۔' : 'Microphone permission denied. Please type your question.');
      }
    }
  };

  const speak = (text: string, speechLang?: 'ur' | 'en') => {
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && 'speechSynthesis' in window) {
      window.speechSynthesis.resume();
      window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
      const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(text);
      utterance.lang = speechLang === 'ur' ? 'ur-PK' : 'en-US';
      utterance.rate = 0.9;
      utterance.onstart = () => setIsSpeaking(true);
      utterance.onend = () => setIsSpeaking(false);
      utterance.onerror = () => setIsSpeaking(false);
      window.speechSynthesis.speak(utterance);
    }
  };

  const handleManualPlayVoice = () => {
    const textToSpeak = audioTextToPlay || response;
    if (textToSpeak) {
      speak(textToSpeak, lang === 'ur' ? 'ur' : 'en');
    }
  };

  const sendPrompt = async (text: string, explicitLang?: 'ur' | 'en') => {
    if (!text.trim()) return;
    setIsProcessing(true);
    setTranscript(text);
    setPrompt('');
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && 'speechSynthesis' in window) { window.speechSynthesis.resume(); }
    
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/chat', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ 
          prompt: text, 
          lang: explicitLang || lang,
          explicitLang,
          context: { balance, holdings, transactions } 
        }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (data.reply) {
        setResponse(data.reply);
        setAudioTextToPlay(data.audioText || data.reply);
        speak(data.audioText || data.reply, explicitLang || lang);
      } else {
        setResponse(lang === 'ur' ? 'معذرت، ایک خرابی پیش آ گئی۔' : 'Sorry, an error occurred.');
      }
    } catch {
      setResponse(lang === 'ur' ? 'نیٹ ورک کا مسئلہ ہے۔' : 'Network error.');
    } finally {
      setIsProcessing(false);
    }
  };

  const handleKeyPress = (e: React.KeyboardEvent) => {
    if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey) sendPrompt(prompt);
  };

  return (
    <>
      {/* Floating Action Button */}
      <button 
        onClick={() => setIsOpen(true)}
        className="fixed bottom-24 right-6 w-14 h-14 bg-gradient-to-br from-emerald-600 to-teal-700 text-white rounded-2xl shadow-2xl shadow-emerald-900/40 flex items-center justify-center text-2xl z-[100] hover:scale-110 active:scale-90 transition-all border border-emerald-400/30 group"
        title="Roziq AI Master Guardian"
      >
        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-emerald-400 rounded-2xl animate-ping opacity-25 group-hover:opacity-40"></div>
        🤖
      </button>

      {/* Unified AI Master Modal (Voice + Write + Account Context) */}
      {isOpen && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[2000] flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-3 sm:p-4" dir={lang === 'ur' ? 'rtl' : 'ltr'}>
          <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-slate-900/70 backdrop-blur-sm" onClick={() => setIsOpen(false)}></div>
          
          <div className="relative w-full max-w-md bg-white rounded-[2.5rem] shadow-2xl overflow-hidden animate-in slide-in-from-bottom-10 duration-300 flex flex-col max-h-[85vh] border border-slate-100">
            {/* Top Brand Header */}
            <div className="bg-gradient-to-br from-emerald-600 via-emerald-700 to-teal-800 p-5 sm:p-6 text-white flex-shrink-0 relative overflow-hidden">
              <div className="absolute -right-10 -top-10 w-32 h-32 bg-white/10 rounded-full blur-2xl"></div>
              
              <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-3 relative z-10">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                  <div className={`w-11 h-11 bg-white/15 backdrop-blur-md rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center text-2xl shadow-inner border border-white/20 transition-transform duration-500 ${isListening ? 'rotate-12 scale-110 bg-amber-500' : isSpeaking ? 'animate-bounce bg-emerald-50' : ''}`}>
                    🤖
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <h3 className="font-black text-base tracking-tight flex items-center gap-1.5">
                      ROZIQ <span className="text-emerald-200">AI</span>
                      <span className="text-[8px] bg-white/20 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full border border-white/30 uppercase tracking-widest">Master Agent</span>
                    </h3>
                    <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-emerald-100/80">{lang === 'ur' ? 'شرعی و مالیاتی مشیر (اکاؤنٹ سے منسلک)' : 'Sovereign Wealth Guardian (Account Linked)'}</p>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <button onClick={() => setIsOpen(false)} className="text-white/70 hover:text-white text-lg p-1 bg-white/10 rounded-xl w-8 h-8 flex items-center justify-center backdrop-blur-sm border border-white/10">✕</button>
              </div>
              
              {/* AI Response Display Area */}
              <div className="min-h-[80px] max-h-[150px] overflow-y-auto flex flex-col justify-center pr-1 relative z-10 mb-2">
                <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-black leading-relaxed mb-1 text-white shadow-sm">"{response}"</p>
                {transcript && <p className="text-emerald-200 text-[11px] font-bold opacity-80 italic">"{transcript}"</p>}
              </div>

              {/* Prominent Manual Play Voice Button */}
              <div className="relative z-10 flex justify-end mb-1">
                <button 
                  onClick={handleManualPlayVoice}
                  className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-white text-emerald-700 hover:bg-emerald-50 rounded-xl text-[10px] sm:text-xs font-black flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow-md transition-all active:scale-95 border border-emerald-100 flex-shrink-0"
                >
                  <span className={`${isSpeaking ? 'animate-spin' : ''}`}>🔊</span> 
                  <span>{lang === 'ur' ? 'آواز سنیں (Play Voice)' : 'Play Voice'}</span>
                </button>
              </div>

              {/* Live Context Badge */}
              <div className="mt-2 pt-2 border-t border-white/10 flex justify-between items-center text-[10px] font-bold text-emerald-100/90 relative z-10">
                <span>🏦 {lang === 'ur' ? 'موجودہ کیش بیلنس:' : 'Live Cash Balance:'} PKR {balance.toLocaleString()}</span>
                <span>💼 {holdings.length} {lang === 'ur' ? 'اسٹاکس' : 'Stocks'}</span>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Input & Controls Area (Voice + Write) */}
            <div className="p-4 sm:p-6 flex-1 overflow-y-auto flex flex-col justify-between space-y-4 bg-slate-50">
              <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 w-full">
                {/* Dual Mic Row */}
                <div className="flex gap-2 w-full justify-center">
                  <button 
                    onClick={() => toggleListening('ur')}
                    className={`flex-1 py-2.5 px-3 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-black transition-all active:scale-95 shadow-sm border ${
                      isListening === 'ur' ? 'bg-red-500 text-white animate-pulse border-red-600' : 'bg-white text-emerald-700 border-emerald-200 hover:bg-emerald-50'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <span>{isListening === 'ur' ? '⏹️' : '🎤'}</span> Speak Urdu (اردو)
                  </button>

                  <button 
                    onClick={() => toggleListening('en')}
                    className={`flex-1 py-2.5 px-3 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-black transition-all active:scale-95 shadow-sm border ${
                      isListening === 'en' ? 'bg-red-500 text-white animate-pulse border-red-600' : 'bg-white text-slate-700 border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <span>{isListening === 'en' ? '⏹️' : '🎤'}</span> Speak English
                  </button>
                </div>

                {/* Text Input Row */}
                <div className="flex gap-2 items-center">
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    value={prompt}
                    onChange={(e) => setPrompt(e.target.value)}
                    onKeyDown={handleKeyPress}
                    placeholder={lang === 'ur' ? 'لکھ کر سوال پوچھیں (زکوٰۃ، حلال اسٹاکس، بیلنس)...' : 'Write a question (Zakat, Halal stocks, Balance)...'}
                    className="flex-1 bg-white text-slate-800 placeholder-slate-400 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-xs font-bold outline-none border border-slate-200 focus:border-emerald-500 shadow-inner transition-colors"
                  />

                  <button 
                    onClick={() => sendPrompt(prompt)}
                    disabled={isProcessing || !prompt.trim()}
                    className="w-10 h-10 bg-emerald-600 text-white rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center text-lg font-black hover:bg-emerald-700 active:scale-95 transition-all disabled:opacity-50 flex-shrink-0 shadow-md"
                  >
                    {isProcessing ? '⏳' : '↑'}
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
              
              {/* Quick Prompt Chips */}
              <div>
                <p className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-400 text-center mb-2">
                  {lang === 'ur' ? 'یا ذیل میں سے فوری احکامات منتخب کریں:' : 'Or choose quick commands below:'}
                </p>
                <div className="w-full flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 justify-center">
                  {(lang === 'ur' 
                    ? ['میرا بیلنس کیا ہے؟', 'زکوٰۃ کا حساب لگائیں', 'میرے اسٹاکس دکھائیں', 'کیا میزان بینک حلال ہے؟', 'فیملی ہیلتھ کیا ہے؟', 'سکوک کیا ہیں؟']
                    : ['What is my balance?', 'Calculate my Zakat', 'Review my stocks', 'Is Meezan Bank halal?', 'What is Family Health?', 'What are Sukuks?']
                  ).map(t => (
                    <button 
                      key={t} 
                      onClick={() => sendPrompt(t, lang === 'ur' ? 'ur' : 'en')}
                      className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-white border border-slate-200/80 rounded-xl text-[10px] font-black text-slate-600 hover:bg-emerald-600 hover:text-white hover:border-emerald-600 transition-all active:scale-95 shadow-sm"
                    >
                      {t}
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="text-center pt-2 border-t border-slate-200/60">
                <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400">✓ Hackathon Master Agent (Voice + Write + Account Linked)</p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </>
  );
}



/* ================================================================ */
/* FILE: src\components\SettingsTab.tsx */
/* ================================================================ */

'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';

interface SettingsTabProps {
  lang: 'en' | 'ur';
  darkMode: boolean;
  onLangChange: (l: 'en' | 'ur') => void;
  onDarkToggle: (val: boolean) => void;
  onLock: () => void;
  isPremium?: boolean;
  eligibility?: any;
}

interface SettingItem {
  icon: string;
  labelEn: string;
  labelUr: string;
  descEn: string;
  descUr: string;
  action?: (() => void) | null;
  badge?: string | null;
  toggle?: { value: boolean; onToggle: () => void } | null;
}

interface SettingSection {
  titleEn: string;
  titleUr: string;
  items: SettingItem[];
}

export default function SettingsTab({ lang, darkMode, onLangChange, onDarkToggle, onLock, isPremium }: SettingsTabProps) {
  const isRtl = lang === 'ur';
  const t = (en: string, ur: string) => isRtl ? ur : en;

  const [showPinChange, setShowPinChange] = useState(false);
  const [showWaliSubs, setShowWaliSubs] = useState(false);
  const [oldPin, setOldPin] = useState('');
  const [newPin, setNewPin] = useState('');
  const [confirmPin, setConfirmPin] = useState('');
  const [pinMsg, setPinMsg] = useState<{ text: string; ok: boolean } | null>(null);

  const handlePinChange = () => {
    const stored = localStorage.getItem('roziq_pin') || '1234';
    if (oldPin !== stored) {
      setPinMsg({ text: t('Wrong current PIN', 'موجودہ پن غلط ہے'), ok: false });
      return;
    }
    if (newPin.length !== 4 || !/^\d{4}$/.test(newPin)) {
      setPinMsg({ text: t('PIN must be 4 digits', 'پن 4 ہندسوں کا ہونا چاہیے'), ok: false });
      return;
    }
    if (newPin !== confirmPin) {
      setPinMsg({ text: t('PINs do not match', 'پن مماثل نہیں ہیں'), ok: false });
      return;
    }
    localStorage.setItem('roziq_pin', newPin);
    setPinMsg({ text: t('PIN changed successfully!', 'پن کامیابی سے تبدیل ہو گیا!'), ok: true });
    setOldPin(''); setNewPin(''); setConfirmPin('');
    setTimeout(() => { setShowPinChange(false); setPinMsg(null); }, 1500);
  };

  const sections: SettingSection[] = [
    {
      titleEn: 'Elite Services', titleUr: 'ایلیٹ سروسز',
      items: [
        {
          icon: '🛡️',
          labelEn: 'Wali AI Autopilot', labelUr: 'ولی اے آئی آٹو پائلٹ',
          descEn: isPremium ? 'Active Guardian' : 'Elite Access Restricted',
          descUr: isPremium ? 'فعال محافظ' : 'ایلیٹ رسائی محدود ہے',
          action: () => setShowWaliSubs(v => !v),
          badge: isPremium ? 'PREMIUM' : 'LOCKED',
        },
      ],
    },
    {
      titleEn: 'Preferences', titleUr: 'ترجیحات',
      items: [
        {
          icon: '🌐',
          labelEn: 'Language', labelUr: 'زبان',
          descEn: lang === 'en' ? 'English (Current)' : 'اردو (موجودہ)',
          descUr: lang === 'ur' ? 'اردو (موجودہ)' : 'English (Current)',
          action: () => onLangChange(lang === 'en' ? 'ur' : 'en'),
          badge: lang === 'en' ? '🇬🇧 EN' : '🇵🇰 UR',
          toggle: null,
        },
        {
          icon: darkMode ? '🌙' : '☀️',
          labelEn: 'Dark Mode', labelUr: 'ڈارک موڈ',
          descEn: darkMode ? 'Dark theme active' : 'Light theme active',
          descUr: darkMode ? 'ڈارک تھیم فعال ہے' : 'لائٹ تھیم فعال ہے',
          action: null,
          badge: null,
          toggle: { value: darkMode, onToggle: () => onDarkToggle(!darkMode) },
        },
      ],
    },
    {
      titleEn: 'Security', titleUr: 'سیکیورٹی',
      items: [
        {
          icon: '🔐',
          labelEn: 'Change PIN', labelUr: 'پن تبدیل کریں',
          descEn: 'Update your 4-digit security PIN', descUr: '4 ہندسوں کا سیکیورٹی پن تبدیل کریں',
          action: () => setShowPinChange(v => !v),
          badge: null,
        },
        {
          icon: '🔒',
          labelEn: 'Lock App', labelUr: 'ایپ بند کریں',
          descEn: 'Return to PIN screen', descUr: 'پن اسکرین پر واپس جائیں',
          action: onLock,
          badge: null,
        },
      ],
    },
    {
      titleEn: 'About', titleUr: 'کے بارے میں',
      items: [
        {
          icon: '🕌',
          labelEn: 'Shariah Board', labelUr: 'شریعت بورڈ',
          descEn: 'Certified by AAOIFI Standards', descUr: 'AAOIFI معیارات کے مطابق تصدیق شدہ',
          action: null,
          badge: '✅',
        },
        {
          icon: 'ℹ️',
          labelEn: 'Version', labelUr: 'ورژن',
          descEn: 'Roziq v1.0 — Sovereign Edition', descUr: 'روزق v1.0 — خودمختار ایڈیشن',
          action: null,
          badge: null,
        },
      ],
    },
  ];

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4 sm:space-y-6" dir={isRtl ? 'rtl' : 'ltr'}>
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="mb-1 sm:mb-2">
        <h2 className="text-xl sm:text-3xl font-black text-slate-800 tracking-tighter">{t('Settings', 'ترتیبات')}</h2>
        <p className="text-slate-500 text-xs sm:text-sm font-medium mt-0.5">{t('Customize your Roziq experience.', 'اپنا روزق تجربہ اپنی مرضی کے مطابق بنائیں۔')}</p>
      </div>

      {/* Profile Card */}
      <div className="premium-card p-4 sm:p-6 flex items-center gap-3 sm:gap-4 mb-4 sm:mb-6">
        <div className="w-12 h-12 sm:w-16 sm:h-16 rounded-2xl sm:rounded-3xl bg-gradient-to-br from-emerald-500 to-teal-600 flex items-center justify-center text-2xl sm:text-3xl shadow-lg shadow-emerald-200 flex-shrink-0">
          🇵🇰
        </div>
        <div>
          <p className="font-black text-slate-800 text-base sm:text-lg tracking-tight">{t('Halal Investor', 'حلال سرمایہ کار')}</p>
          <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-emerald-600 font-bold">{t('Sovereign Account', 'خودمختار اکاؤنٹ')}</p>
          <p className="text-[10px] sm:text-xs text-slate-400 mt-0.5">{t('Data saved on this device', 'ڈیٹا اس ڈیوائس پر محفوظ ہے')}</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Sections */}
      {sections.map((section) => (
        <div key={section.titleEn} className="space-y-2 sm:space-y-3">
          <p className="text-[9px] sm:text-[10px] font-black text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-[0.2em] mb-1 sm:mb-2 px-1">
            {t(section.titleEn, section.titleUr)}
          </p>
          <div className="space-y-2">
            {section.items.map((item) => (
              <div key={item.labelEn}>
                <button
                  onClick={item.action || item.toggle?.onToggle || undefined}
                  disabled={!item.action && !item.toggle}
                  className={`w-full flex items-center gap-3 sm:gap-4 p-3.5 sm:p-5 bg-white rounded-2xl sm:rounded-3xl border border-slate-100 transition-all duration-200 text-left ${
                    item.action || item.toggle ? 'hover:border-emerald-200 hover:shadow-md active:scale-[0.98] cursor-pointer' : 'cursor-default'
                  }`}
                >
                  <div className="w-9 h-9 sm:w-11 sm:h-11 rounded-xl sm:rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 flex items-center justify-center text-lg sm:text-xl flex-shrink-0">
                    {item.icon}
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                    <p className="font-black text-slate-800 text-xs sm:text-sm tracking-tight">{t(item.labelEn, item.labelUr)}</p>
                    <p className="text-[10px] sm:text-xs text-slate-400 font-medium mt-0.5">{t(item.descEn, item.descUr)}</p>
                  </div>
                  {item.badge && (
                    <span className="text-[10px] sm:text-xs font-black bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 px-2.5 py-1 sm:px-3 sm:py-1 rounded-xl border border-emerald-100 flex-shrink-0">
                      {item.badge}
                    </span>
                  )}
                  {item.toggle && (
                    <div className={`w-10 h-5 sm:w-12 sm:h-6 rounded-full transition-colors flex items-center px-1 flex-shrink-0 ${
                      item.toggle.value ? 'bg-emerald-500' : 'bg-slate-200'
                    }`}>
                      <div className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 sm:w-4 sm:h-4 rounded-full bg-white transition-transform ${
                        item.toggle.value ? 'translate-x-5 sm:translate-x-6' : 'translate-x-0'
                      }`} />
                    </div>
                  )}
                  {item.action && !item.badge && !item.toggle && (
                    <span className="text-slate-300 text-base sm:text-lg flex-shrink-0">{isRtl ? '←' : '→'}</span>
                  )}
                </button>

                {/* Wali Subscription UI */}
                {item.labelEn === 'Wali AI Autopilot' && showWaliSubs && !isPremium && (
                  <div className="mt-2 p-4 sm:p-8 bg-slate-900 rounded-2xl sm:rounded-3xl border border-emerald-500/20 space-y-4 sm:space-y-6 animate-in fade-in slide-in-from-top-2 duration-300 shadow-lg">
                    <div className="text-center">
                      <p className="text-lg sm:text-xl font-black text-white italic tracking-tight">UNLOCK <span className="text-emerald-500">WALI AI</span></p>
                      <p className="text-[8px] sm:text-[9px] font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-widest mt-0.5 sm:mt-1">{t('Elite Guardian Access', 'ایلیٹ گارڈین تک رسائی')}</p>
                    </div>

                    <div className="space-y-3 sm:space-y-4">
                      <div className="space-y-1.5 sm:space-y-2">
                        <div className="flex justify-between text-[8px] sm:text-[9px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-400">
                          <span>{t('Total Invested', 'کل سرمایہ کاری')}</span>
                          <span className="text-emerald-500">15 Cr / 25 Cr</span>
                        </div>
                        <div className="w-full h-1 sm:h-1.5 bg-white/5 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                          <div className="h-full bg-emerald-500" style={{ width: '60%' }}></div>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                      <div className="space-y-1.5 sm:space-y-2">
                        <div className="flex justify-between text-[8px] sm:text-[9px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-400">
                          <span>{t('Realized Profit', 'حاصل شدہ منافع')}</span>
                          <span className="text-emerald-500">20 Cr / 50 Cr</span>
                        </div>
                        <div className="w-full h-1 sm:h-1.5 bg-white/5 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                          <div className="h-full bg-emerald-500" style={{ width: '40%' }}></div>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="p-3 sm:p-4 bg-emerald-500/5 rounded-xl sm:rounded-2xl border border-emerald-500/10 text-center">
                      <p className="text-[8px] sm:text-[9px] text-emerald-400/80 font-bold leading-relaxed italic">
                        {t('Reach 25 Crore Invested and 50 Crore Profit to Unlock Elite Subscription.', 'ایلیٹ سبسکرپشن کھولنے کے لیے 25 کروڑ کی سرمایہ کاری اور 50 کروڑ کا منافع حاصل کریں۔')}
                      </p>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 sm:gap-3">
                      <button disabled className="w-full py-3 sm:py-4 bg-white/5 text-slate-500 font-black text-[9px] sm:text-[10px] uppercase rounded-xl sm:rounded-2xl border border-white/5 cursor-not-allowed tracking-wider">
                        {t('Elite Monthly (Locked)', 'ایلیٹ ماہانہ (مقفل)')}
                      </button>
                      <button disabled className="w-full py-3 sm:py-4 bg-white/5 text-slate-500 font-black text-[9px] sm:text-[10px] uppercase rounded-xl sm:rounded-2xl border border-white/5 cursor-not-allowed tracking-wider">
                        {t('Sovereign Yearly (Locked)', 'سالانہ (مقفل)')}
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}
                {/* PIN Change Form */}
                {item.labelEn === 'Change PIN' && showPinChange && (
                  <div className="mt-2 p-4 sm:p-6 bg-slate-50 rounded-2xl sm:rounded-3xl border border-slate-100 space-y-3 sm:space-y-4 animate-in fade-in slide-in-from-top-2 duration-300 shadow-md">
                    {[
                      { label: t('Current PIN', 'موجودہ پن'), val: oldPin, set: setOldPin },
                      { label: t('New PIN', 'نیا پن'), val: newPin, set: setNewPin },
                      { label: t('Confirm PIN', 'پن کی تصدیق'), val: confirmPin, set: setConfirmPin },
                    ].map(({ label, val, set }) => (
                      <div key={label}>
                        <label className="text-[10px] sm:text-xs font-black text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider block mb-1 sm:mb-1.5">{label}</label>
                        <input
                          type="password"
                          inputMode="numeric"
                          maxLength={4}
                          value={val}
                          onChange={e => set(e.target.value.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 4))}
                          className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 sm:px-4 sm:py-3 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl sm:rounded-2xl font-black text-slate-800 text-center text-xl sm:text-2xl tracking-[0.5em] focus:outline-none focus:border-emerald-400 transition-colors outline-none"
                          placeholder="••••"
                        />
                      </div>
                    ))}
                    {pinMsg && (
                      <p className={`text-[11px] sm:text-xs font-bold text-center ${pinMsg.ok ? 'text-emerald-600' : 'text-red-500'}`}>
                        {pinMsg.text}
                      </p>
                    )}
                    <button
                      onClick={handlePinChange}
                      className="w-full py-2.5 sm:py-3 bg-emerald-600 text-white font-black rounded-xl sm:rounded-2xl hover:bg-emerald-700 transition-colors active:scale-95 text-xs sm:text-sm shadow-md"
                    >
                      {t('Update PIN', 'پن اپڈیٹ کریں')}
                    </button>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      ))}

      {/* Shariah pledge */}
      <div className="premium-card p-4 sm:p-6 text-center border border-emerald-100 mb-4 sm:mb-6">
        <p className="text-emerald-800 text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold leading-relaxed">
          {t(
            '"And Allah has permitted trade and forbidden interest." — Quran 2:275',
            '«اللہ ভাগে تجارت کو حلال اور سود کو حرام قرار دیا ہے» — القرآن 2:275'
          )}
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
