/* FILE: src\components\ShariahScreener.tsx */
/* ================================================================ */

'use client';
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';

const mockStocks = [
  { symbol: 'SYS', name: 'Systems Limited', status: 'Halal', debt: '2.5%', income: '0.1%', shariah: 'Compliant' },
  { symbol: 'LUCK', name: 'Lucky Cement', status: 'Halal', debt: '12.4%', income: '1.2%', shariah: 'Compliant' },
  { symbol: 'MEBL', name: 'Meezan Bank', status: 'Halal', debt: '0%', income: '0%', shariah: 'Islamic Bank' },
  { symbol: 'ENGRO', name: 'Engro Corp', status: 'Halal', debt: '28.1%', income: '4.2%', shariah: 'Compliant' },
  { symbol: 'TRG', name: 'TRG Pakistan', status: 'Halal', debt: '8.1%', income: '1.2%', shariah: 'Compliant' },
  { symbol: 'HBL', name: 'Habib Bank', status: 'Doubtful', debt: 'N/A', income: 'Conventional', shariah: 'Non-Compliant' },
];

export default function ShariahScreener() {
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<any>(null);
  const [livePrice, setLivePrice] = useState<any>(null);
  const [loadingPrice, setLoadingPrice] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!selected) return;
    setLivePrice(null);
    setLoadingPrice(true);

    // Direct client-side fetch to Yahoo Finance (no server route needed)
    const symbol = `${selected.symbol}.KA`;
    fetch(`https://query1.finance.yahoo.com/v8/finance/chart/${symbol}?interval=1d&range=1d`)
      .then(r => r.json())
      .then(data => {
        const result = data?.chart?.result?.[0];
        if (result) {
          const price = result.meta.regularMarketPrice;
          const prev = result.meta.chartPreviousClose;
          setLivePrice({
            price,
            changePercent: ((price - prev) / prev) * 100,
          });
        }
      })
      .catch(() => setLivePrice(null))
      .finally(() => setLoadingPrice(false));
  }, [selected]);

  const filtered = mockStocks.filter(s =>
    s.symbol.toLowerCase().includes(query.toLowerCase()) ||
    s.name.toLowerCase().includes(query.toLowerCase())
  );

  return (
    <div className="premium-card p-8">
      <div className="mb-8">
        <h3 className="text-xs font-black text-emerald-600 uppercase tracking-widest mb-1">Stock Guard</h3>
        <p className="text-2xl font-black text-slate-800 tracking-tighter">Shariah Screener</p>
      </div>

      <div className="relative mb-6">
        <input
          type="text"
          placeholder="Search Symbol (e.g. LUCK, SYS)..."
          className="w-full bg-slate-50 border border-slate-100 rounded-2xl py-4 px-6 text-sm font-bold focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-500/20 transition-all"
          value={query}
          onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)}
        />
        <span className="absolute right-6 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-300 text-lg">🔍</span>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-3">
        {query && filtered.map(s => (
          <button
            key={s.symbol}
            onClick={() => setSelected(s)}
            className="w-full flex justify-between items-center p-4 bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-50 hover:border-emerald-100 hover:shadow-lg transition-all text-left"
          >
            <div>
              <div className="font-black text-slate-800">{s.symbol}</div>
              <div className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase">{s.name}</div>
            </div>
            <div className={`px-4 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest ${
              s.status === 'Halal' ? 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600' : 'bg-red-50 text-red-600'
            }`}>
              {s.status}
            </div>
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      {selected && (
        <div className="mt-8 p-6 bg-slate-900 rounded-[2rem] text-white">
          <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-6">
            <div>
              <h4 className="text-xl font-black tracking-tighter">{selected.name}</h4>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap mt-1">
                <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-emerald-400 uppercase tracking-widest">Compliance Audit</p>
                {loadingPrice && <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 font-bold animate-pulse">Fetching live price...</span>}
                {livePrice && (
                  <span className={`text-[10px] px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full font-black ${
                    livePrice.changePercent >= 0 ? 'bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-400' : 'bg-red-500/20 text-red-400'
                  }`}>
                    LIVE: PKR {livePrice.price.toLocaleString()} ({livePrice.changePercent >= 0 ? '+' : ''}{livePrice.changePercent.toFixed(2)}%)
                  </span>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
            <button onClick={() => { setSelected(null); setLivePrice(null); }} className="text-slate-500 hover:text-white text-2xl">✕</button>
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
            <div className="bg-white/5 p-4 rounded-2xl border border-white/10">
              <div className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase mb-1">Debt Ratio</div>
              <div className="text-lg font-black">{selected.debt}</div>
              <div className="text-[8px] text-emerald-500 font-bold uppercase mt-1">Limit: &lt;33%</div>
            </div>
            <div className="bg-white/5 p-4 rounded-2xl border border-white/10">
              <div className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase mb-1">Impure Income</div>
              <div className="text-lg font-black">{selected.income}</div>
              <div className="text-[8px] text-emerald-500 font-bold uppercase mt-1">Limit: &lt;5%</div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="mt-4 p-4 bg-emerald-600/20 border border-emerald-500/30 rounded-2xl flex items-center gap-3">
            <span className="text-xl text-emerald-400">🛡️</span>
            <p className="text-xs font-bold leading-relaxed">
              This stock is <strong>{selected.shariah}</strong> according to the latest Shariah audit.
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}



/* ================================================================ */
/* FILE: src\components\SovereignScan.tsx */
/* ================================================================ */

'use client';
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';

interface SovereignScanProps {
  onComplete: () => void;
  isOpen: boolean;
}

export default function SovereignScan({ onComplete, isOpen }: SovereignScanProps) {
  const [progress, setProgress] = useState(0);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState('Initializing Sovereign Bio-Link...');

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) return;

    setProgress(0);
    setStatus('Initializing Sovereign Bio-Link...');
    
    const timer1 = setTimeout(() => setStatus('Scanning Biometrics...'), 1000);
    const timer2 = setTimeout(() => setStatus('Verifying Shariah Identity...'), 2500);
    const timer3 = setTimeout(() => setStatus('Authorized.'), 4000);

    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      setProgress(prev => {
        if (prev >= 100) {
          clearInterval(interval);
          setTimeout(onComplete, 500);
          return 100;
        }
        return prev + 2;
      });
    }, 80);

    return () => {
      clearTimeout(timer1);
      clearTimeout(timer2);
      clearTimeout(timer3);
      clearInterval(interval);
    };
  }, [isOpen, onComplete]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-slate-900/90 backdrop-blur-xl flex items-center justify-center z-[100] animate-in fade-in duration-500">
      <div className="text-center max-w-xs w-full p-8">
        {/* Biometric Icon Animation */}
        <div className="relative w-32 h-32 mx-auto mb-12">
          <div className="absolute inset-0 border-4 border-emerald-500/20 rounded-full"></div>
          <div 
            className="absolute inset-0 border-4 border-emerald-500 rounded-full animate-spin transition-all duration-300"
            style={{ clipPath: `inset(0 0 ${100 - progress}% 0)` }}
          ></div>
          <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center text-5xl">
            {progress < 40 ? '🔐' : progress < 80 ? '👤' : '✅'}
          </div>
          
          {/* Scanning Line */}
          {progress < 100 && (
            <div 
              className="absolute left-0 right-0 h-1 bg-emerald-400 shadow-[0_0_15px_rgba(52,211,153,0.8)] z-10 animate-pulse"
              style={{ top: `${progress}%` }}
            ></div>
          )}
        </div>

        <h2 className="text-white font-black text-xl tracking-tight mb-2">{status}</h2>
        <div className="w-full bg-white/10 h-1 rounded-full overflow-hidden mb-8">
          <div 
            className="h-full bg-emerald-500 transition-all duration-300 shadow-[0_0_10px_rgba(16,185,129,0.5)]"
            style={{ width: `${progress}%` }}
          ></div>
        </div>
        
        <p className="text-slate-400 text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-widest">
          Sovereign ID Protection Active
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}



/* ================================================================ */
/* FILE: src\components\Toast.tsx */
/* ================================================================ */

﻿"use client";
import { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from "react";

export type ToastType = "success" | "error" | "info" | "warning";

interface ToastItem {
  id: number;
  message: string;
  type: ToastType;
}

let toastListeners: Array<(toast: Omit<ToastItem, "id">) => void> = [];

export const toast = {
  success: (message: string) => toastListeners.forEach(fn => fn({ message, type: "success" })),
  error: (message: string) => toastListeners.forEach(fn => fn({ message, type: "error" })),
  info: (message: string) => toastListeners.forEach(fn => fn({ message, type: "info" })),
  warning: (message: string) => toastListeners.forEach(fn => fn({ message, type: "warning" })),
};

const ICONS: Record<ToastType, string> = {
  success: "✅",
  error: "❌",
  info: "ℹ️",
  warning: "⚠️",
};

const COLORS: Record<ToastType, string> = {
  success: "border-l-emerald-500",
  error: "border-l-red-500",
  info: "border-l-blue-500",
  warning: "border-l-amber-500",
};

export default function ToastContainer() {
  const [toasts, setToasts] = useState<ToastItem[]>([]);

  const addToast = useCallback((t: Omit<ToastItem, "id">) => {
    const id = Date.now() + Math.random();
    setToasts(prev => [...prev, { ...t, id }]);
    setTimeout(() => {
      setToasts(prev => prev.filter(x => x.id !== id));
    }, 4000);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    toastListeners.push(addToast);
    return () => {
      toastListeners = toastListeners.filter(fn => fn !== addToast);
    };
  }, [addToast]);

  if (toasts.length === 0) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed bottom-24 sm:bottom-8 right-4 z-[9999] flex flex-col-reverse gap-2 max-w-xs w-full">
      {toasts.map(t => (
        <div
          key={t.id}
          className={`flex items-center gap-3 p-4 rounded-2xl border-l-4 ${COLORS[t.type]} shadow-2xl border border-slate-100 animate-in slide-in-from-right-5 duration-300`}
          style={{ background: "var(--bg-card)", color: "var(--text-primary)" }}
        >
          <span className="text-base flex-shrink-0">{ICONS[t.type]}</span>
          <p className="text-sm font-bold leading-tight flex-1">{t.message}</p>
          <button
            onClick={() => setToasts(prev => prev.filter(x => x.id !== t.id))}
            className="text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 text-sm flex-shrink-0 ml-1"
          >
            ✕
          </button>
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}



/* ================================================================ */
/* FILE: src\components\VoiceAgent.tsx */
/* ================================================================ */

'use client';
import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';

interface VoiceAgentProps {
  onCommand: (cmd: string) => void;
  lang?: 'en' | 'ur';
}

export default function VoiceAgent({ onCommand, lang = 'en' }: VoiceAgentProps) {
  const [isListening, setIsListening] = useState(false);
  const [statusText, setStatusText] = useState('');
  const [hasSupport, setHasSupport] = useState(true);
  const [showHelp, setShowHelp] = useState(false);
  const recognitionRef = useRef<any>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
      const SpeechRecognition = (window as any).SpeechRecognition || (window as any).webkitSpeechRecognition;
      if (SpeechRecognition) {
        recognitionRef.current = new SpeechRecognition();
        recognitionRef.current.lang = lang === 'ur' ? 'ur-PK' : 'en-US';
        recognitionRef.current.continuous = false;
        recognitionRef.current.interimResults = false;

        recognitionRef.current.onresult = (event: any) => {
          const text = event.results[0][0].transcript.toLowerCase();
          setStatusText(text);
          onCommand(text);
          setIsListening(false);
          setTimeout(() => setStatusText(''), 3000);
        };

        recognitionRef.current.onerror = (err: any) => {
          console.error('Speech recognition error', err);
          setStatusText(lang === 'ur' ? 'آواز سمجھ نہیں آئی، مینوئل بٹن استعمال کریں' : 'Could not understand, please use manual buttons');
          setIsListening(false);
          setTimeout(() => setStatusText(''), 3000);
        };

        recognitionRef.current.onend = () => setIsListening(false);
      } else {
        setHasSupport(false);
      }
    }
  }, [onCommand, lang]);

  const speak = (text: string) => {
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && 'speechSynthesis' in window) {
      window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
      const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(text);
      utterance.lang = lang === 'ur' ? 'ur-PK' : 'en-US';
      utterance.rate = 0.9;
      window.speechSynthesis.speak(utterance);
    }
  };

  const toggleListening = () => {
    if (!hasSupport) {
      setShowHelp(true);
      return;
    }

    if (isListening) {
      recognitionRef.current?.stop();
      setIsListening(false);
    } else {
      const promptText = lang === 'ur' ? 'بولیں۔ ڈیپوزٹ، وِڈڈرا، یا بیلنس؟' : 'Speak now: Deposit, Withdraw, or Balance?';
      setStatusText(promptText);
      speak(promptText);
      try {
        recognitionRef.current?.start();
        setIsListening(true);
      } catch (e) {
        setIsListening(false);
        setStatusText(lang === 'ur' ? 'مائیکروفون کی اجازت نہیں ہے، مینوئل بٹن استعمال کریں' : 'Microphone permission denied, use manual buttons');
        setShowHelp(true); // Auto-open manual menu if mic fails
      }
    }
  };

  const sampleCommands = [
    { labelEn: 'Deposit 5000', labelUr: 'ڈیپوزٹ 5000', cmd: 'deposit' },
    { labelEn: 'Withdraw 2000', labelUr: 'وِڈڈرا 2000', cmd: 'withdraw' },
    { labelEn: 'Check Balance', labelUr: 'بیلنس بتائیں', cmd: 'balance' },
    { labelEn: 'Pay Zakat', labelUr: 'زکوٰۃ ادا کریں', cmd: 'zakat' },
    { labelEn: 'Buy Stock', labelUr: 'اسٹاک خریدیں', cmd: 'buy' },
    { labelEn: 'Family Health', labelUr: 'فیملی ہیلتھ', cmd: 'health' },
    { labelEn: 'Overseas Invest', labelUr: 'اوورسیز انویسٹ', cmd: 'overseas' }
  ];

  return (
    <div className="fixed bottom-20 right-6 z-50 flex flex-col items-end gap-3" dir={lang === 'ur' ? 'rtl' : 'ltr'}>
      {/* Status / Command Feedback */}
      {statusText && (
        <div className="bg-slate-900/90 backdrop-blur-md text-white border border-emerald-500/30 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-2xl shadow-xl text-xs font-bold animate-in fade-in slide-in-from-bottom-2 duration-300 max-w-xs text-right">
          <p className="text-[8px] text-emerald-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-0.5">Voice AI Status</p>
          {statusText}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Manual Command Helper / Fallback Menu */}
      {showHelp && (
        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-[2rem] p-4.5 shadow-2xl max-w-xs w-full animate-in fade-in slide-in-from-bottom-4 duration-300">
          <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-3 border-b border-slate-100 pb-2.5">
            <h4 className="text-xs font-black text-slate-800 flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <span>⌨️</span> {lang === 'ur' ? 'آواز کے احکامات (مینوئل)' : 'Voice Commands (Manual)'}
            </h4>
            <button onClick={() => setShowHelp(false)} className="text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 text-xs font-bold p-1">✕</button>
          </div>
          
          {!hasSupport && (
            <p className="text-amber-700 text-[10px] font-bold mb-3 bg-amber-50 p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-amber-200/60 leading-relaxed">
              ⚠️ {lang === 'ur' ? 'آپ کا براؤزر وائس فیچر کو سپورٹ نہیں کرتا۔ نیچے دیے گئے بٹن استعمال کریں:' : 'Your browser does not support speech recognition. Use quick commands below:'}
            </p>
          )}

          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
            {sampleCommands.map((sc, i) => (
              <button key={i} onClick={() => { onCommand(sc.cmd); setShowHelp(false); }}
                className="p-2.5 bg-slate-50 hover:bg-emerald-50 hover:text-emerald-700 text-slate-700 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold border border-slate-200/60 hover:border-emerald-200 transition-all text-center active:scale-95 shadow-sm">
                {lang === 'ur' ? sc.labelUr : sc.labelEn}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
          <p className="text-[9px] text-slate-400 text-center mt-3 font-bold">✓ Shariah Voice Navigation</p>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Main Mic Button & Manual Toggle */}
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <button 
          onClick={() => setShowHelp(v => !v)}
          className="w-11 h-11 rounded-2xl bg-white border border-slate-200 shadow-lg flex items-center justify-center text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-50 hover:text-slate-900 transition-all active:scale-95 text-lg"
          title="Manual Command Menu"
        >
          💬
        </button>

        <button
          onClick={toggleListening}
          className={`px-4.5 py-3 rounded-2xl shadow-xl transition-all duration-300 font-black text-xs flex items-center gap-2 ${
            isListening ? 'bg-red-500 text-white scale-105 animate-pulse ring-4 ring-red-500/30' : 
            hasSupport ? 'bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-600 to-teal-600 hover:from-emerald-500 hover:to-teal-500 text-white hover:shadow-emerald-900/20' : 
            'bg-slate-800 text-white hover:bg-slate-700'
          }`}
        >
          <span className="text-base">{isListening ? '🔴' : hasSupport ? '🎤' : '⌨️'}</span>
          <span>{isListening ? (lang === 'ur' ? 'سن رہا ہے...' : 'Listening...') : hasSupport ? (lang === 'ur' ? 'وائس کمانڈ' : 'Voice AI') : (lang === 'ur' ? 'کمانڈز مینو' : 'Commands')}</span>
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* FILE: src\components\WaliAutopilot.tsx */
/* ================================================================ */

'use client';
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';

interface WaliAutopilotProps {
  lang: 'en' | 'ur';
}

export default function WaliAutopilot({ lang }: WaliAutopilotProps) {
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<'idle' | 'scanning' | 'proposing' | 'locked'>('locked');
  const [eligibility, setEligibility] = useState({ 
    isPremium: false, 
    totalInvested: 0, 
    totalProfit: 0, 
    reqInvest: 25000000, 
    reqProfit: 2500000 
  });
  const [logs, setLogs] = useState<{ msg: string; urdu: string; time: string }[]>([]);
  const [vaultBalance, setVaultBalance] = useState(0);
  const [showManageVault, setShowManageVault] = useState(false);
  const [allocAmount, setAllocAmount] = useState('');
  const [proposal, setProposal] = useState<any>(null);
  const [showPinEntry, setShowPinEntry] = useState(false);
  const [pin, setPin] = useState('');

  const t = (en: string, ur: string) => (lang === 'ur' ? ur : en);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetch('/api/agent/autopilot', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ action: 'scan' })
    }).then(r => r.json()).then(d => {
      if (d.waliBalance !== undefined) setVaultBalance(d.waliBalance);
      setEligibility({
        isPremium: d.isPremium || false,
        totalInvested: d.totalInvested || 0,
        totalProfit: d.totalProfit || 0,
        reqInvest: 25000000,
        reqProfit: 2500000
      });
      if (d.isPremium) setStatus('idle');
    });
  }, []);

  const handleAllocate = async (action: 'allocate' | 'deallocate') => {
    const res = await fetch('/api/agent/autopilot', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ action, amount: parseFloat(allocAmount) })
    });
    const data = await res.json();
    if (data.success) {
      setVaultBalance(data.waliBalance);
      setAllocAmount('');
      addLog(`Vault updated: ${data.waliBalance} PKR`, `والٹ اپ ڈیٹ ہو گیا: ${data.waliBalance} روپے`);
      window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('balanceUpdate'));
    }
  };

  const startScanning = async () => {
    if (vaultBalance <= 0) {
      alert(t('Please add funds to Wali Vault first.', 'براہ کرم پہلے ولی والٹ میں فنڈز شامل کریں۔'));
      return;
    }
    setStatus('scanning');
    addLog('Scanning PSX for secure opportunities...', 'محفوظ مواقع کے لیے پی ایس ایکس کو اسکین کیا جا رہا ہے...');
    
    await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 2500));
    
    const res = await fetch('/api/agent/autopilot', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ action: 'scan' })
    });
    const data = await res.json();
    
    if (data.recommendations && data.recommendations.length > 0) {
      setProposal(data.recommendations[0]);
      setStatus('proposing');
      addLog('Investment proposal ready for review.', 'سرمایہ کاری کی تجویز جائزے کے لیے تیار ہے۔');
    } else {
      setStatus('idle');
      addLog('No stable opportunities found. Try again later.', 'کوئی مستحکم مواقع نہیں ملے۔ بعد میں دوبارہ کوشش کریں۔');
    }
  };

  const addLog = (msg: string, urdu: string) => {
    const time = new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit', second: '2-digit' });
    setLogs(prev => [{ msg, urdu, time }, ...prev]);
  };

  const confirmInvestment = async () => {
    const storedPin = localStorage.getItem('roziq_pin') || '1234';
    if (pin !== storedPin) {
      alert(t('Incorrect PIN', 'غلط پن'));
      return;
    }
    setShowPinEntry(false);
    setPin('');
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/agent/autopilot', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ action: 'invest', symbol: proposal.symbol })
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (data.success) {
        addLog(data.message, data.urduMessage);
        setVaultBalance(data.waliBalance);
        setProposal(null);
        setStatus('idle');
        window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('balanceUpdate'));
        if ('vibrate' in navigator) navigator.vibrate(200);
      } else {
        addLog(data.error || 'Trade failed', 'تجارت ناکام ہو گئی');
      }
    } catch (err) {
      addLog('Network error during trade.', 'تجارت کے دوران نیٹ ورک کی خرابی۔');
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="p-4 sm:p-6 rounded-2xl sm:rounded-[3rem] bg-slate-950 border border-emerald-500/10 shadow-xl overflow-hidden relative transition-all duration-500 mb-4 sm:mb-6">
      <div className="absolute top-0 right-0 w-32 h-32 sm:w-48 sm:h-48 bg-emerald-500/5 rounded-full blur-[60px] sm:blur-[80px]" />
      
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4 sm:mb-6">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 sm:gap-4">
          <div className={`w-10 h-10 sm:w-14 sm:h-14 rounded-xl sm:rounded-[2rem] flex items-center justify-center text-xl sm:text-3xl transition-all duration-700 flex-shrink-0 ${status === 'scanning' ? 'bg-emerald-500 shadow-[0_0_20px_rgba(16,185,129,0.4)] rotate-12' : 'bg-slate-900 border border-white/5'}`}>
            🤖
          </div>
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-lg sm:text-2xl font-black text-white tracking-tighter italic">WALI<span className="text-emerald-500 text-xs sm:text-sm align-top ml-1">AI</span></h2>
            <p className="text-[8px] sm:text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-[0.2em]">{t('Sovereign Guardian', 'خود مختار محافظ')}</p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {eligibility.isPremium ? (
          status === 'idle' && (
            <button
              onClick={startScanning}
              className="px-5 py-2.5 sm:px-8 sm:py-3 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white rounded-xl sm:rounded-[1.5rem] font-black text-[11px] sm:text-xs uppercase tracking-widest shadow-lg shadow-emerald-900/40 transition-all active:scale-95"
            >
              {t('Start Scanning', 'اسکیننگ شروع کریں')}
            </button>
          )
        ) : (
          <div className="text-right flex-shrink-0">
            <p className="text-[7px] sm:text-[8px] font-black text-amber-500 uppercase tracking-widest mb-0.5 sm:mb-1">{t('Elite Access Pending', 'ایلیٹ رسائی باقی ہے')}</p>
            <div className="flex gap-1 justify-end">
              <div className="w-8 sm:w-12 h-1 bg-white/10 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                <div className="h-full bg-emerald-500" style={{ width: `${Math.min(100, (eligibility.totalInvested / 250000000) * 100)}%` }}></div>
              </div>
              <div className="w-8 sm:w-12 h-1 bg-white/10 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                <div className="h-full bg-emerald-500" style={{ width: `${Math.min(100, (eligibility.totalProfit / 500000000) * 100)}%` }}></div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {!eligibility.isPremium && (
        <div className="mb-4 sm:mb-6 p-3.5 sm:p-5 bg-white/5 rounded-xl sm:rounded-3xl border border-white/5 flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
          <div>
            <p className="text-[8px] sm:text-[10px] font-black text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest">{t('Progress to Wali AI', 'ولی اے آئی کی طرف پیشرفت')}</p>
            <p className="text-[11px] sm:text-xs font-bold text-emerald-400 mt-0.5 sm:mt-1">
              {Math.floor((eligibility.totalInvested / 250000000) * 100)}% {t('Invested', 'سرمایہ کاری')} • {Math.floor((eligibility.totalProfit / 500000000) * 100)}% {t('Profit', 'منافع')}
            </p>
          </div>
          <button className="text-[8px] sm:text-[9px] font-black text-white bg-white/10 px-3 py-1.5 sm:px-4 sm:py-2 rounded-lg sm:rounded-xl hover:bg-white/20 transition-all uppercase tracking-widest flex-shrink-0">
             {t('Elite Access Only', 'صرف ایلیٹ رسائی')}
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
      
      {/* Vault Stats */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-5 gap-2 sm:gap-3 mb-4 sm:mb-6">
        <div className="col-span-3 bg-white/5 border border-white/5 rounded-xl sm:rounded-[2rem] p-4 sm:p-6 relative overflow-hidden group">
          <div className="absolute top-0 right-0 p-3 sm:p-4 opacity-10 group-hover:opacity-20 transition-opacity text-xl sm:text-2xl pointer-events-none">🏦</div>
          <p className="text-[8px] sm:text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-widest mb-1 sm:mb-2">{t('Wali Vault', 'ولی والٹ')}</p>
          <p className="text-lg sm:text-3xl font-black text-white tabular-nums tracking-tighter">
            <span className="text-[10px] sm:text-xs font-medium text-emerald-500 mr-1 sm:mr-1.5">PKR</span>
            {vaultBalance.toLocaleString()}
          </p>
          <button 
            onClick={() => setShowManageVault(!showManageVault)}
            className="mt-2 sm:mt-3 text-[9px] sm:text-[10px] font-black text-emerald-500 uppercase flex items-center gap-1 hover:gap-1.5 transition-all"
          >
            {t('Manage Capital', 'سرمایہ کا انتظام')} <span>→</span>
          </button>
        </div>
        <div className="col-span-2 bg-emerald-500/5 border border-emerald-500/10 rounded-xl sm:rounded-[2rem] p-4 sm:p-6 flex flex-col justify-center items-center text-center">
          <p className="text-[8px] sm:text-[10px] font-bold text-emerald-500/60 uppercase tracking-widest mb-0.5 sm:mb-1">{t('Security', 'سیکیورٹی')}</p>
          <p className="text-sm sm:text-xl font-black text-emerald-500">{t('Verified', 'تصدیق شدہ')}</p>
          <div className="flex gap-1 mt-1 sm:mt-2">
            {[1,2,3].map(i => <div key={i} className="w-1 h-1 bg-emerald-500 rounded-full" />)}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Manage Vault Overlay */}
      {showManageVault && (
        <div className="mb-4 sm:mb-6 p-4 sm:p-6 bg-slate-900 border border-white/5 rounded-xl sm:rounded-[2.5rem] animate-in slide-in-from-top-4 duration-300">
          <div className="flex gap-2 sm:gap-3">
            <input 
              type="number" 
              value={allocAmount}
              onChange={(e) => setAllocAmount(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="0.00"
              className="flex-1 bg-black border border-white/10 rounded-xl sm:rounded-2xl px-3.5 py-2.5 sm:px-5 sm:py-4 text-white font-bold focus:border-emerald-500 outline-none transition-all text-xs sm:text-sm"
            />
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5 sm:gap-2">
              <button onClick={() => handleAllocate('allocate')} className="bg-emerald-600 text-white px-4 py-1.5 sm:px-6 sm:py-2 rounded-lg sm:rounded-xl text-[9px] sm:text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-widest hover:bg-emerald-500 transition-colors">Deposit</button>
              <button onClick={() => handleAllocate('deallocate')} className="bg-slate-800 text-white px-4 py-1.5 sm:px-6 sm:py-2 rounded-lg sm:rounded-xl text-[9px] sm:text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-widest hover:bg-slate-700 transition-colors">Refund</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Proposal Logic */}
      {status === 'proposing' && proposal && !showPinEntry && (
        <div className="mb-4 sm:mb-6 p-5 sm:p-8 bg-emerald-600 rounded-2xl sm:rounded-[3rem] text-white shadow-lg shadow-emerald-900/40 animate-in zoom-in-95 duration-300 relative overflow-hidden">
          <div className="absolute top-0 right-0 -mr-10 -mt-10 w-28 h-28 sm:w-40 sm:h-40 bg-white/10 rounded-full blur-2xl pointer-events-none" />
          <h3 className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-black uppercase tracking-[0.2em] mb-4 sm:mb-6 opacity-80">{t('Wali Proposal', 'ولی کی تجویز')}</h3>
          
          <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4 sm:mb-6">
            <div>
              <p className="text-2xl sm:text-4xl font-black tracking-tighter">{proposal.symbol}</p>
              <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold opacity-70">{proposal.name}</p>
            </div>
            <div className="text-right flex-shrink-0">
              <p className="text-[10px] sm:text-xs font-bold uppercase opacity-60 mb-0.5 sm:mb-1">{t('Halal Score', 'حلال اسکور')}</p>
              <div className="bg-white/20 px-3 py-1 sm:px-4 sm:py-1 rounded-full text-base sm:text-xl font-black">{proposal.halalScore}</div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-3 sm:space-y-4 mb-4 sm:mb-6">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 sm:gap-3 bg-white/10 p-3 sm:p-4 rounded-xl sm:rounded-2xl border border-white/10">
              <span className="text-xl sm:text-2xl flex-shrink-0">⚖️</span>
              <p className="text-[11px] sm:text-xs leading-relaxed font-medium">
                {t('Shariah compliance verified. Debt-to-Equity is ', 'شریعت کی تعمیل کی تصدیق ہو گئی۔ قرض اور ایکویٹی کا تناسب ')}
                <span className="font-bold underline">{proposal.debtToEquity}%</span>
              </p>
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 sm:gap-3 bg-emerald-400/20 p-3 sm:p-4 rounded-xl sm:rounded-2xl border border-white/20">
              <span className="text-xl sm:text-2xl flex-shrink-0">🇵🇰</span>
              <div>
                <p className="text-[10px] sm:text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest">{t('Sovereign GDP Impact', 'قومی جی ڈی پی پر اثر')}</p>
                <p className="text-[9px] sm:text-[10px] opacity-80 leading-relaxed mt-0.5">
                  {t(`This investment supports approximately ${proposal.jobsCreated || 50} jobs in Pakistan's economy.`, `یہ سرمایہ کاری پاکستان کی معیشت میں تقریباً ${proposal.jobsCreated || 50} ملازمتوں کا باعث بنتی ہے۔`)}
                </p>
                <div className="mt-2 w-full bg-white/20 h-1 sm:h-1.5 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                  <div className="bg-white h-full" style={{ width: `${(proposal.gdpImpact || 7) * 10}%` }} />
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex gap-3 sm:gap-4">
            <button 
              onClick={() => setShowPinEntry(true)}
              className="flex-1 bg-white text-emerald-700 py-3 sm:py-5 rounded-xl sm:rounded-[1.5rem] font-black text-[11px] sm:text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.2em] hover:scale-105 transition-all active:scale-95 shadow-lg shadow-emerald-900/20"
            >
              {t('Confirm & Invest', 'تصدیق کریں اور سرمایہ کاری کریں')}
            </button>
            <button 
              onClick={() => { setProposal(null); setStatus('idle'); }}
              className="px-5 sm:px-8 bg-black/20 text-white py-3 sm:py-5 rounded-xl sm:rounded-[1.5rem] font-black text-[11px] sm:text-xs uppercase hover:bg-black/40 transition-all active:scale-95"
            >
              {t('Reject', 'مسترد کریں')}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* PIN Entry */}
      {showPinEntry && (
        <div className="mb-4 sm:mb-6 p-6 sm:p-10 bg-white rounded-2xl sm:rounded-[3.5rem] text-slate-900 shadow-2xl animate-in fade-in slide-in-from-bottom-8 duration-300">
          <p className="text-center text-[9px] sm:text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-[0.2em] sm:tracking-[0.3em] text-slate-400 mb-4 sm:mb-8">{t('Authorize Transaction', 'لین دین کی تصدیق کریں')}</p>
          <input 
            type="password" 
            maxLength={4}
            value={pin}
            onChange={(e) => setPin(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="****"
            className="w-full text-center text-4xl sm:text-6xl font-black tracking-[1rem] sm:tracking-[1.5rem] py-4 sm:py-6 bg-slate-50 rounded-xl sm:rounded-[2rem] border-2 border-slate-100 focus:border-emerald-500 outline-none transition-all mb-4 sm:mb-8"
          />
          <button 
            onClick={confirmInvestment}
            className="w-full bg-emerald-600 text-white py-3 sm:py-6 rounded-xl sm:rounded-[2rem] font-black text-xs sm:text-sm uppercase tracking-widest shadow-lg shadow-emerald-900/40 hover:bg-emerald-500 transition-all active:scale-95"
          >
            {t('Verify Security PIN', 'سیکیورٹی پن کی تصدیق کریں')}
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Logs */}
      <div className="space-y-3 sm:space-y-4">
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center px-1 sm:px-2">
          <h3 className="text-[9px] sm:text-[10px] font-black text-slate-600 uppercase tracking-widest">{t('Guardian Log', 'محافظ لاگ')}</h3>
          <div className="flex gap-1 sm:gap-1.5">
            <div className={`w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full ${status === 'scanning' ? 'bg-emerald-500 animate-ping' : 'bg-slate-800'}`} />
            <div className={`w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full ${status === 'proposing' ? 'bg-amber-500 animate-pulse' : 'bg-slate-800'}`} />
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="space-y-2 sm:space-y-3 max-h-32 sm:max-h-40 overflow-y-auto pr-1 sm:pr-2 scrollbar-hide">
          {logs.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="text-slate-700 text-[9px] sm:text-[10px] italic font-medium p-4 sm:p-6 text-center border-2 border-dashed border-white/5 rounded-xl sm:rounded-[2rem]">
              {t('Awaiting sovereign instructions...', 'خود مختار ہدایات کا انتظار ہے...')}
            </p>
          ) : (
            logs.map((log, i) => (
              <div key={i} className="text-[10px] sm:text-[11px] bg-white/5 p-3.5 sm:p-5 rounded-xl sm:rounded-[1.8rem] border border-white/5 flex gap-3 sm:gap-4 items-start group hover:bg-white/10 transition-colors">
                <span className="text-slate-500 font-mono text-[8px] sm:text-[9px] mt-0.5 sm:mt-1">{log.time}</span>
                <p className="text-white/90 font-bold leading-relaxed tracking-tight">
                  {lang === 'ur' ? log.urdu : log.msg}
                </p>
              </div>
            ))
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* FILE: src\components\WirasatCalculator.tsx */
/* ================================================================ */

'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';

interface Heirs {
  wife: number;
  husband: boolean;
  father: boolean;
  mother: boolean;
  sons: number;
  daughters: number;
}

export default function WirasatCalculator({ portfolioValue = 2500000 }: { portfolioValue?: number }) {
  const [heirs, setHeirs] = useState<Heirs>({
    wife: 1,
    husband: false,
    father: true,
    mother: true,
    sons: 2,
    daughters: 1
  });
  
  const [debt, setDebt] = useState(0);
  const [wasiyyat, setWasiyyat] = useState(0); // Max 1/3
  const [calculated, setCalculated] = useState(false);

  const calculateShares = () => {
    const netWealth = portfolioValue - debt - wasiyyat;
    const shares: { name: string, ratio: string, amount: number }[] = [];

    let remaining = netWealth;

    // 1. Wife/Husband Shares
    if (heirs.husband) {
      const share = netWealth * (1/4); // 1/4 if children exist
      shares.push({ name: 'Husband', ratio: '1/4', amount: share });
      remaining -= share;
    } else if (heirs.wife > 0) {
      const share = netWealth * (1/8); // 1/8 if children exist
      shares.push({ name: `Wife (${heirs.wife})`, ratio: '1/8', amount: share });
      remaining -= share;
    }

    // 2. Parents
    if (heirs.father) {
      const share = netWealth * (1/6);
      shares.push({ name: 'Father', ratio: '1/6', amount: share });
      remaining -= share;
    }
    if (heirs.mother) {
      const share = netWealth * (1/6);
      shares.push({ name: 'Mother', ratio: '1/6', amount: share });
      remaining -= share;
    }

    // 3. Children (Residue)
    const totalParts = (heirs.sons * 2) + heirs.daughters;
    if (totalParts > 0) {
      const perPart = remaining / totalParts;
      if (heirs.sons > 0) {
        shares.push({ name: `Sons (${heirs.sons})`, ratio: 'Residue (2x)', amount: perPart * 2 * heirs.sons });
      }
      if (heirs.daughters > 0) {
        shares.push({ name: `Daughters (${heirs.daughters})`, ratio: 'Residue (1x)', amount: perPart * heirs.daughters });
      }
    }

    return shares;
  };

  const results = calculated ? calculateShares() : [];

  return (
    <div className="premium-card p-0 overflow-hidden group rounded-2xl sm:rounded-[3rem] bg-white border border-slate-100 shadow-xl relative mb-4 sm:mb-6 animate-in fade-in duration-500">
      <div className="bg-slate-950 p-4 sm:p-10 text-white relative border-b border-white/5">
        <div className="absolute top-0 right-0 w-24 h-24 sm:w-32 sm:h-32 bg-emerald-500/10 rounded-full blur-[50px] sm:blur-[80px] pointer-events-none"></div>
        
        <div className="relative z-10">
          <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row justify-between items-start sm:items-center gap-3 sm:gap-0 mb-4 sm:mb-8">
            <div>
              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 sm:gap-2 mb-2 sm:mb-4">
                <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 sm:gap-2 bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-400 text-[7px] sm:text-[9px] font-black uppercase tracking-[0.2em] sm:tracking-[0.3em] px-3 py-1.5 sm:px-4 sm:py-2 rounded-full border border-emerald-500/30">
                  <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 bg-emerald-400 rounded-full animate-pulse"></span>
                  Shariah Legacy Engine
                </div>
                <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 sm:gap-2 bg-slate-800 text-slate-400 text-[7px] sm:text-[9px] font-black uppercase tracking-[0.2em] sm:tracking-[0.3em] px-3 py-1.5 sm:px-4 sm:py-2 rounded-full border border-white/5">
                  Free Service
                </div>
              </div>
              <h2 className="text-xl sm:text-4xl font-black tracking-tighter italic text-white">SOVEREIGN <span className="text-emerald-500">WIRASAT</span></h2>
              <p className="text-slate-500 text-[8px] sm:text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-widest mt-0.5 sm:mt-2">Algorithmic Inheritance Distribution</p>
            </div>
            <div className="w-10 h-10 sm:w-16 sm:h-16 bg-white/5 rounded-xl sm:rounded-3xl flex items-center justify-center text-xl sm:text-4xl border border-white/10 shadow-lg flex-shrink-0">⚖️</div>
          </div>

          {!calculated ? (
            <div className="space-y-4 sm:space-y-6 animate-in fade-in duration-300">
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 gap-3 sm:gap-4 items-end">
                <div className="sm:col-span-2 md:col-span-3">
                  <label className="text-[8px] sm:text-[9px] font-black text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-1 sm:mb-2 block">Deductions (PKR)</label>
                  <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-2 sm:gap-3">
                    <input 
                      type="number" 
                      placeholder="Outstanding Debts"
                      className="w-full sm:w-1/2 p-3 sm:p-4 bg-white/5 border border-white/5 rounded-xl sm:rounded-2xl text-xs font-bold text-white focus:bg-white/10 outline-none focus:border-emerald-500 transition-all"
                      onChange={(e) => setDebt(Number(e.target.value))}
                    />
                    <input 
                      type="number" 
                      placeholder="Wasiyyat (Max 1/3)"
                      className="w-full sm:w-1/2 p-3 sm:p-4 bg-white/5 border border-white/5 rounded-xl sm:rounded-2xl text-xs font-bold text-white focus:bg-white/10 outline-none focus:border-emerald-500 transition-all"
                      onChange={(e) => setWasiyyat(Number(e.target.value))}
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="text-[8px] sm:text-[9px] font-black text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-1 sm:mb-2 block">Primary Spouse</label>
                  <select 
                    className="w-full p-3 sm:p-4 bg-white/5 border border-white/5 rounded-xl sm:rounded-2xl text-xs font-bold text-white outline-none focus:border-emerald-500 transition-all"
                    onChange={(e) => setHeirs({ ...heirs, wife: e.target.value === 'wife' ? 1 : 0, husband: e.target.value === 'husband' })}
                  >
                    <option value="wife" className="text-black">Wife Alive</option>
                    <option value="husband" className="text-black">Husband Alive</option>
                    <option value="none" className="text-black">None</option>
                  </select>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="text-[8px] sm:text-[9px] font-black text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-1 sm:mb-2 block">Parents</label>
                  <select 
                    className="w-full p-3 sm:p-4 bg-white/5 border border-white/5 rounded-xl sm:rounded-2xl text-xs font-bold text-white outline-none focus:border-emerald-500 transition-all"
                    onChange={(e) => setHeirs({ ...heirs, father: e.target.value !== 'mother', mother: e.target.value !== 'father' })}
                  >
                    <option value="both" className="text-black">Both Alive</option>
                    <option value="father" className="text-black">Father Only</option>
                    <option value="mother" className="text-black">Mother Only</option>
                    <option value="none" className="text-black">None</option>
                  </select>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="text-[8px] sm:text-[9px] font-black text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-1 sm:mb-2 block">No. of Sons</label>
                  <input 
                    type="number" 
                    value={heirs.sons}
                    className="w-full p-3 sm:p-4 bg-white/5 border border-white/5 rounded-xl sm:rounded-2xl text-xs font-bold text-white outline-none focus:border-emerald-500 transition-all"
                    onChange={(e) => setHeirs({ ...heirs, sons: Number(e.target.value) })}
                  />
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="text-[8px] sm:text-[9px] font-black text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-1 sm:mb-2 block">No. of Daughters</label>
                  <input 
                    type="number" 
                    value={heirs.daughters}
                    className="w-full p-3 sm:p-4 bg-white/5 border border-white/5 rounded-xl sm:rounded-2xl text-xs font-bold text-white outline-none focus:border-emerald-500 transition-all"
                    onChange={(e) => setHeirs({ ...heirs, daughters: Number(e.target.value) })}
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <button 
                onClick={() => setCalculated(true)}
                className="w-full py-3 sm:py-5 bg-emerald-600 text-white font-black rounded-xl sm:rounded-2xl shadow-lg shadow-emerald-900/30 hover:bg-emerald-500 transition-all text-xs uppercase tracking-widest active:scale-95 mt-2"
              >
                Generate Legal Shariah Will
              </button>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-4 sm:space-y-6 animate-in fade-in slide-in-from-bottom-4 duration-300">
              <div className="p-4 sm:p-6 bg-white/5 rounded-xl sm:rounded-[2rem] border border-white/5 text-center shadow-sm">
                <p className="text-[8px] sm:text-[9px] font-black text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-0.5 sm:mb-1 italic">Net Distributable Estate</p>
                <p className="text-xl sm:text-3xl font-black text-white italic tracking-tight">PKR {(portfolioValue - debt - wasiyyat).toLocaleString()}</p>
              </div>

              {/* Responsive 2-Column Grid on Desktop */}
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:gap-4 items-start">
                {results.map((res, i) => (
                  <div key={i} className="flex justify-between items-center p-3.5 sm:p-5 bg-white/5 rounded-xl sm:rounded-2xl border border-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 transition-all shadow-sm">
                    <div>
                      <p className="font-black text-white text-xs sm:text-sm tracking-tight">{res.name}</p>
                      <p className="text-[8px] sm:text-[9px] font-bold text-emerald-400 uppercase tracking-widest mt-0.5">{res.ratio} Share</p>
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-right flex-shrink-0">
                      <p className="font-black text-white text-xs sm:text-sm">PKR {res.amount.toLocaleString(undefined, { maximumFractionDigits: 0 })}</p>
                      <button className="text-[7px] font-black text-emerald-400 uppercase tracking-tighter mt-1 bg-emerald-500/10 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full border border-emerald-500/20 active:scale-95 transition-all">Link CNIC</button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>

              <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-2 sm:gap-4 mt-4 sm:mt-6 pt-2">
                <button 
                  onClick={() => setCalculated(false)}
                  className="w-full sm:flex-1 py-3 sm:py-5 bg-white/5 text-slate-400 font-black text-[9px] sm:text-[10px] uppercase rounded-xl sm:rounded-2xl border border-white/5 active:scale-95 transition-all text-center"
                >
                  Edit Family
                </button>
                <button 
                  className="w-full sm:flex-[2] py-3 sm:py-5 bg-white text-slate-900 font-black text-[9px] sm:text-[10px] uppercase rounded-xl sm:rounded-2xl shadow-lg active:scale-95 transition-all text-center"
                >
                  Lock Heir CNICs 🔒
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* FILE: src\components\WithdrawFlow.tsx */
/* ================================================================ */

'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';

export default function WithdrawFlow({ onSuccess }: { onSuccess: (data: any) => void }) {
  const [step, setStep] = useState(1);
  const [amount, setAmount] = useState('');
  const [method, setMethod] = useState('');
  const [pin, setPin] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  const methods = [
    { id: 'jazzcash', name: 'JazzCash', icon: '📱' },
    { id: 'easypaisa', name: 'EasyPaisa', icon: '🟢' },
    { id: 'bank', name: 'Bank Account', icon: '🏦' }
  ];

  const validate = () => {
    setError('');
    if (!amount || Number(amount) < 100) return setError('کم از کم PKR 100 درج کریں');
    if (!method) return setError('رقم وصولی کا طریقہ منتخب کریں');
    if (pin.length !== 4 || !/^\d{4}$/.test(pin)) return setError('4 ہندسوں کا درست PIN درج کریں');
    return true;
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!validate()) return;
    setLoading(true);
    await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 1200));
    onSuccess({ type: 'withdraw', amount: Number(amount), method, pin: '****' });
    setLoading(false);
  };

  return (
    <div className="w-full max-w-sm mx-auto p-6 bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-xl border border-gray-100">
      <h2 className="text-xl font-bold mb-5 text-gray-800">💸 وِڈڈرا کریں</h2>
      {step === 1 && (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">رقم (PKR)</label>
          <input type="number" value={amount} onChange={(e) => setAmount(e.target.value)} placeholder="e.g. 2000" className="w-full p-3 border rounded-xl focus:ring-2 focus:ring-green-500 outline-none" />
          <button onClick={() => setStep(2)} className="w-full py-3 bg-green-600 text-white rounded-xl hover:bg-green-700 font-medium">آگے بڑھیں</button>
        </div>
      )}
      {step === 2 && (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">رقم وصولی کا طریقہ</label>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-3">
            {methods.map((m) => (
              <button key={m.id} onClick={() => setMethod(m.id)} className={`p-3 border rounded-xl text-center transition ${method === m.id ? 'border-green-500 bg-green-50 ring-2 ring-green-200' : 'hover:bg-gray-50'}`}>
                <div className="text-2xl mb-1">{m.icon}</div><div className="text-xs font-medium">{m.name}</div>
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <button onClick={() => setStep(1)} className="flex-1 py-3 border rounded-xl hover:bg-gray-50">واپس</button>
            <button onClick={() => setStep(3)} className="flex-1 py-3 bg-green-600 text-white rounded-xl hover:bg-green-700">آگے بڑھیں</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
      {step === 3 && (
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
          <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">4 ہندسوں کا PIN</label>
          <input type="password" inputMode="numeric" maxLength={4} value={pin} onChange={(e) => setPin(e.target.value.replace(/\D/g, ''))} placeholder="****" className="w-full p-3 border rounded-xl text-center text-xl tracking-widest focus:ring-2 focus:ring-green-500 outline-none" />
          {error && <p className="text-red-500 text-sm text-center">{error}</p>}
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <button type="button" onClick={() => setStep(2)} className="flex-1 py-3 border rounded-xl hover:bg-gray-50">واپس</button>
            <button type="submit" disabled={loading} className="flex-1 py-3 bg-green-600 text-white rounded-xl hover:bg-green-700 disabled:opacity-50 font-medium">{loading ? 'پروسیس...' : 'تصدیق کریں'}</button>
          </div>
        </form>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}



/* ================================================================ */
/* FILE: src\components\ZakatCalculator.tsx */
/* ================================================================ */

'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';

interface ZakatCalculatorProps {
  balance: number;
  portfolioValue: number;
  onDonate: (amount: number, recipient: string) => void;
}

const NISAB_THRESHOLD = 300000; // Mock Gold Nisab value (approx 87g gold)

export default function ZakatCalculator({ balance, portfolioValue, onDonate }: ZakatCalculatorProps) {
  const totalWealth = balance + portfolioValue;
  const zakatAmount = totalWealth * 0.025;
  const isEligible = totalWealth >= NISAB_THRESHOLD;

  const [modalOpen, setModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [selectedRecipient, setSelectedRecipient] = useState('');
  const [pin, setPin] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [donated, setDonated] = useState(false);

  const recipients = [
    { name: 'Edhi Foundation', icon: '🚑', focus: 'Emergency' },
    { name: 'Al-Khidmat', icon: '🤲', focus: 'Social' },
    { name: 'TCF (Education)', icon: '📚', focus: 'Education' },
    { name: 'Indus Hospital', icon: '🏥', focus: 'Health' },
  ];

  const handleDonate = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (pin.length !== 4) return;
    
    setLoading(true);
    await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 2000)); // Simulate processing
    onDonate(zakatAmount, selectedRecipient);
    setLoading(false);
    setDonated(true);
  };

  if (donated) {
    return (
      <div className="premium-card p-10 text-center animate-in zoom-in duration-500 overflow-hidden relative">
        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-br from-emerald-50 to-transparent pointer-events-none"></div>
        <div className="relative z-10">
          <div className="w-20 h-20 bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-600 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-4xl mx-auto mb-6 shadow-sm animate-bounce">🤲</div>
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-black text-emerald-800 tracking-tight">JazakAllah Khair</h2>
          <p className="text-slate-500 font-medium mt-2 max-w-[240px] mx-auto">Your purification of wealth is complete. May your wealth be blessed and multiplied.</p>
          <div className="mt-8 pt-8 border-t border-emerald-100">
            <button 
              onClick={() => { setDonated(false); setModalOpen(false); }} 
              className="px-8 py-3 bg-emerald-600 text-white rounded-2xl font-black text-sm hover:bg-emerald-700 transition-all active:scale-95 shadow-lg shadow-emerald-900/10"
            >
              Back to Portfolio
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <>
      <div className="premium-card p-8 overflow-hidden relative group">
        {/* Background Aura */}
        <div className={`absolute -top-12 -right-12 w-48 h-48 rounded-full blur-[80px] transition-all duration-1000 ${
          isEligible ? 'bg-amber-100 opacity-60' : 'bg-slate-100 opacity-40'
        }`}></div>

        <div className="relative z-10">
          <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-8">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
              <div className="w-14 h-14 bg-slate-50 text-slate-400 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center text-3xl shadow-inner border border-slate-100">🤲</div>
              <div>
                <h2 className="text-xl font-black text-slate-800 tracking-tight">Zakat OS</h2>
                <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest mt-1">Nisab Tracker & Distribution</p>
              </div>
            </div>
            {isEligible ? (
              <span className="text-[10px] font-black text-amber-600 bg-amber-50 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full border border-amber-200 uppercase tracking-widest animate-pulse">Liability Due</span>
            ) : (
              <span className="text-[10px] font-black text-slate-400 bg-slate-50 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full border border-slate-200 uppercase tracking-widest">Below Nisab</span>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-4 mb-8">
            <div className="flex justify-between text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest">
              <span>Nisab Progress</span>
              <span>{( (totalWealth / NISAB_THRESHOLD) * 100 ).toFixed(0)}%</span>
            </div>
            <div className="w-full bg-slate-50 rounded-full h-3 p-0.5 overflow-hidden border border-slate-100 shadow-inner">
              <div className={`h-full rounded-full transition-all duration-1000 ${
                isEligible ? 'bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 to-emerald-400' : 'bg-slate-200'
              }`} style={{ width: `${Math.min((totalWealth / NISAB_THRESHOLD) * 100, 100)}%` }}></div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {isEligible ? (
            <div className="p-6 bg-amber-50 rounded-[2.5rem] border border-amber-100 relative overflow-hidden">
              <div className="relative z-10">
                <div className="text-[10px] font-black text-amber-700 uppercase tracking-widest mb-1">Total Zakat Payable</div>
                <div className="text-3xl font-black text-amber-800 tracking-tighter">PKR {zakatAmount.toLocaleString(undefined, { maximumFractionDigits: 0 })}</div>
                <button 
                  onClick={() => setModalOpen(true)}
                  className="mt-6 w-full py-4 bg-amber-600 text-white font-black rounded-2xl hover:bg-amber-700 transition-all active:scale-95 shadow-xl shadow-amber-900/10"
                >
                  Fulfill Duty Now
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="p-6 bg-slate-50 rounded-[2.5rem] border border-slate-100 text-center">
              <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest leading-relaxed">
                Nisab threshold: PKR {NISAB_THRESHOLD.toLocaleString()}<br />
                Your wealth is currently safe from liability.
              </p>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Modal - Noble Design */}
      {modalOpen && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-md flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-0 sm:p-4 z-[70] animate-in fade-in">
          <div className="w-full max-w-md bg-white rounded-t-[3rem] sm:rounded-[3rem] p-8 shadow-2xl animate-in slide-in-from-bottom-full duration-500 relative">
            <button onClick={() => setModalOpen(false)} className="absolute top-8 right-8 text-slate-300 hover:text-slate-500 transition-colors text-2xl font-light">✕</button>
            
            <div className="mb-8">
              <h3 className="text-2xl font-black text-slate-800 tracking-tight">Select Recipient</h3>
              <p className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest mt-1">Purify wealth through impact</p>
            </div>
            
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 mb-8">
              {recipients.map(r => (
                <button
                  key={r.name}
                  onClick={() => setSelectedRecipient(r.name)}
                  className={`p-4 border rounded-[2rem] text-left transition-all duration-300 ${
                    selectedRecipient === r.name 
                      ? 'border-amber-500 bg-amber-50 shadow-xl shadow-amber-900/5 ring-2 ring-amber-200' 
                      : 'border-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-50'
                  }`}
                >
                  <div className="text-2xl mb-2">{r.icon}</div>
                  <div className="font-black text-slate-800 text-xs tracking-tight">{r.name}</div>
                  <div className="text-[9px] font-black text-slate-400 uppercase mt-0.5">{r.focus}</div>
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>

            {selectedRecipient && (
              <form onSubmit={handleDonate} className="space-y-6 animate-in slide-in-from-top-4">
                <div className="p-4 bg-emerald-50 rounded-2xl text-emerald-800 text-[10px] font-bold text-center uppercase tracking-widest leading-relaxed">
                  Impact: Your PKR {zakatAmount.toLocaleString()} will provide meals, clean water, and education to those in need.
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="text-[10px] font-black text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2 block px-1">Security PIN</label>
                  <input
                    type="password"
                    inputMode="numeric"
                    maxLength={4}
                    value={pin}
                    onChange={(e) => setPin(e.target.value.replace(/\D/g, ''))}
                    placeholder="••••"
                    className="w-full p-4 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-100 rounded-2xl text-center text-3xl font-black tracking-[1em] focus:bg-white focus:border-amber-500 outline-none transition-all"
                  />
                </div>
                <button 
                  type="submit" 
                  disabled={loading || pin.length !== 4} 
                  className="w-full py-5 bg-slate-900 text-white rounded-[2rem] hover:bg-slate-800 disabled:opacity-50 font-black text-lg transition-all shadow-2xl shadow-slate-900/20 active:scale-95"
                >
                  {loading ? 'Fulfilling Duty...' : `Confirm PKR ${zakatAmount.toLocaleString()}`}
                </button>
              </form>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </>
  );
}

/* FILE: src\components\ZakatDistribution.tsx */
/* ================================================================ */

'use client';
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';

const charities = [
  { id: 0, name: 'Roziq Direct', desc: 'Direct-to-family verified distribution via CNIC.', logo: '🇵🇰', verified: true },
  { id: 1, name: 'Edhi Foundation', desc: 'Largest ambulance network and social welfare.', logo: '🚑', verified: true },
  { id: 2, name: 'Shaukat Khanum', desc: 'Cancer hospital providing free treatment.', logo: '🏥', verified: true },
  { id: 3, name: 'Saylani Trust', desc: 'Feeding thousands and vocational training.', logo: '🍲', verified: true },
  { id: 4, name: 'Akhuwat', desc: 'Interest-free microfinance for the poor.', logo: '🤝', verified: true },
];

export default function ZakatDistribution({ balance, portfolioValue }: { balance: number, portfolioValue: number }) {
  const totalWealth = balance + portfolioValue;
  const nisab = 300000;
  const zakatDue = totalWealth >= nisab ? totalWealth * 0.025 : 0;
  
  const [selectedCharity, setSelectedCharity] = useState<any>(null);
  const [isDistributing, setIsDistributing] = useState(false);
  const [success, setSuccess] = useState(false);
  const [vaultData, setVaultData] = useState<any>(null);

  // Fetch National Vault Data
  useEffect(() => {
    fetch('/api/zakat/vault').then(r => r.json()).then(setVaultData);
  }, [success]);

  const handleDistribute = async () => {
    setIsDistributing(true);
    if (selectedCharity.id === 0) {
      // National Vault Logic
      const res = await fetch('/api/zakat/vault', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ amount: zakatDue })
      });
      if (res.ok) {
        setSuccess(true);
        window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('balanceUpdate'));
      }
    } else {
      // Direct NGO Logic
      await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 2000));
      setSuccess(true);
    }
    setIsDistributing(false);
  };

  return (
    <div className="premium-card p-0 bg-slate-950 border border-emerald-500/10 text-white relative overflow-hidden rounded-2xl sm:rounded-[3rem]">
      
      {/* National Vault Header */}
      <div className="p-4 sm:p-8 bg-gradient-to-br from-emerald-900/40 to-slate-900 border-b border-white/5">
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-3 sm:mb-6">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 sm:gap-3">
            <div className="w-10 h-10 sm:w-12 sm:h-12 bg-emerald-500 rounded-xl sm:rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center text-xl sm:text-2xl shadow-lg shadow-emerald-500/20 flex-shrink-0">🏛️</div>
            <div>
              <h3 className="text-base sm:text-xl font-black tracking-tight">National Zakat Vault</h3>
              <p className="text-[8px] sm:text-[10px] font-bold text-emerald-500 uppercase tracking-widest">Public Sovereignty Fund</p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="text-right flex-shrink-0">
            <p className="text-[8px] sm:text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase mb-0.5 sm:mb-1">Total Collected</p>
            <p className="text-lg sm:text-2xl font-black text-white tracking-tight">
              <span className="text-[10px] sm:text-xs text-emerald-500 mr-1">PKR</span>
              {vaultData?.totalVault?.toLocaleString() || '4,580,250'}
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="p-4 sm:p-8 space-y-4 sm:space-y-8">
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:gap-4">
          <div className="bg-white/5 p-3.5 sm:p-5 rounded-2xl sm:rounded-3xl border border-white/5">
            <div className="text-[9px] sm:text-[10px] font-bold text-emerald-400 uppercase mb-1">Your Zakat Due</div>
            <div className="text-lg sm:text-2xl font-black tracking-tighter">PKR {zakatDue.toLocaleString()}</div>
          </div>
          <div className="bg-white/5 p-3.5 sm:p-5 rounded-2xl sm:rounded-3xl border border-white/5">
            <div className="text-[9px] sm:text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase mb-1">Status</div>
            <div className="text-[11px] sm:text-xs font-black flex items-center gap-1.5 sm:gap-2 mt-1 sm:mt-2">
              <span className={`w-2 h-2 rounded-full ${zakatDue > 0 ? 'bg-amber-500 animate-pulse' : 'bg-emerald-500'}`} />
              {zakatDue > 0 ? 'Action Required' : 'Purified'}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {zakatDue > 0 && !success ? (
          <div className="space-y-4 sm:space-y-6">
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-2.5 sm:gap-3">
              {charities.slice(0, 2).map(c => (
                <button
                  key={c.id}
                  onClick={() => setSelectedCharity(c)}
                  className={`p-3.5 sm:p-5 rounded-2xl sm:rounded-[2rem] border transition-all flex items-center gap-3 sm:gap-4 group ${
                    selectedCharity?.id === c.id 
                      ? 'bg-emerald-600 border-emerald-400 shadow-xl' 
                      : 'bg-white/5 border-white/5 hover:bg-white/10'
                  }`}
                >
                  <div className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl flex-shrink-0">{c.logo}</div>
                  <div className="text-left">
                    <div className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-black">{c.name}</div>
                    <p className="text-[9px] sm:text-[10px] opacity-60 font-medium">{c.desc}</p>
                  </div>
                  {c.id === 0 ? (
                    <div className="ml-auto bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300 px-2 sm:px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-[7px] sm:text-[8px] font-black uppercase flex-shrink-0">0.5% Fee</div>
                  ) : (
                    <div className="ml-auto bg-white/10 px-2 sm:px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-[7px] sm:text-[8px] font-black uppercase flex-shrink-0">0% Fee</div>
                  )}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>

            {selectedCharity && (
              <button
                onClick={handleDistribute}
                disabled={isDistributing}
                className="w-full py-3 sm:py-5 bg-white text-slate-900 rounded-xl sm:rounded-[2rem] font-black text-[11px] sm:text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.2em] shadow-lg hover:scale-[1.02] active:scale-95 transition-all"
              >
                {isDistributing ? 'Authorizing Ledger...' : `Authorize PKR ${zakatDue.toLocaleString()} Payment`}
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        ) : success ? (
          <div className="bg-emerald-500/10 border border-emerald-500/20 p-5 sm:p-8 rounded-2xl sm:rounded-[2.5rem] text-center animate-in zoom-in-95">
            <div className="text-3xl sm:text-5xl mb-2 sm:mb-4">✨</div>
            <h4 className="text-base sm:text-xl font-black text-emerald-400 mb-1 sm:mb-2">Wealth Purified</h4>
            <p className="text-[11px] sm:text-xs text-emerald-100/60 leading-relaxed">
              Your contribution is now in the **National Vault**. You will receive notifications as it is spent on verified impact projects.
            </p>
          </div>
        ) : null}

        {/* Impact Feed */}
        <div className="space-y-3 sm:space-y-4">
          <h4 className="text-[9px] sm:text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-500 px-1">Personal Impact Tracking</h4>
          <div className="space-y-2.5 sm:space-y-3">
            {vaultData?.userContributions?.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="p-5 sm:p-8 text-center border-2 border-dashed border-white/5 rounded-2xl sm:rounded-[2.5rem]">
                <p className="text-[9px] sm:text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-600 italic">No contributions yet. Purify wealth to see live impact.</p>
              </div>
            ) : (
              vaultData?.userContributions?.map((c: any) => (
                <div key={c.id} className="bg-white/5 p-4 sm:p-5 rounded-2xl sm:rounded-[2rem] border border-white/5">
                  <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-2 sm:mb-3">
                    <span className="text-[9px] sm:text-[10px] font-black text-emerald-500 uppercase tracking-widest">{c.status}</span>
                    <span className="text-[9px] sm:text-[10px] font-mono text-slate-500">{new Date(c.date).toLocaleDateString()}</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex justify-between items-end">
                    <div>
                      <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-black text-white">PKR {c.amount.toLocaleString()}</p>
                      <p className="text-[9px] sm:text-[10px] text-slate-500">Awaiting national distribution...</p>
                    </div>
                    <div className="w-8 h-8 sm:w-10 sm:h-10 bg-emerald-500/10 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center text-emerald-500 flex-shrink-0">
                      🔒
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

  'general.confirm': { en: 'Confirm', ur: 'تصدیق کریں' },
  'general.back': { en: 'Back', ur: 'واپس' },

const LangContext = createContext<LangContextType>({
  lang: 'en',
  setLang: () => {},
  t: (k) => k,
});

export function LangProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [lang, setLangState] = useState<Lang>('en');

  useEffect(() => {
    const saved = localStorage.getItem('roziq_lang') as Lang;
    if (saved) setLangState(saved);
  }, []);

  const setLang = (l: Lang) => {
    localStorage.setItem('roziq_lang', l);
    setLangState(l);
  };

  const t = (key: string): string => {
    return translations[key]?.[lang] ?? key;
  };

  return (
    <LangContext.Provider value={{ lang, setLang, t }}>
      <div dir={lang === 'ur' ? 'rtl' : 'ltr'} className={lang === 'ur' ? 'font-[system-ui]' : ''}>
        {children}
      </div>
    </LangContext.Provider>
  );
}

export const useLang = () => useContext(LangContext);

/* FILE: src\lib\db.ts */
/* ================================================================ */

import type { DbSchema, Transaction, Holding } from './types';
import fs from 'fs';
import path from 'path';

const DATA_DIR = path.join(process.cwd(), 'data');
const DB_PATH = path.join(DATA_DIR, 'db.json');

const DEFAULT_DB: DbSchema = {
  balance: 5000000,
  waliBalance: 0,
  globalZakatVault: 4580250,
  transactions: [
    {
      id: 1,
      type: 'deposit',
      amount: 250000,
      method: 'JazzCash',
      status: 'success',
      date: new Date().toISOString(),
    },
  ],
  holdings: [],
  zakatHistory: [],
  isPremium: false,
  waliSubscriptionExpiry: null,
  totalInvestedHistory: 150000000,
  totalRealizedProfit: 200000000,
  userZakatContributions: [],
};

function ensureDataDir(): void {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') {
    if (!fs.existsSync(DATA_DIR)) {
      fs.mkdirSync(DATA_DIR, { recursive: true });
    }
  }
}

export function getDb(): DbSchema {
  if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
    try {
      const raw = localStorage.getItem('roziq_db');
      if (!raw) return { ...DEFAULT_DB };
      return { ...DEFAULT_DB, ...JSON.parse(raw) };
    } catch {
      return { ...DEFAULT_DB };
    }
  }

  // Server-side (Node.js API routes)
  try {
    ensureDataDir();
    if (!fs.existsSync(DB_PATH)) {
      saveDb(DEFAULT_DB);
      return { ...DEFAULT_DB };
    }
    const raw = fs.readFileSync(DB_PATH, 'utf8');
    return { ...DEFAULT_DB, ...JSON.parse(raw) };
  } catch {
    return { ...DEFAULT_DB };
  }
}

export function saveDb(db: DbSchema): void {
  if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
    try {
      localStorage.setItem('roziq_db', JSON.stringify(db));
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn('Failed to save to localStorage:', err);
    }
    return;
  }

  // Server-side (Node.js API routes)
  try {
    ensureDataDir();
    fs.writeFileSync(DB_PATH, JSON.stringify(db, null, 2), 'utf8');
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('Failed to save to DB_PATH:', err);
  }
}

export type { DbSchema, Transaction, Holding };

export function getHalalStocks() {
  return MOCK_PSX_STOCKS.filter(s => s.debtToEquity < 33 && s.interestIncomeRatio < 5 && s.halalScore >= 6);
}
export function getStockBySymbol(symbol: string) {
  return MOCK_PSX_STOCKS.find(s => s.symbol === symbol);
}
export function getStocksBySector(sector: string) {
  return MOCK_PSX_STOCKS.filter(s => s.sector === sector);
}
export function getAllSectors(): string[] {
  return [...new Set(MOCK_PSX_STOCKS.map(s => s.sector))];
}
export async function fetchLivePrice(symbol: string) {
  try {
    const formatted = symbol.endsWith('.KA') ? symbol : `${symbol}.KA`;
    const res = await fetch(`/api/psx?symbol=${formatted}`);
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Failed');
    return await res.json();
  } catch { return null; }
}

// src/lib/payment-sandbox.ts

export interface SandboxRequest {
  orderId: string;
  amount: number;
  method: 'jazzcash' | 'easypaisa' | 'bank';
  pin: string; // In real life, this goes directly to the bank, never to your server
}

export interface SandboxResponse {
  success: boolean;
  message: string;
  bankReference: string;
  transactionId: string;
}

// Simulates the external Bank API
export async function processSandboxPayment(data: SandboxRequest): Promise<SandboxResponse> {
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    console.log(`[SANDBOX] Processing ${data.method} request for PKR ${data.amount}...`);

    // Simulate network delay (3 seconds)
    setTimeout(() => {
      // 90% Success rate for demo, 10% Failure to show error handling
      const isSuccess = Math.random() > 0.1;

      if (isSuccess) {
        resolve({
          success: true,
          message: 'Transaction Successful',
          bankReference: `REF-${Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000000)}`,
          transactionId: `TXN-${Date.now()}`
        });
      } else {
        resolve({
          success: false,
          message: 'Transaction Failed: Insufficient Balance or Invalid PIN',
          bankReference: '',
          transactionId: ''
        });
      }
    }, 3000);
  });
}

/* FILE: src\utils\formatters.ts */
/* ================================================================ */

/**
 * ==============================================================================
 * Centralized Formatting Utilities
 * Standardized functions to format currency, percentages, dates, and CNIC numbers.
 * ==============================================================================
 */

/**
 * Format a number as Pakistani Rupee (e.g. 1500000 -> "₨ 1,500,000" or "PKR 1,500,000")
 */
export function formatPKR(amount: number | string | undefined | null, includeSymbol: boolean = true): string {
  const num = typeof amount === 'string' ? parseFloat(amount) : (amount ?? 0);
  if (isNaN(num)) return includeSymbol ? '₨ 0' : '0';
  
  const formatted = new Intl.NumberFormat('en-PK', {
    maximumFractionDigits: 2,
    minimumFractionDigits: 0,
  }).format(num);

  return includeSymbol ? `₨ ${formatted}` : formatted;
}

/**
 * Format compact numbers for charts / mobile cards (e.g. 1,500,000 -> "1.5M")
 */
export function formatCompact(amount: number): string {
  if (Math.abs(amount) >= 1_000_000) {
    return `${(amount / 1_000_000).toFixed(2)}M`;
  }
  if (Math.abs(amount) >= 1_000) {
    return `${(amount / 1_000).toFixed(1)}k`;
  }
  return amount.toLocaleString();
}

/**
 * Format percentage with + / - indicator
 */
export function formatPercentage(val: number, withSign: boolean = true): string {
  const sign = withSign && val > 0 ? '+' : '';
  return `${sign}${val.toFixed(2)}%`;
}

/**
 * Format Pakistani CNIC with dashes (e.g. 4210112345671 -> "42101-1234567-1")
 */
export function formatCNIC(cnic: string): string {
  const clean = cnic.replace(/\D/g, '');
  if (clean.length === 13) {
    return `${clean.slice(0, 5)}-${clean.slice(5, 12)}-${clean.slice(12)}`;
  }
  return cnic;
}

/**
 * Safely format ISO date strings into clean human-readable dates
 */
export function formatDate(dateString?: string): string {
  if (!dateString) return new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-PK');
  const d = new Date(dateString);
  return isNaN(d.getTime()) ? dateString : d.toLocaleDateString('en-PK', {
    year: 'numeric',
    month: 'short',
    day: 'numeric'
  });
}

/* FILE: src\utils\storage.ts */
/* ================================================================ */

/**
 * ==============================================================================
 * Safe LocalStorage Wrapper Utility
 * Prevents SSR crashes, handles JSON parse errors gracefully, and offers strong typing.
 * ==============================================================================
 */

export function getSafeStorage<T>(key: string, fallback: T): T {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return fallback;
  try {
    const item = window.localStorage.getItem(key);
    if (item === null) return fallback;
    return JSON.parse(item) as T;
  } catch (error) {
    console.warn(`[SafeStorage] Error reading key "${key}":`, error);
    return fallback;
  }
}

export function setSafeStorage<T>(key: string, value: T): boolean {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return false;
  try {
    window.localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(value));
    return true;
  } catch (error) {
    console.warn(`[SafeStorage] Error writing key "${key}":`, error);
    return false;
  }
}

export function removeSafeStorage(key: string): void {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return;
  try {
    window.localStorage.removeItem(key);
  } catch (error) {
    console.warn(`[SafeStorage] Error removing key "${key}":`, error);
  }
}
